Motivation: 随机环境下的模型驱动强化学习需要考虑预测不确定性进行规划。传统解析传播完整状态分布的方法受限于策略或奖励结构的严格约束而难以处理，现代深度强化学习要么依赖随机采样（目标方差大），要么使用忽略预测协方差的确定性点估计。作者希望在不施加上述约束的前提下，实现分布感知的规划。

Method: 1) 采用二次动作值（quadratic action-value）参数化，将Bellman备份约简为对状态值函数的期望；2) 提出预测转移分布与值函数类之间的兼容性原理，使该期望可以表示为预测分布矩的解析函数；3) 以高斯转移模型+径向基值函数实例化该原理，获得封闭形式的backup，同时传播预测均值与协方差。

Result: 在连续控制任务、随机观测条件下，该方法有效降低了目标方差（target variance），并且产生的预测不确定性具有良好的校准性（well-calibrated）。

Conclusion: 该工作为使用学习到的分布模型进行规划提供了一个原则性的理论框架，在不依赖严格策略或奖励假设的前提下实现了分布感知的解析规划，兼顾了计算效率与不确定性量化。

Abstract: Effective model-based reinforcement learning in stochastic environments requires planning that accounts for predictive uncertainty. Propagating full state distributions analytically offers a principled way to do this, but has traditionally required restrictive policy or reward structures to remain tractable. Consequently, modern deep reinforcement learning has largely retreated to either stochastic sampling, which introduces significant target variance, or deterministic point estimates that ignore predictive covariance entirely. We investigate whether distribution-aware planning is possible without these constraints. Using a quadratic action-value parameterization, we first reduce the Bellman backup to an expectation over the state-value function alone; the key idea is then a compatibility principle between the predictive transition distribution and the value function class, under which this expectation is analytic in the distribution's moments. We instantiate this principle with a Gaussian transition model paired with a radial-basis value function, yielding a closed-form backup that propagates both predictive mean and covariance. Empirically, our approach reduces target variance and yields well-calibrated predictive uncertainty under stochastic observations in continuous control, providing a principled framework for planning with learned distribution models.

</details>


### [154] [Uncertainty Is Not Enough: Value-of-Information Routing for Mixtures of LoRA Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02528)
*Tom Saliencro,Rohan Desai,Priya Nair,Maya Lindqvist,Daniel Whitmore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VI-MoLE将路由建模为认证信息价值分配问题。它学习每个专家前缀后的反事实剩余风险，在留出校准数据上转换为同时上风险证书，并按单位成本的认证边际风险减少量全局分配适配器预算。终端证书决定回答或弃权，从而区分当前歧义与可恢复及残留风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的动态路由器在不确定性高时激活更多专家，但这一规则将不确定性等同于有用的额外计算——不确定的样本可能包含互补且未查询的专家证据，但也可能在所有专家意见一致后仍然模糊。因此需要一种方法来区分"当前歧义"、"可通过更多计算恢复的风险"和"残余风险"。

Method: 将路由形式化为认证信息价值分配：1）学习每个专家前缀后的反事实剩余风险；2）利用留出校准数据将这些预测转换为同时有效的上风险证书；3）在全局适配器预算约束下，选择每个Token-层动作中单位成本下认证边际风险减少量最大的项进行分配；4）终端证书决定回答或弃权。同时证明同时证书有效性、递减认证收益下的最优贪心分配性质以及价值估计误差下的分配后悔界。

Result: 在匹配计算量的准确率、证书覆盖度、风险-覆盖曲线、分布偏移和尾部延迟等指标上，对固定和动态MoE-LoRA路由器进行评测，验证了VI-MoLE相比不确定性门控方法的优势。

Conclusion: VI-MoLE通过证书化的信息价值分配解决了不确定性路由中"不确定性不等于需要更多计算"的根本缺陷，能够明确区分可恢复风险与残余歧义，提供了理论保证和实证验证。

Abstract: Mixtures of low-rank adaptation experts increase parameter-efficient capacity by routing each input through a subset of adapters. Recent dynamic routers activate more experts when the router or prediction is uncertain. This rule silently equates uncertainty with useful additional computation: an uncertain example may contain complementary, unqueried expert evidence, but it may instead remain ambiguous after every expert agrees. We formulate routing as certified value-of-information allocation. VI-MoLE learns the counterfactual risk remaining after each expert prefix, converts these predictions into simultaneous upper-risk certificates on held-out calibration data, and spends a global adapter budget on the token--layer action with the largest certified marginal risk reduction per unit cost. A terminal certificate then decides whether to answer or abstain. Unlike an uncertainty gate, this procedure distinguishes present ambiguity from recoverable and residual risk. We prove simultaneous certificate validity, optimal greedy allocation under diminishing certified gains, and allocation regret under value-estimation error. The evaluation protocol tests matched-compute accuracy, certificate coverage, risk--coverage, distribution shift, and tail latency against fixed and dynamic MoE-LoRA routers.

</details>


### [155] [Benchmarking Sheaf Neural Networks for Inductive Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02558)
*Stefano Fiorini,Edoardo Coppola,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地对Sheaf神经网络（SNNs）在归纳（inductive）设置下的设计空间进行基准测试。通过1,890个受控实验覆盖14个归纳数据集，发现限制映射（restriction maps）是主导性设计选择且通用映射更优，更大的茎（stalk）维度增加容量但不提升长程建模能力，整体架构组件的选择比sheaf特定设计空间对性能的影响更大。SNNs可以迁移到归纳设置但未达到最强基线，且差距因数据集而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sheaf神经网络虽然在理论上具有坚实基础且在直推式（transductive）节点分类任务上表现良好，但几乎所有现有评估都局限于直推式协议，其在归纳式（inductive）协议下的行为完全未知。为了填补这一空白，需要对sheaf设计空间进行系统的归纳基准测试。

Method: 作者构建了一个消息传递重构框架（message-passing reformulation），避免显式组装沉重的sheaf Laplacian矩阵，使得完整的设计空间可以在跨图批处理（cross-graph batching）下进行训练。评测覆盖三个维度：三种扩散机制（neural sheaf diffusion、sheaf attention、sheaf attention with GATv2）、三种限制映射参数化方式、三种茎维度，以及六个现代GNN架构组件，在14个归纳数据集上进行1,890个受控实验。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 限制映射是sheaf设计中占主导地位的选择，且通用映射表现优于其他映射；(2) 增大茎（stalk）维度可以增加模型容量，但并不会提升长程（long-range）建模能力；(3) 架构组件的选择对性能变异性的解释力大于整个sheaf特定设计空间本身；(4) 在匹配的协议下，SNNs可以迁移到归纳设置，但未能达到最强基线，差距与数据集相关；(5) 单个sheaf配置可以在多个数据集上泛化，因此调优重点应放在外围架构配方而非sheaf算子本身。

Conclusion: SNNs在归纳设置下具有可迁移性，但其性能不如最强的基线且差距因数据集而异。实际建议是将调优精力集中在架构配方上而非sheaf算子本身，因为单一sheaf配置即可跨数据集泛化，而架构组件对性能的影响更大。

Abstract: Sheaf Neural Networks (SNNs) generalize message passing by replacing scalar edge weights of standard Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) with learnable, edge-dependent restriction maps between node stalks. Despite their strong theoretical foundations and promising transductive results, SNNs have been evaluated almost exclusively on transductive node classification, leaving their behaviour under inductive protocols unknown. We address this gap through the first systematic benchmark of the sheaf design space, evaluating three diffusion mechanisms (neural sheaf diffusion, sheaf attention, and sheaf attention with Graph Attention Network v2), three restriction-map parameterizations, three stalk dimensions, and six modern GNN architectural components, within a message-passing reformulation that never assembles the heavy sheaf Laplacian, making the full design space trainable under cross-graph batching. Across $1{,}890$ controlled experiments on 14 inductive datasets, multiple insights emerge: restriction maps are the dominant design choice and general maps are preferable, larger stalks add capacity but not long-range reach, architectural components explain more performance variation than the entire sheaf-specific design space itself. Under a matched protocol, SNNs transfer to inductive settings but do not reach the strongest baselines, with gaps being dataset-dependent. Practically, a single sheaf configuration can generalize across datasets, so effort is better spent tuning the surrounding architectural recipe than the sheaf operator itself.

</details>


### [156] [Structured Memory for Edge Language Models: Persistent Context and Corpus Retrieval via O(1) SSM State Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02560)
*Anusha Madan Gopal,Aras Pirbadian,Kristofor D. Carlson,M Anthony Lewis,Jonathan Tapson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PRECOG，一种利用状态空间模型（SSM）特有属性消除检索增强生成（RAG）prefill成本的机制。SSM的固定大小、位置无关的循环隐藏状态可完整概括模型读取的所有内容，因此PRECOG在离线阶段将文档语料预编码为SSM隐藏状态，查询时直接注入最佳匹配状态，将prefill从O(L_context)降至O(1)。在1.2B参数的TENNs-LLM上实现约4500倍prefill延迟加速（27秒降至6毫秒内），且保持与上下文RAG相当的答案质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RAG的prefill成本与检索到的上下文长度成正比，且Transformer架构的KV缓存随生成token数线性增长。虽然SSM天然规避了KV缓存问题，但prefill成本仍然存在。研究者旨在彻底消除prefill成本，使大规模RAG在边缘设备上达到可交互的延迟水平。

Method: （1）PRECOG：利用SSM固定大小且位置无关的循环隐藏状态是读取内容完整摘要这一特性，离线预编码文档语料为SSM隐藏状态，查询时直接注入最佳匹配隐藏状态，绕过上下文重新摄取。（2）SMC（结构化记忆整合）：层次化持久记忆系统，包含认知域聚类、可调节保真度-存储旋钮和O(1)会话初始化，将短期情节状态整合为长期语义记忆，并在查询时将两者与检索到的语料状态融合。（3）在1.2B参数的门控SSM语言模型TENNs-LLM（192KB隐藏状态）上验证。

Result: PRECOG在答案质量上与上下文RAG相当，prefill延迟从约27秒降至6毫秒以内（约4500倍加速），在边缘硬件上从不可用跨越到可交互的延迟阈值。该机制在Transformer的KV缓存上架构上不可能实现，因为后者位置纠缠且随上下文长度线性增长。

Conclusion: PRECOG验证了SSM作为RAG后端在延迟效率上的根本优势：通过将检索从"重新读一遍"变为"直接注入隐藏状态"，将prefill从随上下文长度线性增长降低为O(1)，实现了大规模RAG在边缘设备上的实时交互，并为层次化记忆系统提供了新的架构基础。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) imposes a prefill cost proportional to retrieved context length, and -- with Transformer backbones -- a KV-cache that grows with each generated token. State-Space Models (SSMs) avoid the second cost by construction; we eliminate the first, collapsing prefill from $O(L_{context})$ to $O(1)$ per query. We introduce PRECOG (Pre-Computed Context Injection), a retrieval mechanism that exploits a property unique to SSMs: the fixed-size, position-agnostic recurrent hidden state is a complete summary of everything the model has read. PRECOG pre-encodes document corpora offline as SSM hidden states and injects the best-matching state directly at query time, bypassing in-context re-ingestion entirely. The same state-injection mechanism enables SMC (Structured Memory Consolidation): a hierarchical persistent memory with cognitive-domain clustering, an adjustable fidelity-vs-storage dial, and $O(1)$ session initialization, which consolidates short-term episodic states into long-term semantic memory and fuses both with retrieved corpus states at query time. We demonstrate the system on TENNs-LLM, a 1.2B-parameter gated-SSM language model with a 192 KB hidden state. PRECOG matches in-context RAG answer quality, reducing prefill latency from $\sim$27 s to $<$6 ms on edge hardware -- a $\sim$4500$\times$ speedup that crosses the threshold from unusable to interactive. The mechanism is architecturally impossible for Transformer KV-caches, which are position-entangled and grow linearly with context length.

</details>


### [157] [Pseudorandom Streams within Diffusion Models Act as Learnable Inputs That Affect Generation Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02575)
*Shengzhi Deng,Chenqi Ye,Yanze Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩散模型在有限精度硬件上消耗的伪随机数（PRNG）轨道结构如何成为可学习的输入，影响训练和生成质量。通过一个MLP预测器和一个扩散探针两种度量，发现不同伪随机源的轨道结构即使控制边际统计量后仍会导致显著不同的扩散损失和生成质量，且与宏观生成退化存在强秩相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型依赖随机输入，但在有限精度硬件上，实际使用的是确定性伪随机规则产生的数值轨道。这些轨道结构可能被模型作为可学习输入利用，影响损失和梯度计算，从而影响训练和生成。然而此前研究较少关注伪随机源本身作为结构化输入这一层面。

Method: 提出两种度量方法：(1) 小型多层感知机（MLP）从序列近期历史预测下一个值，衡量一般序列可预测性；(2) 扩散探针——将真实图像替换为在线随机张量，同时保留扩散架构和训练目标，衡量目标系统是否能利用轨道结构。实验中控制了边际统计量并筛选出动力学和有限精度失败案例，在MNIST和CIFAR-10上进行验证。

Result: 控制边际统计量后，不同伪随机轨道仍产生显著不同的扩散损失和生成质量。两种度量均与宏观生成退化呈现强秩相关性（但局部排名有差异）。经IID基线归一化后，探针损失与真实数据扩散损失近似遵循经验幂律，两个数据集上的指数不同。

Conclusion: 伪随机源不仅是分布选择，更是模型依赖的结构化输入。选择PRNG时需要考虑其轨道结构对特定扩散模型的实际影响，这对扩散模型的复现性和工程部署具有重要启示。

Abstract: Diffusion models rely on stochastic inputs, yet on finite-precision hardware, the "randomness" they consume is realized as deterministic numerical orbits generated by pseudorandom rules. Accessible orbit structure can become a learnable input and affect both training and generation because the realized loss and its gradient depend on the concrete pseudorandom values consumed at each optimization step. A small multilayer perceptron predicts the next value of an orbit from its recent history, measuring general sequence predictability. A diffusion probe replaces real images with online random tensors while preserving the diffusion architecture and training objective, measuring whether the target system can exploit orbit structure. After controlling marginal statistics and screening out clear dynamical and finite-precision failures, the remaining orbits still produce markedly different diffusion losses and generation quality on MNIST and CIFAR-10. Both measures show strong rank correlations with macroscopic generation degradation, although their local rankings differ. After normalization by the IID baseline, the probe loss and the real-data diffusion loss approximately follow an empirical power law, with different exponents on the two datasets. These results suggest that a pseudorandom source is not only a distributional choice, but also a model-dependent structured input.

</details>


### [158] [Smooth Reparameterizations of Functions on Simplicial Product Spaces: Applications to Probabilistic Tensor Decomposition and Functional Data Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02576)
*Shashwat Kumar,Arafat Rahman,Anuj Srivastava,P. -A. Absil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将单纯形乘积空间上的约束优化问题通过元素级严格凸的平滑重参数化转化为流形上的无约束优化问题，并证明该重参数化保持二阶KKT条件性质，从而可以采用黎曼梯度下降（RGD）算法求解，在低秩离散多元概率分布学习和SRVF函数配准等任务中优于投影梯度下降（PGD）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多重要优化问题（如单纯形约束的张量分解学习低秩离散多元概率分布、SRVF表示下的函数数据配准）定义在单纯形乘积空间上，属于约束优化问题。直接在乘积单纯形上进行投影梯度下降等方法可能存在收敛慢或解不忠实的问题，因此需要探索更优的求解策略。

Method: 将乘积单纯形通过光滑且元素级严格凸的映射重参数化为流形上的无约束优化问题。作者证明了在该流形上求得的二阶KKT点在映射回乘积单纯形后恰好对应于弱二阶KKT点，从而保证了最优性条件的一致性。基于此，采用黎曼梯度下降（RGD）算法求解重参数化后的问题。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的RGD算法在求解重参数化问题上优于投影梯度下降（PGD）算法；在曲线配准任务中，该方法能够更忠实地保留原始函数形状特征。

Conclusion: 单纯形乘积空间上的约束优化问题可以通过平滑严格凸重参数化转化为流形上的无约束优化问题求解，这不仅保持了二阶最优性条件，还带来了算法性能的提升，为低秩概率分布学习和函数配准等应用提供了更有效的优化框架。

Abstract: We consider optimization problems defined on product spaces of simplices. Examples of this class of problems include learning low-rank discrete multivariate probability distributions via simplex constrained tensor decomposition and performing functional data registration under the Square Root Velocity Function (SRVF) representation. In this work, we demonstrate the feasibility of replacing the product simplex with a smooth, elementwise strictly convex reparameterization, resulting in an unconstrained optimization problem on a manifold. We show that performing such a reparameterization results in the second order Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) points on the smooth manifold being mapped to the weak second order KKT points on the product simplex. This leads to a Riemannian Gradient Descent (RGD) algorithm for solving the reparameterized problem, which outperforms Projected Gradient Descent (PGD), and provides a more faithful representation of the original function shapes while performing curve registration.

</details>


### [159] [GradCuit: Credit-Assigned Gradient Flow Enables Robust and Interpretable Test-Time Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02585)
*Zhaoxin Yu,Qi Shen,Hengli Li,Zhaowei Zhang,Song-Chun Zhu,Chi Zhang,Zilong Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GradCuit是一种新的测试时推理优化方法，通过在选定的Transformer层中插入可优化的潜状态，利用因果自注意力的可微路径将整个延续序列的奖励加权梯度直接分配给潜状态，实现端到端直接的序列级信用分配。在五个指令微调骨干模型、三个推理基准和两种答案格式上平均准确率64.5%，比链式思维提示高6.6个百分点，比最强竞争方法高2.4个百分点，且对学习率更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于优化的潜推理方法通常通过解码token将潜状态与推理轨迹连接，导致序列级信用分配是间接的，难以理解潜状态更新如何塑造后续推理。作者希望建立一条从整个续写到每个潜状态的可微路径，实现直接的序列级梯度分配。

Method: GradCuit在选定Transformer层的隐表示与生成续写之间插入可优化的潜状态。利用因果自注意力机制，使得续写中每个token的对数概率都能通过剩余Transformer块对每个前置潜状态形成可微路径，从而将来自整个续写的奖励加权梯度直接分配给潜状态。此外，论文还通过token级梯度归因和层分析来研究潜影响的可解释性。

Result: 在五个指令微调骨干模型、三个推理基准和两种答案格式上，GradCuit平均准确率64.5%，比链式思维提示高6.6个百分点，比最强竞争方法高2.4个百分点。在七个学习率设置下，准确率标准差从LatentSeek的1.53降至0.82；连随机游走变体都保持与LatentSeek相当的性能。token级梯度归因显示潜影响集中在推理连接token上，层分析指出早期到中间Transformer层是最有效的优化空间。

Conclusion: GradCuit通过直接从结果反馈优化内部推理，开辟了稳健且可解释的测试时缩放新方向——LLM适应其推理方式，而非仅仅重新生成、采样或重排序输出，为测试时计算扩展提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Optimization-based latent reasoning improves large language model outputs by optimizing instance-specific continuous states at test time while keeping model parameters frozen. Existing methods, however, typically connect these states to the reasoning trajectory through decoded tokens, making sequence-level credit assignment indirect and obscuring how latent updates shape subsequent reasoning. We introduce GradCuit (gradient through circuit), which inserts optimizable latent states at a selected Transformer layer between the hidden representations of the prompt and the generated continuation. Causal self-attention provides every continuation-token log-probability with a differentiable path to every preceding latent state through the remaining Transformer blocks, enabling reward-weighted gradients from the entire continuation to be assigned directly to the latents. Across five instruction-tuned backbones, three reasoning benchmarks, and two answer formats, GradCuit achieves an average accuracy of 64.5%, outperforming chain-of-thought prompting by 6.6 percentage points and the strongest competing method by 2.4 points. GradCuit also demonstrates greater robustness: across seven learning-rate settings, it consistently outperforms LatentSeek while reducing the standard deviation of accuracy from 1.53 to 0.82, and even its random-walk variant remains competitive with LatentSeek. For interpretability, token-level gradient attribution reveals that latent influence concentrates on reasoning-connector tokens, while layer analysis identifies early-to-middle Transformer layers as the most effective optimization space. By directly optimizing internal reasoning from outcome feedback, GradCuit opens a new axis of robust and interpretable test-time scaling, where LLMs adapt how they reason rather than merely regenerate, sample, or rerank outputs.

</details>


### [160] [onepot-Bench 0: towards lab-aware in silico chemistry benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02595)
*Brandon Wang,Andrei S. Tyrin,Daniil A. Boiko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了onepot-Bench 0，一个用于评估语言模型在湿实验（wet-lab）合成化学能力的专有基准套件，包含三个互补评估：ChemAbacus（无工具化学信息学素养与数值推理）、SynthRefusal（安全与拒答行为）和SynthBench（基于私有实验数据的反应结果预测与催化剂选择）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在实验室科学中的应用日益重要，但现有评估难以精确衡量其实际实验室决策能力：科学能力需要问题求解技能与领域直觉的结合，而现有评测要么无法覆盖物理实验室中的可靠决策能力，要么依赖可能已出现在模型训练语料中的公开数据，导致评估结果不可靠。

Method: 构建onepot-Bench 0专有基准套件，包含三个互补评估任务：(1) ChemAbacus——衡量无工具化学信息学素养和数值推理；(2) SynthRefusal——涵盖良性、受控和设计药物目标的拒答行为与安全性评估；(3) SynthBench——使用实验室自产的私有实验数据评估反应结果预测和催化剂选择能力。

Result: 论文介绍了onepot-Bench 0基准套件的整体框架，通过三个互补的评估维度（基础能力、可靠性、深层知识）全面衡量语言模型在湿实验室中对可靠性能至关重要的技能。

Conclusion: onepot-Bench 0通过专有数据和多维评估设计，弥补了现有基准依赖公开数据、无法衡量实验室可靠决策能力的不足，为语言模型的合成化学湿实验能力提供了更可靠的评估方案。

Abstract: Language models are playing an increasingly important role in laboratory science, performing tasks such as experiment planning, execution, and post-hoc analysis. However, precisely measuring their abilities is difficult, as scientific capabilities require a mixture of both problem-solving skills and domain-specific intuition. Existing evaluations rarely measure the capabilities required to make reliable decisions in a physical laboratory and often rely on public data that may have appeared in model training corpora.
  We introduce onepot-Bench 0, a proprietary benchmark suite for evaluating language models on synthetic chemistry capabilities relevant to wet-lab execution. onepot-Bench 0 comprises three complementary evaluations: ChemAbacus measures tool-free cheminformatics literacy and numerical reasoning; SynthRefusal characterizes safety and refusal behavior across a variety of benign, controlled, and designer-drug targets; and SynthBench evaluates reaction-outcome prediction and catalyst selection using private experimental data generated in our laboratory. Together, these evaluations probe basic competency, reliability, and deeper knowledge, all skills which are required for reliable performance in the lab.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [161] [A Portable and Versatile Limited-Memory BFGS Implementation in PETSc/TAO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00196)
*Hansol Suh,Tobin Isaac,Alp Dener,Todd Munson,Hong Zhang,Richard Tran Mills*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文在PETSc/TAO中实现了一种L-BFGS的中间稠密（intermediate dense）公式化方法，兼顾递归式（$2m$个顺序rank-1更新）和紧凑稠密表示（compact dense representation）两种传统方法的优点，并在美国能源部的Polaris和Frontier超算上进行了单节点CPU和GPU性能测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统L-BFGS实现采用$2m$个顺序rank-1更新，在同步延迟显著时性能受影响，且当向量无法放入缓存时因时间局部性差导致内存流量增大；而紧凑稠密表示虽具有最小同步延迟和更好的时间局部性，但需要额外遍历基向量，且当$B_0$矩阵变化（如变度量方法）时需重算基向量。作者希望找到一种兼顾两种方法优点的中间表示。

Method: 在PETSc/TAO中实现了一种中间的稠密BFGS公式化方法，该方法保留了递归式方法和紧凑稠密方法的大部分优点，即在避免额外基向量遍历和$B_0$变度量重算需求的同时，减少了同步延迟并改善了时间局部性。在Polaris和Frontier超级计算机上进行单节点性能测试，涵盖GPU和CPU两种计算后端。

Result: 在Polaris和Frontier机器上完成了单节点性能评测，对比了递归式、紧凑稠密式和中间稠密式三种L-BFGS实现的CPU与GPU计算性能。

Conclusion: 提出的中间稠密L-BFGS公式化方法在PETSc/TAO中实现并验证，兼具递归式与紧凑稠密式的优势，在同步延迟和内存局部性方面取得折中。

Abstract: The limited-memory BFGS (L-BFGS) Hessian update scheme is the critical kernel in many quasi-Newton optimization algorithms. The most common approach to implementing L-BFGS uses $2m$ sequential rank-1 updates as part of solving a linear system when there are $m$ history steps. The performance of this approach suffers when the latency of synchronization is significant, and its poor temporal locality increases the memory traffic when vectors do not fit in cache. The compact dense representation of L-BFGS results in an approach that has minimal synchronization latency and better temporal locality, but it requires an additional pass over the basis vectors and an additional basis that must be recomputed when the $B_0$ matrix changes as in variable-metric methods. In the Portable Extensible Toolkit for Scientific Computation and the Toolkit for Advanced Optimization (PETSc/TAO), we have implemented an intermediate dense formulation of BFGS that retains most of the good characteristics of both the recursive and compact dense approaches. We report single-node performance tests of these implementations on the U.S. Department of Energy's Polaris and Frontier machines, testing both GPU-based and CPU-based computations.

</details>


### [162] [Design and Implementation of Schwarz Information Criterion-Aided Intelligent Decentralized Resource Allocation in Dynamic LoRa Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00409)
*Aohan Li,Ryota Ariyoshi,Mikio Hasegawa,Miao Pan,Tomoaki Ohtsuki,Zhu Han*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级分布式学习方法，结合Thompson采样（TS）和Schwarz信息准则（SIC）用于LoRa网络中传输参数的动态选择。TS负责参数选择的探索与利用平衡，SIC负责检测通信环境变化并重置学习历史，从而在资源受限的LoRa终端设备上实现快速自适应。实验表明，在高密度动态LoRa网络中，该方法将传输成功率从64.0%提升至71.1%，能量效率从293.9 bit/J提升至328.3 bit/J。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRa网络中的传输参数选择面临动态变化的通信环境，传统方法（如UCB1-tuned）适应性不足。同时，LoRa终端设备（EDs）具有有限的存储和计算资源，需要轻量级的解决方案。此外，基于TS的方法过度依赖历史学习经验，在通信环境突变时适应缓慢。

Method: 提出一种结合TS和SIC的轻量级分布式学习方法。TS用于传输参数选择，通过概率分布更新在探索与利用间取得平衡，适合资源受限设备；SIC用于环境变化检测，具备低计算开销的特点。检测到环境变化时，重置TS的学习历史以加速重新学习。该方法仅依赖ACK反馈和所选传输参数，实现完全分布式的参数选择。

Result: 在高密度动态LoRa网络中，相较传统UCB1-tuned方案，传输成功率从64.0%提升至71.1%，能量效率从293.9 bit/J提升至328.3 bit/J。

Conclusion: 所提出的TS+SIC方法能够在资源受限的LoRa设备上实现高效的分布式传输参数自适应选择，同时提升传输可靠性和能量效率，适用于动态高密度LoRa网络场景。

Abstract: This paper proposes a lightweight distributed learning method for selecting transmission parameters in Long-Range (LoRa) networks that adapts to dynamically changing communication environments. In the proposed method, the Thompson Sampling (TS) is adopted for transmission parameter selection, whereas the Schwarz Information Criterion (SIC) is employed for environmental change detection. TS is a reinforcement learning approach that effectively balances exploration and exploitation by updating parameters based on probability distributions. Additionally, it demonstrates stable performance even with a small number of trials, thereby making it well-suited for LoRa end devices (EDs) with limited memory capacity and computational resources. Furthermore, to address the issue that TS-based methods strongly depend on past learning histories and therefore adapt slowly to abrupt changes in communication environments, a statistical change detection mechanism based on the SIC is integrated into our proposed method. SIC is adopted because it can detect environmental changes with low computational cost and is suitable for implementation on resource-constrained LoRa EDs. When a change in the communication environment is detected by SIC, the learning history of TS is reset, thereby enabling rapid re-learning under new environmental conditions. Moreover, to achieve fully distributed communication parameter selection while enhancing transmission reliability and energy efficiency, the proposed method relies solely on Acknowledgment (ACK) feedback and the selected transmission parameters. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method improves the transmission success rate from 64.0% to 71.1% and increases energy efficiency from 293.9 bit/J to 328.3 bit/J compared with the conventional Upper Confidence Bound (UCB)1-tuned scheme under high-density dynamic LoRa networks.

</details>


### [163] [Cache-Consistent Dynamic Load Balancing for Kubernetes Controllers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00454)
*Shoma Ansai,Yasuo Okabe,Daisuke Kotani*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种Kubernetes控制器的横向扩展方法，通过二级哈希映射和一致性哈希实现轻量负载均衡，并结合缓存同步机制，解决了控制器扩展中对象标签重写成本高和缓存一致性问题，实验表明吞吐量随实例数提升且重分配时间可接受。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着Kubernetes集群规模增长，控制器成为性能瓶颈，但控制器目前只能单实例运行。原因有二：一是重分配成本高（基于Label检索对象，若用Label记录分配实例，实例增减时需重写所有对象的Label）；二是缓存一致性问题（控制器只读本地缓存，重分配时需显式更新缓存，且不同种类对象的缓存更新需同步）。

Method: 提出结合轻量负载均衡与缓存同步的横向扩展方案。具体采用两级哈希（Two-level Hash）将对象映射到虚拟节点（Virtual Nodes），在Label中记录虚拟节点标识符，并通过一致性哈希（Consistent Hashing）将虚拟节点分配给实例。当实例增减时，只需更新检索对象时指定的Label值，无需重写任何对象上的Label。缓存锁定直至重分配完成，防止读取陈旧缓存；利用数据存储的版本标识符同步不同种类对象的缓存。

Result: 在Kubernetes上实现了所提方法并进行了评估，结果显示处理吞吐量随实例数量增加而提升，重分配所需时间保持在可接受的范围内。

Conclusion: 提出的两级哈希+一致性哈希+缓存同步方案能够有效支持Kubernetes控制器的水平扩展，在保持性能提升的同时将重分配开销控制在可接受范围内，解决了原单实例架构的可扩展性瓶颈。

Abstract: As Kubernetes clusters grow, the scalability of controllers can become a bottleneck for the performance of the system. Distributing the load dynamically across multiple controller instances, however, raises the following two problems, and a controller can therefore be run only as a single instance today. The first problem is the cost of reassignment. A controller retrieves objects on the basis of the Labels attached to them, so in a naive design in which the assigned instance is recorded in a Label on every object, the Labels must be rewritten in proportion to the total number of objects whenever instances are added or removed. The second problem is cache consistency. A controller consults only its own cache when it reads an object and never refers to the actual data, so the cache has to be updated explicitly at the time of a reassignment. Furthermore, a controller manages a cache independently for each kind of object, so cache updates have to be synchronized across the kinds of objects. We propose a method for scaling Kubernetes controllers horizontally that combines lightweight load balancing with cache synchronization. A two-level Hash maps objects to Virtual Nodes, records their identifiers in Labels, and assigns Virtual Nodes to instances by Consistent Hashing. Whenever instances are added or removed, it therefore suffices to update the Label value specified when objects are retrieved, and no Label on an object has to be rewritten. The cache is also locked until the reassignment has completed, which prevents any reference to a stale cache. The version identifier of the data store is used to synchronize the kinds of objects with one another. We implemented the proposed method on Kubernetes and evaluated it: the processing throughput rises with the number of instances, and the time required for reassignment remains within an acceptable range.

</details>


### [164] [AReaL-DTE: Sparse Policy-Weight Transfer for Online Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00455)
*Yingqi Peng,Jiawei Zhang,Wenhao Zhou,Ruida Xu,Ran Yan,Wei Dong,Yi Gao,Zhiqiang Ding,Tongkai Yang,Binhang Yuan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: AReaL-DTE 是一个无快照的 Delta 传输引擎，利用推理可见的权重稀疏性（相邻策略版本间 BF16 权重变化少于 2%），通过按需重建被覆盖权重（反向 AdamW 更新）、转换器对齐的 BF16 变更检测以及接收端局部坐标重映射，实现端到端的高效策略权重同步。相比 ByteCheckpoint 和 PULSE，跨集群加速最高 19.9x 和 3.2x，集群内加速最高 7.6x 和 7.4x，同时显著降低 GPU 和 CPU 峰值内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线智能体强化学习采用微服务架构将策略训练与 rollout 生成分离，提升可扩展性和模块化，但频繁的策略权重同步成为关键系统开销。传统稠密权重同步代价高昂，而基于 checkpoint 的稀疏同步方法仍可能保留旧模型并物化完整中间结果来弥合训练与推理布局的差异，效率不足。

Method: AReaL-DTE 提出无快照的 Delta 传输引擎：(1) 通过反向 AdamW 更新按需重建被覆盖的权重，消除对旧模型的依赖；(2) 采用转换器对齐的 BF16 变更检测，流式比较重建参数与当前参数；(3) 将变更元素直接重映射到接收端本地坐标；(4) 跨集群通过共享存储实现 manifest 提交的稀疏传输，集群内采用死锁安全的两轮协议，并直接应用到推理分片上。

Result: 在 Qwen3-8B 和 Qwen3-30B-A3B 上的四个在线 RL 工作负载中，AReaL-DTE 跨集群相对 ByteCheckpoint 加速最高 19.9x、相对 PULSE 加速 3.2x；集群内分别加速最高 7.6x 和 7.4x。同集群 Qwen3-30B-A3B 实验中，峰值 GPU 内存降低约 41%，峰值 CPU 内存降低至少 87%。

Conclusion: AReaL-DTE 证明在线 RL 中策略权重同步可以利用权重稀疏性实现快照无关的高效 Delta 传输，大幅提升同步速度并降低资源占用，为大规模在线智能体强化学习系统的策略分发提供了高效方案。

Abstract: Online agentic reinforcement learning implemented with micro-services separates policy training from rollout generation, improving scalability and modularity while potentially making frequent policy-weight synchronization a critical systems overhead. Shared storage naturally connects these services across clusters, but vanilla dense policy weight synchronization could incur model-scale construction, transfer, and application costs. Sparse synchronization reduces transferred data, yet checkpoint-oriented approaches can still retain a previous model and materialize complete intermediates to bridge heterogeneous training and inference layouts. We present AReaL-DTE, a snapshot-free Delta Transfer Engine that translates inference-visible weight sparsity into end-to-end system efficiency. Across our evaluated workloads, fewer than 2% of BF16 weight elements change between consecutive policy versions. AReaL-DTE reconstructs overwritten weights on demand by inverting AdamW updates, streams reconstructed and current parameters through converter-aligned BF16 change detection, and remaps changed elements directly into receiver-local coordinates. AReaL-DTE supports manifest-committed sparse transfer through shared storage across clusters and a deadlock-safe two-round protocol within a cluster, followed by direct application to inference shards. We evaluate AReaL-DTE on Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-30B-A3B across four online RL workloads. AReaL-DTE achieves speedups of up to 19.9x over ByteCheckpoint and 3.2x over PULSE across clusters, and up to 7.6x and 7.4x, respectively, within a cluster. In the same-cluster Qwen3-30B-A3B experiments, it reduces peak GPU memory by approximately 41% and peak CPU memory by at least 87%.

</details>


### [165] [Collaborative Orbital Edge Intelligence: A Decentralized Paradigm for Energy-Efficient Computing in Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00487)
*Yuvraj Sahni,Jiannong Cao,Fu Xiao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为协作式轨道边缘智能（COEI）的新型范 paradigm，利用不同提供商的低轨（LEO）卫星之间的去中心化协作，实现节能的星上空间数据处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨道边缘计算研究通常假设集中式控制且不考虑不同提供商卫星之间的协作，导致连接性有限、延迟更高以及卫星电池消耗增加，未能充分探索异构LEO星座下用于节能智能的去中心化卫星间协作。

Method: 提出COEI范式，设计了包含组网、计算和功率管理问题的整体系统架构；并通过去中心化能量感知的卫星任务卸载案例研究加以验证，目标是在最大化任务成功率的同时最小化所有卫星的最大电池放电深度之和。

Result: COEI能够创建多方、多轨道的巨型星座，提供全球弹性连接、实时智能和节能服务等更好的服务质量；案例研究验证了去中心化能量感知任务卸载的有效性。

Conclusion: COEI为LEO卫星间的去中心化协作提供了新方向，未来工作包括进一步探索相关开放问题以获得更深入的研究机会。

Abstract: In recent years, Low Earth Orbit (LEO) satellites have been increasingly deployed to enable connectivity in remote and disaster-prone areas. Researchers have proposed Orbital Edge Computing, which adds computational intelligence to LEO satellites to process data on orbit, providing edge intelligence close to space data sources. Existing work on Orbital Edge Computing typically assumes centralized control without collaboration among satellites from different providers, leading to limited connectivity, higher latency, and increased satellite battery depletion. They have not fully explored decentralized inter-satellite collaboration for energy-efficient intelligence under heterogeneous LEO constellations. This paper introduces a novel paradigm, Collaborative Orbital Edge Intelligence (COEI), that leverages decentralized collaboration among LEO satellites to enable energy-efficient on-orbit processing of space data. We describe the overall system architecture of COEI, including the issues related to networking, computing, and power management. COEI can help create a multi-party, multi-orbit megaconstellation of satellites that delivers better service quality by providing benefits, including global resilient connectivity, real-time intelligence, and energy-efficient services. To demonstrate the COEI benefits, we conduct a case study on decentralized energy-aware satellite task offloading to maximize the task success rate while minimizing the sum of the maximum battery depth-of-discharge across all satellites. Finally, we outline future directions for COEI that offer opportunities for further investigation.

</details>


### [166] [Multi-tenant Kubernetes Use Cases for AI, Secure Computing and Data Services, and More](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00742)
*Jake Watson,Sadaf R Alam,Christopher Woods,Abdelwahab Kawafi,Thomas Green,Ian Johnson,Ellis Pires,Jessica R. Jones,Utz-Uwe Haus*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文评估了在配备HPE Slingshot互连的HPE Cray EX超级计算机（Isambard-AI）上部署Kubernetes的可行性，通过两个协同设计用例（可信研究环境和基于KubeRay/Ray/vLLM的AI模型托管服务）验证其作为国家AI研究资源中补充性服务的能力，并总结了挑战与经验教训。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超级计算生态系统中传统单租户批处理系统缺乏公有云用户习惯的灵活性和可复现性，而Kubernetes作为容器编排引擎在超算领域（特别是裸金属性能和网络设备方面）的采用有限。随着AI、安全机密计算和混合工作负载编排等用例日益多样化，需要探索Kubernetes作为批处理调度系统的补充服务在超算平台上的部署可行性。

Method: 在HPE Cray EX超级计算机（Isambard-AI）上部署Kubernetes，采用HPE Slingshot互连技术，通过两个协同设计用例进行评估：(1) 面向医学和健康科学的可信研究环境；(2) 结合KubeRay、Ray和vLLM的分布式、沙盒化、持久化AI模型托管服务，面向多租户机密计算场景。

Result: 成功在HPE Cray EX平台上部署并运行Kubernetes，验证了其在超算环境中作为补充服务的可行性。两个用例均得到实施，同时揭示了部署过程中面临的挑战（如裸金属性能、网络配置、多租户隔离等）以及需要进一步开发以提供生产级Kubernetes-as-a-Service的领域。

Conclusion: Kubernetes不应被视为大规模MPI批量调度系统的替代品，而是作为国家AI研究资源中互补性的服务。在HPE Cray EX（及后续平台）上实现生产级Kubernetes-as-a-Service仍需进一步开发，本文的经验教训为超算环境中Kubernetes的推广提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Kubernetes, as a container orchestration engine, has been widely used in cloud-native ecosystems for several years. In supercomputing ecosystems, especially where bare-metal performance for compute and network devices are considered, the adoption is somewhat limited. However, with the increasing diversity of use cases such as AI, secure and confidential computing for sensitive data, and mixed workload orchestration, a traditional, single-tenant batch computing system does not offer the flexibility and reproducibility to which public cloud users are accustomed. Note that Kubernetes is not considered a replacement for batch scheduling systems, which have powerful features for large-scale MPI jobs with thousands of network end points. Rather, it is a complementary service provided as part of a national AI Research Resource. We evaluate Kubernetes deployment on a Hewlett Packard Enterprise (HPE) Cray EX supercomputerwith HPE Slingshot interconnect, called Isambard-AI, with co-design use cases. One is a Trusted Research Environment used for medical and health sciences. The other combines KubeRay, Ray, and vLLM to provide a distributed, sandboxed, persistent AI model hosting service targeting multi-tenant confidential computing. We discuss challenges and lessons learned, and where further development is needed to offer a production Kubernetes-as-a-Service on HPE Cray EX (and later) platforms.

</details>


### [167] [NUNA: Characterizing and Mitigating Non-Uniform Network Access in Multi-Die GPU Scale-Up Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00867)
*Conor James Green,William Won,Tuan Ta,Bradford M. Beckmann*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出"非均匀网络访问"（NUNA）概念，描述多GPU系统中因芯片间通信距离不同导致的性能差异，并引入两种优化策略：NUNA感知路由（NAR）和NUNA感知放置（NAP），分别优化GPU间通信路径和线程块/数据放置位置。实验表明，NAP单独可带来最高1.5倍集合通信加速，NAP与NAR结合可达1.8倍加速，并在机器学习推理中实现平均7%（最高28%）的每token输出时间加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着GPU架构规模不断扩大，socket内部的线缆传输延迟显著增加。以往研究已优化socket内的计算和内存局部性，但多GPU系统中GPU间通信的空间影响尚未被充分研究。因此本文旨在填补这一空白，引入NUNA来描述这一新兴优化维度，并针对延迟敏感的集合通信（常见于机器学习推理）提出优化方案。

Method: 本文提出了两项关键优化：(1) NUNA感知路由（NAR）——在大规模纵深网络拓扑中选择空间感知的GPU间路径；(2) NUNA感知放置（NAP）——将线程块（threadblocks）和数据放置靠近I/O，以优化GPU间流量。两者可单独或组合使用。

Result: 与不考虑局部性的基线相比：NAP单独可提供最高1.5倍集合通信加速；NAP与NAR结合可提供最高1.8倍加速；在机器学习推理中实现平均7%（最高28%）的每token输出时间加速。

Conclusion: 研究证明多GPU系统中空间感知的通信优化（路由与放置）能够显著提升集合通信性能，进而改善机器学习推理的端到端延迟，为大规模GPU系统中的通信优化开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Graphics processing unit (GPU) architectures are growing in size to meet the increasing compute and memory requirements. As GPU sizes increase, intra-socket wire transfer delay increases significantly. While previous research has optimized for compute and memory locality within a socket, the spatial impact on inter-GPU communication has not been well-studied. We introduce the term non-uniform network access (NUNA) to describe this emerging optimization dimension in multi-GPU systems. We specifically focus on latency-sensitive collective communication, common in machine learning inference. First, we highlight the need for NUNA-aware routing (NAR), choosing optimized, spatially-aware inter-GPU paths in large scale-up network topologies. Second, we introduce NUNA-aware placement (NAP), placing threadblocks and data near I/O to optimize the inter-GPU traffic. We demonstrate that the NAP optimizations alone offer up to 1.5x collective speedups over a locality-unaware baseline. Combining NAP with NAR yields up to 1.8x faster collectives over the locality-unaware baseline. This leads to 7% mean (28% max) time per output token speedup in machine learning inference.

</details>


### [168] [From Cloud to Crowd: Democratizing LLM Service with Decentralized Edge Collaboration for RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00922)
*Jiaxing Li,Hengzhi Wang,Feng Wang,Chi Xu,Danyang Song,Ruixiao Zhang,Edith C. H. Ngai,Jiangchuan Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DEFRAG是一个面向异构边缘设备的去中心化协作系统，通过检索增强生成（RAG）优化检索和生成两个环节：检索上压缩共享知识图谱并采用混合检索扩展知识覆盖，生成上引入优化器按查询自适应选择小语言模型和RAG参数，在保持稳定服务质量的同时显著缩小SLM与LLM之间的精度差距，成本降低高达98.4%，峰值吞吐提升97.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云端大语言模型（LLM）部署面临高成本、厂商锁定及负载下性能不稳等问题；虽然边缘部署蒸馏/剪枝后的小语言模型（SLM）可降低成本并提升可扩展性，但SLM存在知识覆盖有限、与云端LLM存在显著精度差距的挑战。

Method: DEFRAG采用双维度优化策略：(1) 检索方面，通过压缩并共享知识图谱，结合混合检索手段扩大知识覆盖范围；(2) 生成方面，引入优化器按查询自适应选择SLM和RAG参数，在精度与成本之间取得平衡。系统部署在异构边缘测试床上进行验证。

Result: 在基准QA数据集、移动路由压力、非均匀数据分布及领域特定QA负载下，DEFRAG均保持稳定的服务质量和成本效率；相比集中式服务，精度差距显著缩小，成本降低最高达98.4%，峰值吞吐提升最高达97.8%。

Conclusion: DEFRAG证明了去中心化边缘协作系统能够有效弥合SLM与LLM之间的精度差距，同时实现高成本效益和高吞吐性能，为边缘端民主化LLM服务提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of large language models (LLMs) has increased demand for scalable and cost-effective deployment, especially for mobile and edge devices. Cloud-hosted LLMs are powerful but expensive and difficult to scale due to vendor lock-in and high resource needs, resulting in high expenses and unstable performance under load. Recent efforts focus on deploying small language models (SLMs), distilled or pruned from LLMs, on resource-constrained edge devices to reduce costs and improve scalability. However, edge-based SLMs face limited knowledge coverage and notable accuracy gap compared to cloud-based LLMs. To address this, we present DEFRAG, a decentralized edge collaboration system for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) that optimizes both retrieval and generation across heterogeneous edge devices. For retrieval, DEFRAG compresses and shares knowledge graphs, using hybrid retrieval to expand knowledge coverage. For generation, DEFRAG introduces an optimizer that adaptively selects SLMs and RAG parameters per query, balancing accuracy and cost. We implement DEFRAG on a heterogeneous edge testbed and evaluate it on benchmark QA datasets. We also test it under mobile route stress, non-uniform data placement, and a domain-specific QA workload. The results show that DEFRAG maintains stable service quality and cost efficiency under these broader settings. Results show that DEFRAG narrows the SLM-LLM accuracy gap, while reducing cost by up to 98.4% and increasing peak throughput by up to 97.8% over centralized services. These findings demonstrate the potential of DEFRAG for democratized LLM services at the edge.

</details>


### [169] [TIDE-MC: Two-Sided Interpolative Decomposition for Billion-Scale GPU Matrix Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00977)
*Chengying Huan,Yubo Wang,Pinhuan Wang,Lizheng Chen,Jie Zhang,Fangxin Liu,Qing Wang,Ruixuan Liu,Shaonan Ma,Mingxing Zhang,Zhibin Wang,Rong Gu,Guihai Chen,Chen Tian*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: TIDE-MC是一个基于双侧插值分解(TSID)的有界内存GPU矩阵补全框架，通过模板子矩阵锚定重构完整低秩矩阵，将计算和存储规模从完整矩阵缩减到模板和活动数据块。它通过无冲突同步引擎和分块重构流水线两个执行阶段，实现了超越设备内存容量的矩阵补全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GPU矩阵补全求解器普遍假设观测矩阵或其稠密因子能放入设备内存，但在实际工作负载中这一假设会导致内存溢出(OOM)失败或朴素的页交换带来的严重PCIe开销。因此需要一种有界内存的GPU框架来突破设备内存容量限制。

Method: TIDE-MC基于TSID（双侧插值分解），利用采样的模板子矩阵作为锚点重构完整低秩矩阵。框架包含两个执行阶段：(1) 无冲突同步引擎通过并行分解和分层梯度聚合恢复模板；(2) 分块重建流水线将恢复的模板扩展到剩余矩阵，同时将PCIe传输与GPU计算重叠。此外，采用非对称梯度裁剪方案稳定混合精度Tensor Core执行。

Result: 在15个基准测试中，TIDE-MC成功完成了导致现有GPU求解器内存溢出的工作负载。相比评估的最先进基线，实现了最高11,647倍加速，峰值内存使用降低最多8.5倍，重建误差降低最多99.7%。

Conclusion: 模板锚定分解与阶段特定的GPU执行能够将矩阵补全扩展到超出设备内存容量的规模，证明该方法是解决大规模矩阵补全内存瓶颈的有效途径。

Abstract: Matrix completion supports large-scale recommendation and scientific computing, yet existing GPU solvers commonly assume that the observed matrix or its dense factors fit in device memory. On real workloads, this assumption leads to out-of-memory failures or severe PCIe overhead under naive paging.
  We present TIDE-MC, a bounded-memory GPU framework built on Two-Sided Interpolative Decomposition (TSID). TSID uses a sampled template submatrix as an anchor for reconstructing the full low-rank matrix, allowing computation and storage to scale with the template and active data chunks rather than the complete matrix. TIDE-MC realizes this formulation through two execution stages. First, a conflict-free synchronization engine recovers the template using parallel factorization and hierarchical gradient aggregation. Second, a chunked reconstruction pipeline extends the recovered template to the remaining matrix while overlapping PCIe transfers with GPU computation. An asymmetric gradient-clipping scheme stabilizes mixed-precision Tensor Core execution.
  Across 15 benchmarks, TIDE-MC completes workloads that cause existing GPU solvers to run out of memory. Compared with the evaluated state-of-the-art baselines, it achieves up to 11,647x speedup, reduces peak memory usage by up to 8.5x, and lowers reconstruction error by up to 99.7%. These results show that template-anchored decomposition and stage-specific GPU execution can scale matrix completion beyond device-memory capacity.

</details>


### [170] [Zellige: Moldable Sequence Placement for Mixed Image-Video DiT Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01150)
*Guangyu Xiang,Xueze Kang,Minwei Zhao,Yuxin Wang,Shaohuai Shi,Lin Zhang,Xiaowen Chu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Zellige是一个可塑的序列放置系统，用于解决图像和视频数据混合长度序列在GPU上的分布式训练放置问题，通过联合选择每个序列的并行配置和参与GPU排名，在无需牺牲计算效率的情况下平衡负载并减少通信冗余。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量视频生成需要联合训练图像和视频数据的Diffusion Transformers (DiTs)，产生跨GPU的混合长度序列训练问题。现有系统依赖数据并行(DP)、上下文并行(CP)或其组合，但作者证明这些设计本质上是"不相交分组放置"，面临组间负载不均衡和组内通信冗余之间的根本性权衡，亟需一种更灵活的序列放置方案。

Method: Zellige由三个组件构成：1) 硬件分析器，估计候选放置的执行时间和内存消耗；2) 两阶段规划器，先平衡计算密集的锚定序列，再将较轻的填充序列打包到剩余容量中；3) 合并注意力引擎，高效执行完整序列和分布式注意力分片。

Result: 在21个方案中，硬件分析器预测步骤完成时间和峰值分配内存的平均绝对百分比误差分别为3.4%和1.5%。两阶段规划器每个批次求解耗时33-119毫秒，远快于联合放置参考方案（优化所有序列），且两者模拟完成时间差异不超过0.32%。端到端评估中，Zellige在16块A800 GPU上比KnapFormer快1.12-1.48倍，在32块A6000 GPU上快1.27-1.54倍。

Conclusion: Zellige通过引入可塑的序列放置机制，突破了传统DP/CP不相交分组放置的局限，在混合长度序列训练中同时实现了负载均衡与通信效率，显著优于现有的最先进系统。

Abstract: High-quality video generation requires training Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) jointly on image and video data, posing a mixed-length sequence training problem across GPUs. Existing systems rely on data parallelism (DP), context parallelism (CP), or their combination; we model these designs as disjoint-group placement and prove that they face a fundamental tradeoff between inter-group load imbalance and intra-group communication redundancy. We present Zellige, a moldable sequence placement system that jointly selects each sequence's parallelism configuration and participating ranks. Zellige consists of three components: a hardware profiler that estimates the execution time and memory consumption of candidate placements, a two-stage planner that balances compute-heavy anchor sequences and packs lighter filler sequences into the remaining capacity, and a coalesced attention engine that efficiently executes whole sequences alongside distributed-attention shards. Across 21 plans, the hardware profile predicts step makespan and peak allocated memory with mean absolute percentage errors of $3.4%$ and $1.5%$, respectively. The two-stage planner solves each batch in 33--119 ms, significantly faster than a joint-placement reference that optimizes all sequences together, while their modeled makespans differ by at most $0.32%$. In end-to-end evaluations, Zellige outperforms KnapFormer by $1.12$--$1.48\times$ on 16 A800 GPUs and $1.27$--$1.54\times$ on 32 A6000 GPUs.

</details>


### [171] [NIXT: A NCCL Inspector Exporter Tool for Observability of Collective Communication in Large Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01449)
*Ziyang Jia,Sirshak Das,Jason Sewall,Laxmi Bhuyan,Pasha Shamis,Daniel Wong*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: NIXT是一个NCCL Inspector导出工具，通过将NCCL Inspector采集的大量通信性能数据转化为易于访问的分析和可操作的洞察，提升大规模机器学习训练中集体通信的可观测性，并以Nemotron-4在2,048块H100 GPU上的预训练为案例验证其效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器学习工作负载规模扩大，对集体通信性能的可观测性需求日益重要，以识别性能变化并加速根因定位。虽然NCCL发布了NCCL Inspector profiling插件，但其采集的海量数据难以评估和提取可操作洞察。

Method: 提出了NIXT（NCCL Inspector Exporter Tool），该工具将NCCL Inspector的profiling数据转化为易于访问的分析结果和可操作的见解，增强集体通信的可观测性。

Result: 通过在Nvidia H100 GPU集群（最多2,048块GPU）上对Nemotron-4大语言模型预训练进行案例分析，展示了通信阶段如何随机器学习并行策略和GPU规模变化，并实现了性能变异的归因分析和straggler的根因定位。

Conclusion: NIXT能够有效提升集体通信的可观测性，帮助识别性能变化并加速根因定位，在高规模分布式训练场景中具有实用价值。

Abstract: As machine learning workloads scale, it is increasingly important to gain more observability into the performance of collective communication to easily identify performance vari- ations and accelerate root cause identification. Towards this goal, the Nvidia Collective Communication Library (NCCL) introduced NCCL Inspector, a profiler plugin that provides lightweight and continuous reporting of NCCL communication performance statistics. However, the large volume of data collected by NCCL Inspector can be difficult to assess and to extract actionable insights from. This paper presents NIXT, a NCCL Inspector Exporter Tool that improves the observability of collective communication by providing readily accessible analysis and actionable insights from NCCL Inspector profiling. To highlight the benefits of our Exporter Tool, we present a case study of Nemotron-4 LLM pretraining on an Nvidia H100 GPU cluster with up to 2,048 GPUs, demonstrate observability into how communication phases change with ML parallelism and GPU scale, and perform attribution of performance variation and root cause analysis of stragglers.

</details>


### [172] [GPU-Accelerated Multilevel Graph Clustering: A Parallel Perspective on Louvain and Leiden](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01503)
*Michael S. Gilbert,Kamesh Madduri*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了pLouvain和pLeiden两种GPU并行化算法，分别基于Louvain+和Leiden算法，首次实现了保留顺序Leiden全部质量保证的并行版本，在57个图上分别获得3.1倍和8.8倍的速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Louvain和Leiden算法是广泛使用的大规模图聚类方法，但现有并行化方案存在质量损失或性能瓶颈。作者希望通过GPU并行化同时在速度和聚类质量上获得改进，特别是填补顺序Leiden算法无可保证质量的并行实现的空白。

Method: 提出两种GPU并行化方案：pLouvain基于Louvain+扩展；pLeiden通过新颖的基于生成树的细化方法，首次在并行环境下保留了顺序Leiden的全部质量保证。两者均采用轻量级对称破缺技术来模拟顶点有序遍历，并优化了LambdaCC目标函数（模块化的推广）。此外，pLouvain引入了替代迭代策略以修复Louvain/Louvain+中弱内部簇连通性的问题。

Result: 在来自10个家族的57个图上，pLouvain和pLeiden相比现有最快的开源并行化版本分别实现了3.1倍和8.8倍的几何平均加速比。pLouvain在几乎所有测试图上产生了最高的模块度分数。

Conclusion: 两种GPU并行算法在速度和聚类质量上均优于现有并行方案，且其中的子程序可推广至其他基于Louvain的技术，为多级图聚类方法的并行化提供了新思路。

Abstract: The sequential Louvain and Leiden algorithms are widely used techniques for modularity-optimizing clustering (or community detection) in large graphs. We present pLouvain and pLeiden, two new GPU parallelizations. pLouvain is based on the Louvain+ extension. pLeiden is the first parallel implementation to provably preserve all quality guarantees of sequential Leiden. We achieve this through a novel spanning-tree-based refinement approach. Both pLouvain and pLeiden use a lightweight symmetry-breaking technique that emulates an ordered traversal of vertices. For pLouvain, we develop an alternative iteration strategy to rectify the weak internal cluster connectivity observed in Louvain/Louvain+. Further, both pLouvain and pLeiden optimize the LambdaCC objective function, a generalization of modularity and the related Constant Potts model.
  On a collection of 57 graphs from 10 families, our results show that pLouvain and pLeiden achieve geometric mean speedups of 3.1x and 8.8x, respectively, over the current fastest open-source parallelizations of Louvain and Leiden. For the clusterings generated, pLouvain yields the highest modularity scores on nearly all tested graphs. The subroutines within these two multilevel approaches could aid in the parallelization of other Louvain-based techniques.

</details>


### [173] [Source-Bounded Exact Recovery over Docker's Logs API](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01564)
*Kelvin Amoaba*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究通过Docker支持的Logs API实现精确恢复的契约边界。作者定义了"源有界精确性"（source-bounded exactness），并提出了代际感知的多集oracle来区分源截断与采集器遗漏。将该方法应用于LogDeck时，发现了一个启动到挂载竞争和一个单记录附件重叠，通过有限Docker-API调和与精确插入修复。与Grafana Alloy 1.18.0对比，120次采集中LogDeck在60/60中精确，Alloy仅20/60。因果对照实验证明，仅持久化读位置不足以在源生命周期变化后恢复，生命周期重新获取才是精确恢复的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Docker在采集器挂载前或停机期间保留的记录可能被采集器遗漏。持久化的读位置本身并不能保证生命周期变化后的恢复。需要一个精确的恢复契约定义和验证方法。

Method: 提出源有界精确性（source-bounded exactness）定义，要求每个保留的、可区分的源记录最终恰好出现一次在持久化采集器输出中。使用代际感知的多集oracle区分源截断与采集器遗漏，暴露同时丢失与重放。通过该oracle对LogDeck进行检测，发现竞争条件后用有限Docker-API调和及精确插入修复，并与使用相同API和持久化读位置的Grafana Alloy 1.18.0进行对照实验。

Result: 跨120次采集器运行，LogDeck在60/60次中精确，Alloy仅20/60。Alloy在受保护的启动和进程暂停时成功，但在需要发现已退出或重启源时遗漏保留历史。因果对照中，5,000条记录的源在采集前退出：标准发现0/20次精确且一无所获，而给定容器ID的同一读取器在20/20次中精确恢复全部记录。该结果在OrbStack和独立Ubuntu主机（Docker 29.4.0和24.0.9）上复现。两个采集器都能恢复守护进程重启，但都无法恢复源被移除后的记录。

Conclusion: 在保留源范围内，生命周期重新获取（lifecycle reacquisition）而非持久化位置本身决定了精确恢复的成败。这是有界接口声明，不是通用采集器排名或碰撞自由证明。

Abstract: Docker can retain records that a collector misses before attachment or during downtime. A persisted read position does not by itself ensure recovery after lifecycle changes. We study what exact recovery contract is achievable through Docker's supported Logs API. We define source-bounded exactness: every retained, distinguishable source record eventually appears exactly once in durable collector output. Our method uses a generation-aware multiset oracle that separates source truncation from collector omission and exposes simultaneous loss and replay. Applied to LogDeck, it uncovered a start-to-attachment race; a one-record attachment overlap, finite Docker-API reconciliation, and exact insertion closed the tested boundary. We compare the fixed revision with unmodified Grafana Alloy 1.18.0, which uses the same API and persists read positions; across 120 collector-runs, LogDeck was exact in 60/60 and Alloy in 20/60. Alloy succeeded at guarded startup and process pause but omitted retained history when recovery required discovering an exited or restarted source. In a causal control, a 5,000-record source exited before collection: stock discovery was exact in 0/20 trials and acquired nothing, while the same reader given the container ID recovered all records exactly in 20/20. This reproduced on OrbStack and independent Ubuntu hosts with Docker 29.4.0 and 24.0.9; both collectors recovered daemon restart, while neither recovered records after source removal. Exactness assumes distinct tuples of physical generation, timestamp, stream, and bytes; 200,000 byte-identical records across two drivers produced no observed collisions. Our results show that lifecycle reacquisition, not a persisted position alone, determines exact recovery within the retained-source horizon. This is a bounded interface claim, not a universal collector ranking or proof of collision freedom.

</details>


### [174] [Bole: Efficient Tree Speculation for Hybrid-Attention Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01651)
*Li Wang,Yi Su,Xiabao Wu,Chiran You,Yongchao Liu,Zhan Qiu,Juelu Zhang,Jiajun Zheng,Fangxin Liu,Jie Zhang,Chen Tian,Chengying Huan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Bole是一个针对混合注意力大语言模型（如线性注意力+全注意力的混合架构）的高效树投机解码系统。它通过将线性注意力循环转化为树结构的闭式形式，利用高效的GPU内核并行验证所有候选节点，并将投机状态更新无损编码为token级因子，从而显著提升解码吞吐量并降低暂存内存开销。在SGLang生产推理引擎中集成后，相比自回归解码可实现最高4.72倍离线解码吞吐提升，相比最强树投机基线可提升2.03倍，并在在线智能体工作负载中显著降低TTFT和TPOT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合注意力LLM（结合全注意力与循环线性注意力）虽能降低长上下文推理成本，但其自回归解码仍受内存带宽限制。现有的树投机解码系统是为全注意力模型设计的，在混合模型上会逐个遍历循环层分支并为每个候选节点物化完整状态，导致验证延迟和暂存内存随树规模与批次大小显著增长，亟需针对混合注意力架构的树投机解码优化方案。

Method: Bole采用内核-运行时的协同设计：(1) 将线性注意力循环重写为树结构的闭式形式，实现所有候选节点在GPU上的并行验证；(2) 提出资源高效的GPU内核，加速线性注意力树验证；(3) 将投机状态更新无损编码为token级因子，仅在采样后重建所选状态，将暂存状态内存大幅压缩；(4) 集成到SGLang推理引擎，耦合高效状态管理与面向完整混合前向的批次级验证预算分配。

Result: Bole在四个模型、两个GPU平台和多种数据集上，线性注意力树验证速度提升3.4-7.7倍；暂存状态内存降低82-99倍；离线解码吞吐相比自回归解码最高提升4.72倍，相比最强树投机基线最高提升2.03倍；在线智能体工作负载下，相比最强树投机基线TTFT降低最多67.6%，TPOT降低最多49.9%。

Conclusion: Bole通过内核-运行时协同设计，首次实现了针对混合注意力LLM的高效树投机解码，解决了现有树投机系统对混合模型的适配难题，在吞吐量和延迟指标上均显著超越现有基线，展示了通过树结构闭式变换和token级状态编码实现混合注意力模型高效推理的可行性。

Abstract: Hybrid-attention large language models combine full attention with recurrent linear attention to reduce long-context inference costs, yet their autoregressive decoding remains memory-bound. Tree speculative decoding offers an attractive acceleration path, but existing tree-speculation systems are designed around the key--value caches of full-attention models. On hybrid models, they traverse recurrent layers branch by branch and materialize a full state for every proposal node, causing verification latency and transient memory to scale poorly with tree and batch sizes. We present Bole, a kernel--runtime co-design that enables efficient tree speculation for hybrid-attention LLMs. Bole transforms the linear-attention recurrence into a tree-structured closed form and realizes it with a resource-efficient GPU kernel, verifying all proposal nodes in parallel and accelerating linear-attention tree verification by 3.4--7.7$\times$. It losslessly encodes speculative state updates as token-level factors and reconstructs only the state selected after sampling, reducing transient state memory by 82--99$\times$ and freeing GPU capacity for KV caches. Its integration into SGLang, a widely deployed production LLM serving engine, couples efficient state management with a batch-wide verification budget calibrated to the complete hybrid forward. Across four models, two GPU platforms, and diverse datasets, Bole delivers up to $4.72\times$ the offline decode throughput of autoregressive decoding and up to $2.03\times$ that of the strongest tree-speculative baseline. Under online agent workloads, it reduces TTFT and TPOT by up to $67.6%$ and $49.9%$, respectively, over the strongest tree-speculative baseline.

</details>


### [175] [PrefixPlace: Provable Prefix Key-Value Placement for Large Language Model Serving under Heterogeneous Compute and Transfer Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01655)
*Zhiyu Wang,Rajkumar Buyya*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: PrefixPlace是一个epoch级别的KV缓存放置规划器，突破传统基于命中率的放置策略，将硬件条件、前缀深度、KV吞吐和副本位置等因素纳入成本建模，通过单调设施选址目标函数实现KV缓存的优化分配，平均达到最优解的99.84%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前缀KV复用方案中，本地缓存未命中时需重算或从副本拉取，这两种操作的相对代价会随硬件配置、前缀深度、KV吞吐及副本位置变化而不同，导致基于命中率的放置策略并非最优。需要一种考虑多维度成本的规划方法。

Method: 提出epoch级规划器PrefixPlace：(1) 将目标分解为本地副本价值与首副本覆盖率两部分；(2) 源相关成本可建模为单调设施选址问题；(3) 每次worker更新转换为加性有根树问题，用O(nk)时间精确求解（n为块数，k为容量）；(4) 通过固定顺序的1/2近似算法，结合坐标细化和多样化起始顺序进一步提升性能。

Result: 在T4、L4和A100上测量发现不同的成本区间特征；在432个具有精确最优解的实例中平均达到最优的99.84%，最低不低于98.02%。在RAG回放场景中相比vLLM-APC提升40.3%的材料化成本节省，相比最佳离线基线提升6.3%；在WikiQA上分别提升40.4%和5.3%。50,000节点、16 worker的放置求解仅需12.3秒。

Conclusion: PrefixPlace通过显式建模硬件相关的计算/传输成本，实现了接近最优的KV缓存放置，显著优于基于命中率的传统方案，且具备大规模场景下的实时重规划能力。

Abstract: Prefix Key-Value (KV) reuse avoids repeated prefill in Large Language Model (LLM) inference, but local misses require recomputation or replica fetches. Their relative cost varies with hardware, prefix depth, KV goodput, and replica location, making hit-rate-based placement suboptimal. To address this issue, we propose an epoch-level planner, PrefixPlace, which assigns prefix-complete targets under memory budgets and profiled demand, compute, and transfer costs. The objective decomposes into local-copy value plus first-replica coverage, and source-dependent costs yield a monotone facility-location objective; each worker update is an additive rooted-tree problem solved exactly in O(nk) time for n chunks and capacity k, giving a fixed-order 1/2-approximation that coordinate refinement and order-diverse starts improve without weakening. T4, L4, and A100 measurements reveal distinct regimes. Across 432 instances with exact optima, PrefixPlace averages 99.84% of optimum and never falls below 98.02%. In Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) replays, it improves materialization-cost saving by 40.3% over vLLM Automatic Prefix Caching (vLLM-APC) and 6.3% over the best offline baseline. On WikiQA, gains are 40.4% and 5.3%. Finally, PrefixPlace solves a 50,000-node, 16-worker placement in 12.3 s on one processor, enabling timely replanning.

</details>


### [176] [Preserving Admission Responsibility in Multi-Tenant Large Language Model Prefix Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01657)
*Zhiyu Wang,Rajkumar Buyya*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出PrefixShield，一种面向共享GPU缓存的LLM KV缓存管理机制，通过计量新物化的KV块并追踪"责任"（responsibility），解决多租户共享缓存中的"准入-责任鸿沟"（admission-responsibility gap）问题，在vLLM中实现后大幅提升受害租户的缓存命中率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享前缀缓存将GPU内存变为跨LLM租户共享的持久状态。一个租户新物化KV块会迫使其他租户丢失可复用状态，但现有请求时调度器只考虑临时服务，替换策略主要按对象价值排序，静态分区又浪费空闲容量，这种不匹配称为"准入-责任鸿沟"。

Method: PrefixShield的核心机制包括：计量新物化的完整KV块；在请求之间传递责任（responsibility）；在债务（debt）未清偿时阻止复用晋升（gate reuse promotion）；使用预测债务来选择供出逐出候选的租户组。实现基于vLLM。

Result: 在一次污染（one-touch pollution）配对实验中，PrefixShield比LRU提升受害缓存命中率9.39个百分点，比S3-FIFO提升8.64个百分点，在4096块规模下将受害租户命中率从4.92%恢复到84.87%；两遍重放（two-pass replay）下比S3-FIFO提升2.00个百分点；延迟重放下在债务存续期间获得35.16个百分点的优势；同时保持良性ShareGPT行为和保工作保守地访问空闲容量。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明：对象价值信号（object-value signals）决定了保留什么（what to retain），而持久责任（persistent responsibility）决定了哪个租户组承担回收压力（who bears reclamation pressure）。两者互补，PrefixShield弥合了准入-责任鸿沟。

Abstract: Shared prefix caching turns Graphics Processing Unit (GPU) memory into persistent state shared across Large Language Model (LLM) tenants. A group that materializes new Key-Value (KV) blocks can force another to lose reusable state, yet request-time schedulers account for transient service, replacement policies primarily rank object value, and static partitioning strands idle capacity. We call this mismatch the admission-responsibility gap. To close it, we propose PrefixShield, which meters newly materialized full KV blocks, carries responsibility across requests, gates reuse promotion while debt remains, and uses projected debt to select the group supplying eviction candidates. We implement PrefixShield in vLLM. In paired runs under one-touch pollution, PrefixShield improves victim cache hit ratio by 9.39 percentage points over the Least Recently Used (LRU) policy and 8.64 points over S3-FIFO, restoring the victim from 4.92% to 84.87% at 4096-block scale, and gains 2.00 points over S3-FIFO under two-pass replay. It preserves benign ShareGPT behavior and work-conserving access to idle capacity. Delayed replay yields a 35.16-point advantage while debt remains. These results show that object-value signals rank what to retain, while persistent responsibility determines which group bears reclamation pressure.

</details>


### [177] [HorizonServe: Coordinating Request Scheduling with GPU Sharing for Omni-Model Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01785)
*Yuning Zhang,Dong Yuan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出HorizonServe，一个面向单GPU的omni模型（统一文本、语音、图像和多模态推理）服务系统，通过协调请求接纳与GPU分配来满足异构SLO目标。它能显著提升SLO达成率并降低各类首响延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Omni模型将文本、语音、图像和多模态推理统一部署在单一服务后端，但不同输出模态的请求共享初始多模态骨干网络后分流到不同下游生成阶段，导致同一GPU上存在异构的首响指标和SLO目标。现有LLM和多模态服务系统主要优化token进度或输入侧处理，无法同时控制共享阶段的时间共享与并行运行阶段之间的空间共享，缺乏针对异构SLO的联合调度方案。

Method: HorizonServe包含四个核心机制：(1) 对每类请求的首响应延迟进行profiling；(2) 保护松弛时间有限的请求；(3) 在各执行路径之间轮转共享阶段的执行机会；(4) 当下游阶段活跃时，对共享阶段的流式多处理器（SM）分配进行节流。通过请求接纳控制与GPU资源分配协调来实现异构SLO约束下的调度。

Result: 在三种omni模型工作负载和两个GPU平台上，HorizonServe在到达率扫描场景下SLO达成率最高提升4.9倍，在下游密集型流量下最高提升7.0倍，并将各类首响延迟降低38.4%至63.7%。

Conclusion: HorizonServe通过联合协调请求接纳、共享阶段的时间调度和各阶段的空间资源分配，有效解决了omni模型统一部署中的异构SLO调度问题，显著优于现有LLM/多模态服务系统。

Abstract: Omni models unify text, speech, image, and multimodal reasoning in a single serving backend, but this unified deployment exposes a new scheduling problem. Requests with different output modalities may share an initial multimodal backbone and then diverge into downstream generation stages, creating heterogeneous first-response metrics and service-level objective (SLO) targets on the same GPU. Existing large language model (LLM) and multimodal serving systems mainly optimize token progress or input-side processing, and they do not jointly control temporal sharing in the shared stage and spatial sharing among co-running stages. This paper presents HorizonServe, a single-GPU omni-model serving system that coordinates request admission and GPU allocation under heterogeneous SLOs. HorizonServe profiles per-class first-response latency, protects requests with limited slack, rotates shared-stage opportunities across execution paths, and throttles the shared-stage streaming multiprocessor (SM) allocation when downstream stages are active. Across three omni-model workloads and two GPU platforms, HorizonServe improves SLO attainment by up to 4.9$\times$ in arrival-rate sweeps and 7.0$\times$ under downstream-heavy traffic, and reduces per-class first-response latency by 38.4--63.7\%.

</details>


### [178] [FedJigsaw: Multi-Agent Collaborative Model Reassembly for Decentralized Heterogeneous Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01861)
*Jifeng Chen,Haibo Zhang,Yawen Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: FedJigsaw是一个新型的模型异构联邦学习框架，通过将模型个性化重塑为动态、去中心化的模型组装问题，让客户端从邻近客户端学习并组装可复用模块来构建定制模型，而无需依赖预定义架构模板或超网络，在准确率和资源效率上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的部分训练（Partial Training）方法在模型异构联邦学习中通常依赖预定义的架构模板或过参数化的超网络，这限制了细粒度的个性化能力，并带来了显著的计算和内存开销。因此需要一种更灵活、更轻量的个性化模型构建方式。

Method: FedJigsaw包含三个核心组件：（1）AttenAssemble——客户端级注意力机制的模型组装方法，使每个参与者基于本地观测自适应构建定制模型；（2）SymbioArchitect——在通信和隐私约束下，支持客户端与其拓扑邻居交换细粒度模型模块的知识共享机制；（3）CoRe-Tune——注意力增强的集中式训练与去中心化执行策略，用于缓解去中心化模块交换带来的训练不稳定性，引导本地策略促进隐式协作，且不牺牲数据隐私。

Result: 大量实验表明，FedJigsaw相比最先进的MHFL基线方法在相对准确率上提升最高达13.8%，同时显著缩小了客户端之间的性能方差，并在决策延迟和峰值内存占用方面优于现有的策略驱动方法。

Conclusion: FedJigsaw验证了将模型个性化视为去中心化组装问题的有效性，为模型异构联邦学习提供了一种无需预定义模板、兼顾个性化与资源效率的解决方案，在准确率、稳定性和资源开销方面均实现了全面改进。

Abstract: Model Heterogeneous Federated Learning (MHFL) addresses client-level resource heterogeneity by allowing each participant to train a personalized model architecture under a shared training objective. A prevalent paradigm, Partial Training (PT), achieves this by allowing each client to train a subnetwork of the global model. However, existing PT methods typically rely on predefined architectural templates or over-parameterized supernets, limiting fine-grained personalization and imposing substantial computational and memory overhead. We propose FedJigsaw, a novel framework that reshapes model personalization as a dynamic and decentralized model assembly problem. Instead of selecting subnetworks from a predefined supernetwork, each client constructs its model by assembling reusable modules learned from neighboring clients. At the client level, we introduce AttenAssemble to enable each participant to adaptively construct a tailored model based on local observations. To support efficient knowledge sharing under communication and privacy constraints, we design SymbioArchitect, a mechanism that allows clients to exchange granular model modules with their topological neighbors. To mitigate training instability introduced by decentralized module exchange, we design CoRe-Tune, an attention-enhanced centralized training with a decentralized execution strategy, which guides local policies to foster implicit collaboration and stabilize training dynamics, without compromising data privacy. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that FedJigsaw outperforms state-of-the-art MHFL baselines by up to 13.8% in relative accuracy while significantly shrinking cross-client performance variance, but also slashes decision-making latency and peak memory footprint compared to existing policy-driven methods.

</details>


### [179] [Energy-Efficient LLM Serving via Disaggregated Attention--FFN and Flexible Frequency Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01891)
*Cunchen Hu,Liangliang Xu,Tian Liu,Min Lyu,Yongkun Li,Sa Wang,Shuo Quan,Yanan Yang,Wenda Tang,Yiduo Wang,Fu Yu,Jie Wu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: AFlex是一个面向解耦（disaggregated）Attention和FFN服务的联合优化框架，通过全局调度器与局部算子级DVFS控制器协同优化资源分配和GPU频率，并引入交错A/F流水线减少气泡。在NVIDIA A800与Qwen3-32B、Mixtral-8×7B上，能耗每token最高降低49%（vs SOTA解耦服务）和48%（vs频率缩放系统），同时满足TTFT和TPOT的SLO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务能耗管理方法仅在请求级或推理阶段级调整GPU频率，忽略了Attention算子和FFN算子在频率敏感性上的本质差异。研究发现A/F两类算子的能量最优频率不同，且随推理阶段、工作负载和系统配置动态变化，而运行时可变性和独立频率控制导致搜索空间大、通信开销高，亟需联合优化方案。

Method: AFlex包含三个核心组件：1）全局调度器，联合决定A/F资源分配；2）局部算子级DVFS控制器，独立管理Attention和FFN的GPU频率；3）交错A/F流水线，支持动态微批次深度与自适应请求批处理，以减少流水线气泡。系统在SGLang中实现，针对解耦A/F架构进行端到端优化。

Result: 在NVIDIA A800 GPU上使用Qwen3-32B和Mixtral-8×7B模型，基于生产环境的Conversation和Coding工作负载轨迹评估。AFlex相比最先进的解耦服务系统能耗每token降低最高49%，相比频率缩放系统降低最高48%，同时保持TTFT和TPOT满足SLO要求。

Conclusion: AFlex证明了算子级粒度（Attention与FFN分别调频）结合资源供给与DVFS的联合优化，能够在不牺牲SLO的前提下显著降低LLM服务的能耗，为高能效LLM推理提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) serving spans diverse applications with stringent service-level objectives (SLOs), often requiring GPUs to run at maximum frequencies and increasing energy consumption. Existing energy-management approaches adapt GPU frequencies only at the request or inference-phase level, overlooking operator-level differences in frequency sensitivity between Attention and feed-forward networks (FFNs). We find that the energy-optimal frequencies of Attention and FFN (A/F) differ and vary with the inference phase, workload, and system configurations. However, runtime variability and independent A/F frequency control create a large search space and high communication overhead. To address these challenges, we present AFlex, a framework that jointly optimizes resource provisioning and GPU frequency scaling for disaggregated A/F serving. AFlex introduces a global scheduler and a local operator-level dynamic voltage and frequency scaling (DVFS) controller to determine A/F resource allocations and frequencies. It further introduces an interleaved A/F pipeline with dynamic microbatch depth and adaptive request batching to reduce pipeline bubbles. We implement AFlex in SGLang and evaluate it on NVIDIA A800 GPUs using Qwen3-32B and Mixtral-8$\times$7B under production Conversation and Coding traces. \AFlex reduces energy per token by up to 49\% over state-of-the-art disaggregated serving and 48\% over frequency-scaling systems while satisfying TTFT and TPOT SLOs.

</details>


### [180] [Diagnosing High-Performance BFT Consensus via Mixture Modeling of Block Time Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01934)
*Hongru He,Akihiro Fujihara*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于quorum多播框架的混合模型方法，用于诊断基于HotStuff的高性能BFT共识协议中的区块时间分布。通过将每个区块间隔与quorum形成延迟关联，使用混合模型捕捉多模态分布，并将该方法应用于Hyperliquid和Aptos主网。结果显示Hyperliquid呈单峰分布（验证者部署同质），而Aptos呈持续多模态结构并在共识升级后出现明显偏移（部署异质），表明该方法可作为分析和监控高性能BFT共识的有效诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能BFT区块链虽然旨在实现高吞吐和低延迟，但其实际区块时间分布往往因网络、流水线和部署异质性而表现出复杂行为，缺乏有效的诊断工具来理解和监控这些复杂现象。

Method: 提出quorum-based多播框架，将每个区块间隔与quorum形成延迟相关联；采用混合模型（mixture models）捕捉区块时间的多模态分布，每个分量代表一种由区块信息有效传输速率表征的网络条件；对主网区块时间数据的主体部分进行拟合，同时对尾部衰减进行单独分析以评估渐近行为。

Result: 应用于Hyperliquid和Aptos主网：Hyperliquid由单峰分布很好地解释，与相对同质的验证者部署一致；Aptos则呈现持续的多模态结构，并在共识升级后出现显著偏移，反映了异质部署和多样化通信路径。

Conclusion: 区块时间的混合建模为分析和监控高性能BFT共识提供了一种实用且信息丰富的诊断工具。

Abstract: High-performance Byzantine Fault Tolerant (BFT) blockchains are designed to achieve high throughput and low latency, yet their observed block time distributions often reveal complex behaviors arising from networking, pipelining, and deployment heterogeneity. In this paper, we diagnose HotStuff-based high-performance BFT consensus by modeling block times through a quorum-based multicast framework that links each block interval to quorum formation latency. We capture multimodal block time distributions using mixture models, where each component represents a distinct network condition characterized by effective transfer rate of block information. The proposed model is fitted to the bulk of mainnet block time data, while tail decay is analyzed separately to assess asymptotic behavior. Applying this methodology to Hyperliquid and Aptos mainnets, we find that Hyperliquid is well explained by a unimodal distribution, consistent with a relatively homogeneous validator deployment. In contrast, Aptos exhibits persistent multimodal structure and a pronounced shift following a consensus upgrade, reflecting heterogeneous deployments and diverse communication paths. These results demonstrate that mixture modeling of block time provides a practical and informative diagnostic tool for analyzing and monitoring high-performance BFT consensus.

</details>


### [181] [TALSC: Timeliness-Aware Large-Small VLM Collaboration for Infrastructure-Assisted Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01998)
*Mengmeng Zhu,Yuxuan Sun,Wei Chen,Bo Ai*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出TALSC（时效感知的大小视觉语言模型协作）框架，解决自动驾驶中车载小VLM能力不足与边缘大VLM延迟导致信息时效性下降之间的矛盾，通过Lyapunov在线调度算法在精度与时延之间取得最优平衡，并在nuScenes数据集上实现最高12.6%的Micro-F1分数提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶中的视觉语言模型部署受限于车载算力，只能使用小VLM，感知和推理能力有限。基础设施辅助驾驶虽然可以通过边缘服务器协作使用大VLM，但在动态车联网环境中，感知数据的时效性衰减迅速，大VLM的推理延迟会加剧信息老化，导致任务性能下降。因此需要在LVLM带来的精度增益与其延迟导致的时效性退化之间进行权衡。

Method: 首先建立VLM推理的AoI（信息年龄）演化模型，刻画AoI、token长度与任务性能之间的耦合关系，提出通用时效性度量。在此基础上设计了TALSC在线调度算法。由于调度决策对未来时效性度量具有延迟影响且输出token数在调度时未知，采用Lyapunov漂移加估计惩罚算法，并提供性能保证。

Result: 基于nuScenes数据集进行案例研究拟合时效性度量，并在多种通信和计算场景下验证TALSC优于基线方法，相比最优基线实现最高12.6%的归一化Micro-F1分数提升。

Conclusion: TALSC框架有效协调了大VLM的精度优势与小VLM的低延迟优势，通过时效感知的在线调度在动态车联网环境中实现了感知任务性能的显著提升，为基础设施辅助自动驾驶的资源分配提供了新的理论框架和实用算法。

Abstract: The deployment of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) in autonomous driving (AD) systems is constrained by on-board computing power, restricting vehicles to small VLMs (SVLMs) with limited perception and reasoning capabilities. Infrastructure-assisted AD alleviates this resource constraint by enabling collaboration with large VLMs (LVLMs) at edge servers. However, in dynamic vehicular environments, the utility of sensory data for downstream tasks decays rapidly, making timeliness of information a critical concern. To balance the accuracy gains of LVLMs with their latency-induced timeliness degradation, we develop a Timeliness-Aware Large-Small VLM Collaboration (TALSC) framework. Specifically, we first model the Age of Information (AoI) evolution for VLM inference and characterize the coupling among AoI, token length, and task performance to formulate a general timeliness metric. Building on this, we propose the TALSC online scheduling algorithm. Since scheduling decisions have a delayed impact on future timeliness metric and the output token number is unknown at scheduling time, we design a Lyapunov drift-plus-estimated-penalty algorithm and provides a guaranteed performance. In simulation, we first conduct a case study to derive a fitted timeliness metric based on nuScenes dataset, and further show that TALSC outperforms baselines under various communication and computing settings, achieving up to a 12.6\% normalized improvement in Micro-F1 score compared with the best-performing baseline.

</details>


### [182] [Learning-Based Collaborative MEC for LLM Inference with Soft-Deadline Awareness via Transformer-Enhanced PPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02031)
*Ngoc Hung Nguyen,Bjorn Landfeldt*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究协作式移动边缘计算（MEC）服务器在软截止时间约束下进行大语言模型（LLM）推理的问题，提出了一种带有限制灵活性的扩展截止时间机制，并开发了基于Transformer增强的PPO框架来实现MEC服务器间的高效协作，以最大化截止时间内完成的任务数并最小化截止时间扩展的使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM推理场景中，任务间存在依赖关系，任何一个子任务错过截止时间都可能导致整个请求产生灾难性后果。因此需要在严格的延迟约束下高效调度计算任务，同时限制截止时间扩展的次数，这是一个具有挑战性的优化问题。

Method: 提出扩展截止时间机制（带约束灵活性），并开发Transformer增强的PPO（近端策略优化）框架。Transformer用于捕获任务间的时间依赖性和跨服务器交互，从而改进任务迁移的决策，实现MEC服务器间的协同工作。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的方法在任务完成率和系统整体效率方面显著优于传统PPO和基于启发式的方法。

Conclusion: Transformer增强的PPO框架能够有效解决LLM推理中带有任务依赖的截止时间约束调度问题，在保证服务质量的同时减少截止时间扩展的使用。

Abstract: This paper investigates collaborative mobile edge computing (MEC) servers for large language model (LLM) inference under soft deadline constraints. In this system, to improve the quality of service, computations are expected to be completed within their deadlines. However, due to dependencies among tasks or subtasks, any missed deadline can lead to catastrophic consequences for the entire request. In this context, this work proposes an extended deadline mechanism with constrained flexibility. The main challenges lie in handling large-scale computations under strict latency constraints while limiting the number of allowable deadline extensions, especially in the presence of task dependencies within each request. To tackle these challenges, we develop a transformer-enhanced proximal policy optimization (PPO) framework that enables efficient collaboration among MEC servers. The proposed approach aims to maximize the number of tasks completed within their deadlines while minimizing the use of deadline extensions. By capturing temporal dependencies and cross-server interactions, the transformer improves decision-making for task migration. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed method significantly outperforms conventional PPO and heuristic-based approaches in terms of task completion rate and overall system efficiency.

</details>


### [183] [DEFT: Joint Task Placement and DVFS for Energy-Efficient Multi-GPU Runtimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02122)
*Jing Chen,Miquel Pericas*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DEFT是一个面向多GPU任务系统的能耗感知调度框架，联合优化任务到设备的分配和每GPU的DVFS配置，通过成本模型驱动的策略在CUDASTF运行时中实现，在NVIDIA L40S和L4上分别降低能耗14.8%和4.8%，同时保持性能损失在1.5%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运行时系统将DVFS与任务放置和GPU间通信解耦，聚焦单GPU执行，或无法在多GPU环境中适应任务粒度和运行时竞争。因此当前调度器未能捕捉任务放置、频率选择和GPU间数据移动之间的紧密耦合关系，而这正是多GPU系统能量-性能权衡的根本决定因素。

Method: DEFT采用成本模型驱动策略，集成松弛感知、吞吐量感知，显式建模任务执行成本、GPU间数据移动和DVFS转换开销，在任务粒度下根据动态运行时条件实现协调的放置与频率决策。原型实现于CUDASTF运行时，并针对五个优化目标进行验证。

Result: 在NVIDIA L40S上平均降低能耗14.8%，在NVIDIA L4上平均降低4.8%；EDP分别降低9.9%和3.7%，且性能保持在最快基线方案的1.5%以内。

Conclusion: DEFT证明了在多GPU环境下联合优化任务放置与DVFS配置能够在不显著牺牲性能的前提下有效降低能耗，验证了成本模型驱动方法在动态运行时条件下进行任务粒度协同决策的有效性。

Abstract: Energy efficiency has become a first-order concern in modern high-performance computing systems, as it directly determines achievable throughput under fixed power budgets. Although Dynamic Voltage and Frequency Scaling (DVFS) provides an effective mechanism for reducing GPU energy consumption, existing runtime systems decouple DVFS from task placement and inter-GPU communication, focus on single-GPU execution, or cannot adapt frequency to task granularity and runtime contention in multi-GPU environments. Consequently, current schedulers fail to capture the tight coupling between task placement, frequency selection, and inter-GPU data movement that fundamentally governs energy-performance trade-offs on multi-GPU systems.
  This paper presents DEFT, an energy-aware scheduling framework that jointly optimizes task-to-device assignment and per-GPU DVFS configuration for task-based multi-GPU applications. DEFT employs a cost-model-driven strategy that integrates slack awareness, throughput awareness, and explicit modeling of task execution cost, inter-GPU data movement, and DVFS transition overheads, enabling coordinated placement and frequency decisions at task granularity under dynamic runtime conditions. We prototype DEFT within the CUDASTF runtime and demonstrate its effectiveness across five optimization objectives. The evaluation shows that DEFT reduces energy consumption by 14.8% and 4.8% on average on NVIDIA L40S and L4, and reduces EDP by 9.9% and 3.7%, respectively, while maintaining performance within 1.5% of the fastest baseline.

</details>


### [184] [Efficiency and Cost Alignment in Batched LLM Serving via Resource-Fair Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02244)
*Dayi Yao,Zijie Zhou*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了批量大语言模型（LLM）服务中的资源分配低效问题：异构请求共享解码批次时，最大KV缓存足迹主导批次的计算成本，导致短请求与长请求同批处理时产生不成比例的资源消耗和延迟。作者将这一现象形式化为资源公平调度问题，提出ISJL算法（带限制的短作业插入），并证明其全局竞争比下界为3/4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在批量LLM推理服务中，由于同批次请求的解码步骤墙钟时间主要由最大的活跃KV缓存足迹决定，短请求与长请求共批时会产生"串扰"成本，即短请求被迫承担与其自身令牌工作负载不成比例的延迟和GPU资源消耗。这种最大驱动（max-driven）的计算成本结构导致了资源分配的不公平和低效，而现有的批量调度策略（如FCFS和LJF）均无法同时兼顾公平性与吞吐量。

Method: 本文首先建立一个数学调度模型，将批内资源公平性与系统吞吐量联系起来：公平性约束限制了同批请求之间解码进度（即KV缓存足迹）的差异。基于该模型，设计了一种参数化的混合批处理策略——Insert-Short-Jobs-with-Limit（ISJL）算法。该算法限定了短作业插入的边界，在批次灵活性和成本对齐之间取得平衡。通过竞争比分析证明ISJL的全局下界为3/4，并在令牌计费的商业API定价约定下考察利润含义，进行数值实验对比。

Result: 证明ISJL算法达到3/4的全局竞争比下界；数值实验表明ISJL在FCFS（批处理外部性大但灵活）与LJF（成本对齐但牺牲批次灵活性）之间占据有利的中间位置，实现了双准则调度目标：保持高吞吐量的同时，使最大驱动的批次成本与令牌计费收入对齐。

Conclusion: ISJL提供了一种双准则调度策略，在资源公平性（即费用合理性）与系统吞吐量之间取得了良好折中，为批量LLM服务中的调度问题提供了理论保证和实用性兼顾的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper studies a resource-allocation inefficiency in batched large language model (LLM) serving: heterogeneous requests that share a decode batch impose max-driven computational costs on one another. Because the wall-clock cost of a batch step is largely governed by the largest active KV-cache footprint, a short request co-batched with a long request can experience latency and GPU-resource consumption disproportionate to its own token workload. We formalize this phenomenon as a resource-fair scheduling problem. We develop a mathematical scheduling model that connects within-batch resource fairness to system throughput. The proposed fairness constraint bounds the disparity in decode progress, equivalently KV-cache footprint, among co-batched requests. Based on this model, we design the Insert-Short-Jobs-with-Limit (ISJL) algorithm, a parameterized hybrid batching policy. We prove that ISJL achieves a global competitive-ratio lower bound of $3/4$. We further examine the profit implications of resource-fair scheduling under the token-metered pricing convention used by commercial LLM APIs. Numerical experiments show that ISJL occupies a favorable middle ground between FCFS, which has large batching externalities, and LJF, which is cost-aligned but sacrifices batching flexibility. Thus, ISJL provides a bi-criterion scheduling policy: it maintains high throughput while aligning max-driven batch cost with token-metered revenue.

</details>


### [185] [Epico: Long-Lived WebAssembly Components for High-Performance Serverless Stream Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02361)
*Matteo Della Bartola,Valerio Besozzi,Patrizio Dazzi,Marco Danelutto*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Epico是一个专为流处理设计的serverless运行时，通过持久化WebAssembly组件、基于队列深度的自动扩缩容和无代理的ZeroMQ通道来替代传统FaaS模型，将冷启动延迟从数百毫秒降至亚毫秒级，并提升单worker吞吐量4.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流serverless计算的FaaS模型不适合流处理，因为其无状态、集中式编排的函数无法高效处理持续、低延迟的事件流。

Method: Epico在运行时层面解决FaaS流处理的低效问题：(1) 将流水线阶段作为持久化WebAssembly组件执行，支持基于队列深度SLO的独立零到无限自动扩缩容；(2) 使用无代理的ZeroMQ通道在阶段间直接路由事件；(3) 采用基于信用的滑动窗口摊销进程间通信成本，优化短执行路径；(4) 使用Ahead-of-Time（AOT）编译降低冷启动延迟。

Result: AOT编译将冷启动延迟从数百毫秒降至亚毫秒级；信用窗口将单worker吞吐量提升至4.3倍；与Apache OpenWhisk相比，Epico绕过了编排器瓶颈和容器开销。

Conclusion: Epico通过运行时层的针对性设计，有效解决了FaaS模型在流处理场景中的固有缺陷，为serverless流处理提供了更高效的执行方案。

Abstract: While serverless computing is popular, its dominant Function-as-a-Service (FaaS) model is ill-suited for stream processing because its stateless, centrally orchestrated functions cannot efficiently handle continuous, low-latency event flows.
