Motivation: 基于知识的科学分析中，结构化证据本身并不能表示可执行计算所需的分析知识。证据分配、分析对比、结局和时间点对齐、效应量公式及方法学准入等决策必须在统计执行前明确化。现有自动化方法往往将这些决策嵌入模型输出、生成代码或工作流痕迹中，而非将其表示为可独立验证的知识，缺乏形式化验证和可追溯性。

Method: 提出Executable Analytical Knowledge Representation (EAKR)，一种机器可操作的表示形式，用于编码将结构化证据转化为可执行元分析所需的知识，包括证据、关系、数值输入、约束、来源和未解决问题。将EAKR实例化为MetaSynDec智能代理框架：大语言模型提出结构化更新，确定性服务负责基于模式和契约的验证与执行。

Result: 在58个合成单元中，MetaSynDec构建了全部EAKR，57个进入统计执行。56个具备足够信息定义参考分析对象的单元中，38个（67.9%）实现完整对象保真度，42个（75.0%）精确证据集一致，平均Jaccard相似度为0.909。55个单元中54个（98.2%）生成与发表的置信区间重叠。在参考合成结构一致性上，MetaSynDec优于直接LLM生成（57/58 vs 23/58；p&lt;0.001）；在23个共同完成单元中，精确参考公式一致性为23/23 vs 1/23（p&lt;0.001）。

Conclusion: 研究提供了可行性证据，表明EAKR支持形式化验证、可追溯性、统计执行，并在方法学一致性上显著优于直接LLM生成，为知识驱动的可复现科学分析提供了新的表示范式。

Abstract: Meta-analysis synthesis highlights a fundamental challenge in knowledge-based scientific analysis: structured evidence does not by itself represent the analytical knowledge required for executable computation. Decisions about evidence assignment, analytical contrasts, outcome and time-point alignment, effect-size formulation, and methodological admissibility must be explicit before statistical execution. Existing automated approaches often embed these decisions in model outputs, generated code, or workflow traces rather than representing them as independently verifiable knowledge. We introduce the Executable Analytical Knowledge Representation (EAKR), a machine-actionable representation of the knowledge required to transform structured evidence into executable meta-analysis. An EAKR represents evidence, relations, numerical inputs, constraints, provenance, and unresolved issues. We operationalise EAKR in MetaSynDec, an agentic harness in which large language models propose structured updates and deterministic services govern schema- and contract-based validation and execution. Across 58 synthesis units, MetaSynDec constructed all EAKRs, with 57 proceeding to statistical execution. Of 56 units with sufficient information to define a reference analysis object, 38 (67.9%) achieved complete object fidelity and 42 (75.0%) exact evidence-set agreement, with a mean Jaccard similarity of 0.909. Generated and published confidence intervals overlapped in 54 of 55 units (98.2%). MetaSynDec outperformed direct LLM generation in reference synthesis-structure agreement (57/58 versus 23/58; p<0.001) and among 23 jointly completed units, exact reference-formulation agreement (23/23 versus 1/23; p<0.001). These findings provide feasibility evidence that EAKR supports formal validation, traceability, statistical execution, and improved methodological agreement relative to direct LLM generation.

</details>


### [316] [LaCache: Robust Semantic Caching for LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01718)
*Jiacheng Liang,Yuhui Wang,Tanqiu Jiang,Ting Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LaCache是一种新型语义缓存方案，通过额外检查查询前k个（推测性解码的）token的缓存命中来解决缓存碰撞攻击问题，既提供了形式化的安全保证，又提升了缓存检索的语义相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义缓存方案基于嵌入相似度复用响应，但本质上容易受到缓存碰撞攻击——攻击者注入精心构造的查询来污染缓存，从而破坏后续合法请求的响应。现有方案缺乏对此类安全漏洞的有效防护。

Method: 关键洞见：攻击者虽然能完全控制对抗查询的内容，但对响应的控制力远弱，因为响应必须同时满足多个语义约束。LaCache不仅检查查询的缓存命中，还额外检查其前k个（推测性解码的）token的缓存命中。这一设计通过双重校验实现安全性，同时丰富的索引为缓存检索提供额外语义上下文。

Result: 论文形式化证明了LaCache无法构造能同时引发恶意响应并与良性查询碰撞的对抗查询；在多种LLM和基准上的经验评估验证了其安全保证和效率提升。

Conclusion: LaCache为鲁棒的语义缓存指明了有前景的方向，在不牺牲效率的前提下提供了针对缓存碰撞攻击的形式化安全保障。

Abstract: Semantic caching, which reuses responses to semantically similar requests via their embeddings, has seen growing adoption in LLM serving, offering faster responses and reduced costs. Yet existing schemes are fundamentally vulnerable to cache-collision attacks, wherein an adversary pollutes the cache by injecting crafted queries, corrupting responses to subsequent legitimate requests. We present LaCache, a novel semantic caching scheme that addresses this vulnerability through a conceptually simple yet principled redesign. The key insight is that while the adversary has full control over the adversarial query, it has far less control over its response, which must simultaneously satisfy multiple semantic constraints. Rather than checking only the cache hit of a query, LaCache additionally checks the cache hit of its first k (speculatively) decoded tokens. This design yields two concrete benefits. First, it provides formally guaranteed resilience against cache-collision attacks: we prove that it is impossible to craft adversarial queries that simultaneously elicit malicious responses and collide with benign queries. Second, the enriched index supplies additional semantic context for cache retrieval, improving response relevance. Empirical evaluation across diverse LLMs and benchmarks validates both LaCache's security guarantees and efficiency gains, pointing to a promising direction for robust semantic caching.

</details>


### [317] [DAPD: Dual-Anchored Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01735)
*Jianyu Wu,Yizhou Wang,Encheng Su,Chen Tang,Shixiang Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了双重锚定策略蒸馏（DAPD）框架，用于解决在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）中的"特权幻觉"问题——即学生模型学到了依赖训练时特权信息、但推理时无法复现的行为。DAPD通过双路径锚定和双源锚定两个层次的锚定机制，消除特权教师与学生之间的信息不对称，在Qwen3模型上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD方法虽被广泛用于语言模型后训练，但存在"特权幻觉"问题：教师模型利用训练时的特权信息强化自身，学生虽然学到了依赖这些特权信息的行为，却在推理时无法获得这些信息，导致行为不一致且性能下降。核心根因是特权教师与推理时学生之间存在信息不对称。

Method: 提出DAPD统一框架，包含两个层次的锚定机制：(1) 双路径锚定（DPA）：引入自条件桥接，沿两条信息匹配的路径对齐参考行为和 rollout 行为，防止特权依赖行为转移到推理时的学生；(2) 双源锚定（DSA）：在参考到rollout和rollout到参考两个方向上应用这些路径，减少对特权参考指导的依赖，同时保留正确性监督。

Result: 实验表明，DAPD显著缓解了特权幻觉问题，在Qwen3-4B上平均超越OPSD 2.00分；增益在不同规模下保持一致，4B规模达到+2.69，32B规模达到+2.78。

Conclusion: DAPD通过双层次锚定有效解决了OPSD中的信息不对称问题，在不同模型规模上均展现出稳定且显著的性能提升，为语言模型蒸馏后训练提供了更可靠的方案。

Abstract: On-policy (self) distillation (OPSD) is increasingly adopted for language-model post-training. It strengthens the teacher with privileged information but can induce a privilege illusion: the student learns privilege-dependent behavior it cannot reproduce from its inference-time context, yet behaves as if the training-time privileged information remained available, ultimately degrading performance. In this paper, we identify information asymmetry between the privileged teacher and the student at inference as the root cause of this failure in OPSD. To resolve this asymmetry, we propose Dual-Anchored Policy Distillation (DAPD), a unified framework with two levels of anchoring. Dual-Path Anchoring (DPA) introduces a self-conditioned bridge and aligns reference and rollout behavior along two matched-information paths, preventing privilege-dependent behavior from being transferred to the inference-time student. Dual-Source Anchoring (DSA) applies these paths in both reference-to-rollout and rollout-to-reference directions, reducing reliance on privileged reference guidance while preserving correctness supervision. Extensive experiments show that DAPD significantly alleviates privilege illusion, outperforming OPSD on Qwen3-4B by +2.00 points on average across tasks. Notably, its gains persist across scales, reaching +2.69 at 4B and +2.78 at 32B.

</details>


### [318] [CoEvo-Mem: Co-Evolving Retrieval Policy and Memory Bank for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01739)
*Bowen Ye,Yongchao Xu,Zhijian Li,Xiang Yin,Junkai Ma,Wenzhao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoEvo-Mem是一个闭环框架，通过交替优化检索策略（轻量残差路由器）与记忆库（值更新和图关系演化），实现长期LLM智能体的检索与记忆共同进化，在七个基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期LLM智能体方法将检索优化和记忆演化分离处理，忽略了二者之间的关键反馈回路：检索决定哪些记忆获得使用信号，而更新后的记忆库又会重塑未来的检索结果。这种分离限制了长期任务中查询特定检索与记忆持续精炼之间的协同效果。

Method: CoEvo-Mem提出一个闭环框架，包含三个核心设计：(1) 冻结LLM生成路由特定的查询改写和路由先验，轻量残差路由器在线校正；(2) 检索到的上下文作为耦合接口——任务结果将信用分配给路由决策，轨迹条件反馈更新记忆值和图关系；(3) 为缓解耦合导致的非平稳性，交替执行路由器更新（固定记忆库）和记忆库演化（固定检索策略）。

Result: 在七个不同的基准数据集上，CoEvo-Mem取得了最先进的性能，验证了检索-记忆共同进化的重要性。

Conclusion: 检索策略与记忆库之间存在本质的协同进化关系，通过闭环设计将二者联合优化而非孤立优化，能显著提升长期LLM智能体在跨任务、跨会话累积记忆场景下的性能表现。

Abstract: As memories accumulate across tasks and sessions, the performance of long-term LLM agents depends jointly on query-specific retrieval and continual memory refinement. However, existing methods typically optimize either memory access, through iterative query refinement or adaptive retrieval policies, or memory evolution such as structural update. This separation overlooks a fundamental feedback loop: retrieval determines which memories receive usage signals, while updated memory bank reshape future retrieval. We propose \textbf{CoEvo-Mem}, a closed-loop framework for co-evolving the retrieval policy and memory bank. For each query, a frozen LLM generates route-specific query rewrites and a routing prior, which a lightweight residual router corrects online. The retrieved context serves as the coupling interface between the two learning processes: task outcomes assign credit to routing decisions, while trajectory-conditioned feedback updates memory values and graph relations. These updates alter how memories are ranked and selected for subsequent queries, thereby closing the feedback loop. To mitigate coupling induced non-stationarity, CoEvo-Mem alternates between updating the router with the memory bank fixed and evolving the memory bank with the retrieval policy fixed. Across seven diverse benchmarks, \textbf{CoEvo-Mem} achieves state-of-the-art performance, demonstrating the importance of retrieval-memory coevolution.

</details>


### [319] [MemSIF: From Structured Interactions to Dual-Track Fact Memory for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01742)
*YuFei Luo,Xiucheng Xu,Zhen Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MemSIF框架，通过结构化交互-事实双轨记忆系统应对长期交互场景中LLM智能体的记忆问题，分别解决时序-结构错配（TSM）和延迟效用显现（DUM）两类系统性缺陷，在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-S数据集上超越最强基线2.29%-8.79%和2.87%-6.15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期记忆对LLM智能体在长时程交互中至关重要，但现有记忆系统的持久局限源于两种反复出现的错配模式：时序-结构错配（时间邻近性与主题/事件级关联不一致）和延迟效用显现（写入时显著性与未来查询效用不对齐）。这两类问题制约了记忆系统在实际长期交互中的效果。

Method: MemSIF包含两大核心组件：1) 结构化交互记忆（Structured Interaction Memory）：将原始交互组织为主题片段（Topical Segments）以保持局部主题连贯性，以及事件轨迹（Event Trajectories）以维持跨时间的事件连续性；2) 双轨事实记忆（Dual-Track Fact Memory）：CoreFact记忆在写入时整合稳定的、模式引导的信息；ActiveFact记忆按需形成事实，并促进那些得到多个历史来源支持且满足反复查询需求的事实被重用。

Result: 在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-S数据集上、覆盖五个骨干LLM的实验中，MemSIF在所有设置中均获得最高的Total ACC：在LoCoMo上超越最强基线2.29%-8.79%，在LongMemEval-S上超越2.87%-6.15%。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持将结构化交互记忆与双轨事实记忆相结合能有效缓解TSM和DUM问题，从而提升LLM智能体在长期交互场景下的记忆表现。

Abstract: Long-term memory is critical for LLM agents operating over long-horizon interactions. However, several persistent limitations of existing memory systems can be traced to two recurring misalignment patterns in long-term interaction settings: Temporal-Structural Misalignment (TSM) and Delayed Utility Manifestation (DUM). TSM arises when temporal proximity does not reliably align with topical or event-level relatedness, whereas DUM arises when write-time salience does not reliably predict future query utility. To mitigate these misalignment patterns, we propose MemSIF (Memory with Structured Interactions and Facts), a structured interaction-to-fact memory framework. Structured Interaction Memory organizes raw interactions into Topical Segments that preserve local topical coherence and Event Trajectories that maintain cross-time event continuity. Dual-Track Fact Memory uses two complementary tracks: CoreFact memory consolidates stable, schema-guided information at write time, whereas ActiveFact memory forms facts on demand and promotes those supported by multiple historical sources and recurring query demand for reuse. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval-S across five backbone LLMs show that MemSIF achieves the highest Total ACC in all settings, outperforming the strongest baseline by 2.29%-8.79% on LoCoMo and 2.87%-6.15% on LongMemEval-S. These results support the effectiveness of combining Structured Interaction Memory with Dual-Track Fact Memory to mitigate TSM and DUM. Code is available at https://github.com/luoyufeihaha/MemSIF.

</details>


### [320] [RL-Lock: Reinforcement Learning for Generating Interlocking Assemblies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01744)
*Xuyang Ma,Chaewoon Kim,Haonan Zhang,Rulin Chen,Ziqi Wang,Peng Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RL-Lock，这是首个基于强化学习框架的互锁装配体生成方法，将目标3D体素对象分解为指定数量的互锁部件，无需依赖手工设计的搜索启发式，通过结构化动作分块与MCTS引导的策略-价值学习来高效探索大型组合搜索空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互锁装配体在现实应用中广泛使用，因其结构稳定性。现有方法将互锁装配体生成问题建模为形状分解问题，但依赖手工设计的搜索启发式，在复杂场景下耗时过长甚至无法找到有效解。作者观察到该问题本质上是一个序列决策问题，因此希望利用强化学习来替代手工启发式搜索。

Method: RL-Lock将生成互锁装配体的过程建模为马尔可夫决策过程，智能体逐步决定每个体素应分配给哪个部件。方法结合了结构化动作分块（structured action chunking）与MCTS（蒙特卡洛树搜索）引导的策略-价值学习，以高效导航互锁装配体生成中大型组合搜索空间。

Result: 实验表明，RL-Lock能够有效生成互锁装配体，尤其在具有挑战性的场景中表现突出——现有方法耗时过长甚至无法找到有效解的情况下，RL-Lock仍能成功生成。

Conclusion: RL-Lock作为首个基于强化学习的互锁装配体生成框架，克服了传统方法对手工搜索启发式的依赖，在复杂场景下展现出更优的有效性和效率。

Abstract: An interlocking assembly is an assembly in which component parts are connected purely through their geometric arrangement, without relying on external connectors such as glue and nails. Such assemblies have been widely used in a variety of real-world applications due to their structural stability. The problem of generating interlocking assemblies is generally formulated as a shape decomposition problem, where a target 3D object represented as a voxel grid is partitioned into a prescribed number of interlocking pieces. We observe that generating interlocking assemblies is inherently a sequential decision-making problem, where an agent repeatedly decides which piece each voxel should be assigned to. Inspired by the observation, we propose the first reinforcement learning framework RL-Lock for generating interlocking assemblies, without relying on handcrafted search heuristics as existing works did. RL-Lock combines structured action chunking with MCTS-guided policy-value learning to efficiently navigate the large combinatorial search space for interlocking assembly generation. We demonstrate through experiments that RL-Lock allows effective generation of interlocking assemblies, especially for challenging cases in which existing approaches take too long or even fail to find a valid solution.

</details>


### [321] [Deferred Exposure of Future Trajectories for Verifiable Reasoning in Autonomous Driving VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01755)
*Zixuan Huang,Yang Zhou,Kaixuan Wang,Guli Zhang,Hongyan Xie,Yakun Zhu,Hao Geng,Yikun Ban,Deqing Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对自动驾驶中视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在链式思维（CoT）标注中暴露地面真值（GT）未来轨迹所导致的轨迹锚定偏差问题，提出AD-MCQ（自动驾驶多项选择题）和DEFT-RLVR（延迟暴露未来轨迹用于RLVR）两种方法，将规划任务转化为显式轨迹候选的选择问题，并在决策后暴露未来轨迹作为验证目标，从而在不进行开放式轨迹生成的前提下实现可验证的自动驾驶推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在AD中依赖CoT监督增强VLM推理能力，但标注管线将教师模型暴露于记录的GT未来轨迹，导致轨迹锚定偏差——教师模型不是从场景证据推断决策，而是为已揭示的结果进行合理化解释，产生因果不忠实且更严重的幻觉输出。这尤其在有因果挑战的场景中更为严重，因此需要消除这一捷径。

Method: 本文提出两种互补方法：(1) AD-MCQ：将规划任务转化为从显式轨迹候选中进行选择的多项选择题，避免开放式轨迹生成，使轨迹级驾驶决策可验证；(2) DEFT-RLVR：将未来轨迹的暴露从决策前锚点转变为决策后的验证目标，结合RLVR（带验证的强化学习）框架，使得模型在决策时不依赖GT轨迹，而在决策后利用其进行验证。

Result: 实验结果表明，DEFT-RLVR在提升AD推理能力的同时，保持甚至增强了通用的视觉能力。AD-MCQ支持仅VLM推理，通过候选构建可控制难度，为可验证的AD推理提供了灵活、可扩展的基础。

Conclusion: 移除GT轨迹暴露能消除轨迹锚定偏差这一捷径，而AD-MCQ将规划问题形式化为选择问题，配合DEFT-RLVR将未来轨迹转化为事后验证目标，实现了无需开放轨迹生成的可验证自动驾驶推理，为未来AD推理研究提供了可扩展的基础框架。

Abstract: Recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models for autonomous driving (AD) increasingly utilize chain-of-thought (CoT) supervision to enhance the reasoning capabilities of their Vision-Language Model (VLM) components, yet existing annotation pipelines commonly expose the teacher model to the logged ground-truth (GT) future trajectory. We empirically show that this induces trajectory anchoring bias: teacher models rationalize the revealed outcome rather than infer a decision from scene evidence, producing less causally faithful CoTs and substantially more severe hallucinations, especially in causally challenging scenes. Removing the GT trajectory eliminates this shortcut, but open-ended trajectory generation entangles high-level decision-making with precise geometric synthesis and low-level dynamics. To make trajectory-level driving decisions verifiable without requiring open-ended trajectory synthesis, we introduce Autonomous-Driving Multiple-Choice Question (AD-MCQ), which casts planning as selection among explicit trajectory candidates. Taking this a step further, we propose Deferred Exposure of Future Trajectories for RLVR (DEFT-RLVR) to transform future trajectories from pre-decision anchors into post-decision verification targets. Experimental results show that DEFT-RLVR improves AD reasoning while preserving or even enhancing general visual capabilities. With VLM-only inference and controllable difficulty through candidate construction, AD-MCQ provides a flexible, scalable, and extensible foundation for future research on verifiable AD reasoning.

</details>


### [322] [Leveraging AI for fine-grained food safety risk forecasting in sparse data conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01767)
*Dongqi Wang,Weiwei Chen,Han Zhou,Weihua Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Transformer的框架，通过统一超过1100万条检验记录与人口、经济、环境等辅助指标，实现城市级别的食品安全风险细粒度预测。三阶段预训练设计结合Wilson区间部分监督与半监督标签精炼，在样本不足时仍能有效利用历史数据。2022年数据实验显示该方法显著优于基线，与浙江省市场监督管理局的实地实验也验证了检测率和资源分配的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 食品安全是重要的公共卫生挑战，但监管资源有限且区域采样数据稀疏，传统的反应式检测手段难以在早期、细粒度层面识别食品安全风险，亟需一种能够整合大规模公共检验数据并实现主动、前瞻性风险预测的方法。

Method: 提出Transformer框架，将1100万+检验记录与统计年鉴中的社会经济和环境指标统一整合。采用三阶段预训练设计：(1) 利用Wilson区间捕获安全性与风险排名的部分监督信号；(2) 半监督标签精炼；(3) 在局部样本量不足时仍有效利用历史记录。

Result: 2022年数据上的实验显示所提方法显著优于基线；与浙江省市场监督管理局合作的实地实验证明了相比人工制定的检查计划，该方法提升了检测率并实现了更高效的检查资源配置。同时观察到监管决策中存在的基于阈值的启发式行为，提示额外训练或决策支持界面可进一步增强AI风险评分的实际影响力。

Conclusion: 大规模公共检验数据、Wilson区间置信度建模与先进深度学习的严谨整合，能够促进食品安全的早期与细粒度威胁识别，减少对被动反应式措施的依赖，为全球食品供应链的主动式、数据驱动监管提供可能。

Abstract: Ensuring food safety represents a critical public health challenge, particularly when inspection resources are limited and regional sampling data are sparse. This study proposes a Transformer-based framework capable of forecasting fine-grained, city-level food safety risks by unifying over 11 million inspection records with supplemental demographic, economic, and environmental indicators extracted from the Statistical Yearbook. A three-stage pretraining design leverages partial supervision from the Wilson interval (capturing both safety and risk rankings), together with semi-supervised label refinement, to effectively utilize historical records even when local sample sizes are insufficient. Experimental evaluations on data from 2022 show that the proposed approach outperforms baselines significantly. A subsequent field experiment in collaboration with the Zhejiang Provincial Administration for Market Regulation further demonstrates improved detection rates and more efficient allocation of inspection resources compared to a manually developed plan. Observations of regulatory decision-making reveal a threshold-based heuristic employed by inspectors, hinting that additional training or decision-support interfaces could further enhance the impact of AI-generated risk scores. Overall, these findings underscore that a rigorous integration of large-scale public inspection data, Wilson interval-based confidence modeling, and advanced deep learning can facilitate earlier and more granular identification of food safety threats. By reducing reliance on reactive measures alone, the proposed framework has the potential to advance proactive, data-driven oversight of the global food supply.

</details>


### [323] [FRAMES: Guarded and Dual-Objective Skill Evolution for Agents in Policy-Governed Enterprise Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01772)
*Xuhui Wang,Ruoqi Shu,Chen Dan,Tianhua Xu,Mengxi Luo,Yanming Mai,Bo Wan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FRAMES是一个闭环框架，用于改进运行策略约束企业工作流（如文档审计）的LLM智能体。它从现有资产冷启动可部署技能，并通过基于共识的突变、准确率-成本帕累托选择以及防回退保证来进化技能，同时保持可审计性。在内部生产系统中达到最佳准确率-成本权衡，并在tau-bench上复现了相同优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体越来越多地运行受策略约束的企业工作流，需要对每条规则一致地应用、对每个值进行溯源并保持可审计。但改进这些智能体十分困难：运营反馈稀疏且无标注，修改一条规则可能使无关案例回退，且必须在不增加推理成本或失去可审计性的前提下提升准确率。

Method: FRAMES框架包含四个核心组件：(1) 从现有资产冷启动可部署技能；(2) 基于共识的突变机制进化技能；(3) 对准确率和成本进行帕累托选择；(4) 反回退保证。整个闭环在保持可审计性的条件下运行。

Result: 在内部生产系统上部署后，FRAMES在基线中取得了最佳的准确率-成本权衡，并且相同的收益在tau-bench基准上得到了复现。

Conclusion: FRAMES证明了即使在运营反馈稀疏、无标注且存在规则间回归风险的真实企业环境中，也能通过闭环进化框架在保持可审计性的同时持续改进LLM智能体的准确率-成本表现。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly run policy-bound enterprise workflows such as document auditing, where they must apply rules consistently, ground every value, and stay auditable. Improving these agents is hard: operational feedback is sparse and unlabeled, edits to one rule can regress unrelated cases, and accuracy must improve without inflating inference cost or losing auditability. We present FRAMES, a closed-loop framework that cold-starts deployable skills from existing assets and then evolves them through consensus-based mutation, Pareto selection over accuracy and cost, and an anti-regression guarantee, all while preserving auditability. Deployed on our internal production system, FRAMES attains the best accuracy-cost trade-off among baselines, with the same gains reproduced on tau-bench.

</details>


### [324] [REFLEX: Rethinking MoE Inference as Refinement-Aware Compute Allocation in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01784)
*Xiang Xia,Cheng Yan,Yiming Zhang,Jiazheng Liu,Hongyu Zhang,Wuyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: REFLEX是一种无训练的、面向扩散语言模型（DLM）的精炼感知（refinement-aware）MoE专家分配方法。它保持默认路由器不变，通过粗到细的层次结构按块分配专家预算，并使用Frontier-Progress Score解决活动块优先级问题，在LLaDA-MoE和LLaDA2.0-mini上平均减少15%的专家计算量，同时保持或提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE扩散语言模型中，默认的固定token-choice路由对所有token位置分配统一的专家预算。然而在去噪过程中，每个token位置的精炼需求差异显著，统一的计算分配造成了专家计算与精炼需求之间的错配。作者认为DLM中的MoE推理应被视为跨异构token精炼状态的计算资源分配问题。

Method: REFLEX是一种无训练方法，核心包含两个组件：1）粗到细的专家预算分配层次结构，将计算与块级别的相对精炼角色对齐；2）Frontier-Progress Score用于解决活动块的优先级排序。该方法保持默认路由器不变，仅重新组织专家计算以适应当前去噪过程中的精炼状态。

Result: 在两个代表性的MoE-based DLM（LLaDA-MoE和LLaDA2.0-mini）的多个基准测试上，REFLEX平均减少15%的专家计算分配，同时在大多数基准上保持甚至提升生成质量。与自回归风格的可变专家路由方法相比，REFLEX在质量与计算的权衡上表现更一致。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持了核心论点：在DLM的每次去噪前向传播中，应根据暴露出的异构精炼需求来分配专家计算，而非使用统一的固定预算。REFLEX验证了精炼感知的专家分配的重要性，并展示了优于自回归风格可变专家路由方法的一致质量-计算权衡。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) models increase parameter capacity by activating only a small subset of experts for each token. This conditional-computation paradigm has enabled autoregressive language models to scale model capacity without a proportional increase in per-token computation. In diffusion language models (DLMs), however, each denoising forward jointly revisits all token positions despite their sharply different refinement demands, while the default fixed token-choice routing assigns them a uniform expert budget, creating a mismatch between expert computation and refinement demand. We argue that MoE inference in DLMs should therefore be viewed as refinement-aware compute allocation across heterogeneous token refinement states. We propose REFLEX (\textbf{RE}finement-aware \textbf{FLEX}ible expert allocation), a training-free method that keeps the default router unchanged while reorganizing expert computation around the evolving refinement process. Specifically, REFLEX introduces a coarse-to-fine hierarchy for expert-budget allocation that aligns computation with block-relative refinement roles while using the Frontier-Progress Score to resolve active-block priorities. Across multiple widely used benchmarks on two representative MoE-based DLMs, LLaDA-MoE and LLaDA2.0-mini, REFLEX reduces allocated expert computation by 15\% on average while preserving or even improving generation quality on most benchmarks relative to default routing. Compared with autoregressive-style variable-expert routing methods, REFLEX also yields a more consistent quality--computation trade-off, further supporting the importance of allocating expert computation according to the heterogeneous refinement demands exposed within each denoising forward.

</details>


### [325] [Can You Trust the Confidence? ConfBench for Vision-Language Models on Document Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01792)
*Priyashree Roy,Sujitha Martin,Mohammad Rostami,Spencer Romo,Renhao Xue,Bob Strahan,Diego A. Socolinsky,Boyi Xie,Md Mofijul Islam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ConfBench，首个面向关键信息提取（KIE）任务的可信度校准专用基准，包含20种受控退化管线生成的1,346个变体和70K+实体级评估。系统评估了4个专有和3个开源VLM在多种置信度估计方法下的校准性能，并引入ECARB预算指标用于运营节省评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文档基准被高质量、洁净的样本主导，低准确率区域过于稀疏，无法对VLM在智能文档处理中的置信度分数进行充分的校准评估，而可靠的置信度分数对于在自动化与人工审核之间路由抽取结果至关重要。

Method: 构建ConfBench数据集：对多样文档集施加20种受控退化管线，生成1,346个变体和70K+实体级评估。在三个输入模态下，对七种VLM（4个专有+3个开源）分别使用口头化（verbalized）和对数概率（log-probability）两种置信度估计方法进行评估，比较不同log-prob聚合策略（首token、均值、边际）的性能。

Result: 研究发现：（i）OCR+Image模态产生更准确的置信度估计；（ii）模型能力是主导因素，Claude家族内置信度质量随能力单调提升，而跨家族参数数量并非好的预测指标；（iii）校准质量差异显著，从近乎完美到严重过度自信，逐模型事后校正可调整绝对置信度值用于阈值路由而不改变排序类运营指标；（iv）对数概率配合首token聚合一致优于均值token和边际聚合。

Conclusion: ConfBench提供了系统研究置信度估计器和校准方法的平台，ECARB将区分度增益转化为运营节省，支撑可信赖的智能文档处理应用部署。

Abstract: Intelligent document processing (IDP) with vision-language models (VLMs) hinges on confidence scores trustworthy enough to route extractions between automation and human review. Existing document benchmarks are dominated by clean, high-quality samples, leaving low accuracy regions too sparse for calibration assessment. We introduce ConfBench, the first calibration-specific benchmark for key information extraction (KIE), built by applying 20 controlled degradation pipelines to a diverse document set, yielding 1,346 variants and 70K+ entity-level evaluations spanning the full accuracy spectrum. We evaluate four proprietary and three open-weight VLMs under verbalized and log-probability confidence estimation methods across three input modalities, and find: (i) OCR+Image modality results in more accurate confidence estimates; (ii) model capability is the dominant factor: within the Claude family confidence quality scales monotonically with capability, while across families parameter count is a poor predictor; (iii) calibration quality varies widely across models, from near-perfect to severely overconfident, and per-model post-hoc correction rescales these absolute confidence values for threshold-based routing without altering ranking-based operational metrics; and (iv) log-probability with first-token aggregation consistently outperforms mean-token and margin aggregations. We also introduce ECARB, a review-budget metric translating discriminative gains into operational savings. We release ConfBench publicly to enable systematic study of confidence estimators and calibration methods for trustworthy IDP application deployment.

</details>


### [326] [CoNav-UAV: Cooperative Dual-Altitude Aerial Navigation via Stackelberg Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01802)
*Junru Song,Wenhao Zhang,Yang Yang,Xuekai Qiu,Feifei Wang,Weien Zhou,Tingsong Jiang,Ying Wen,Yang Li,Wen Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CoNav-UAV，一种面向空中平台的目标导向视觉语言导航（VLN）框架。它将该任务显式建模为高空领导者与低空跟随者之间的Stackelberg博弈，仅依赖机载视觉和语言输入，并通过迭代Stackelberg学习（领导者使用基于记忆的上下文学习进行视觉语言推理，跟随者使用DAgger式专家蒸馏进行运动控制）驱动双方收敛至Stackelberg均衡。在AerialVLN基准的三个高保真城市场景中，CoNav-UAV持续优于单智能体和双智能体基线，成功率达最高提升30.8个点，跨场景迁移提升9.0个点，且仅需约三分之一的自适应数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中平台上的目标导向VLN任务（如灾害救援、基础设施巡检、安全巡逻）要求无人机仅根据目标的简要外观和环境描述进行定位，这需要同时进行全局探索与锚定以及无碰撞的近距接近——这两个相互交织的过程难以由单一智能体调和。现有方法多将地面VLN范式迁移至低空无人机并用外部辅助补偿低效探索；虽有工作尝试部署双机协同，但仍依赖特权信息且独立训练两个智能体，无法实现协同所需的相互适应。

Method: CoNav-UAV将任务显式建模为高空领导者与低空跟随者之间的Stackelberg博弈，系统仅依赖机载视觉与语言输入。求解该博弈采用迭代Stackelberg学习：(1) 领导者的高层视觉语言推理通过基于记忆的上下文学习进行精炼；(2) 跟随者的精确运动控制通过DAgger式专家蒸馏进行更新。两者的交替迭代将两个智能体共同推向Stackelberg均衡。

Result: 在AerialVLN基准的三个高保真城市场景中，CoNav-UAV一致优于单智能体和双智能体基线。在已学习场景上成功率提升高达30.8个百分点，跨场景迁移条件下提升9.0个百分点，同时仅使用约为其三分之一的适应数据。进一步分析验证了领导者和跟随者更新的互补增益，并揭示了不同VLM骨干下的稳健增益及不同的学习动态。

Conclusion: CoNav-UAV通过Stackelberg博弈框架有效解决了空中VLN中全局探索与近距接近这对互斥需求的调和问题，其迭代Stackelberg学习在同频双机协同中实现了真正的相互适应，显著提升导航成功率与数据效率，为空中多智能体VLN提供了新范式。

Abstract: Target-oriented vision-and-language navigation (VLN) on aerial platforms is attracting growing attention for missions such as disaster rescue, infrastructure inspection, and security patrol. In this task, an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) needs to locate targets given only a concise description of their appearance and surroundings. This requires global exploration and grounding as well as collision-free close-range approach, two interleaved processes difficult to reconcile within a single agent. Most existing methods transfer the ground VLN paradigm to a low-altitude UAV and compensate for its inefficient exploration with external assistance. A recent attempt deploys two UAVs at complementary altitudes yet still relies on privileged information and trains its two agents independently, precluding any mutual adaptation essential for cooperation. Here we propose CoNav-UAV, which explicitly models the task as a Stackelberg game between a high-altitude leader and a low-altitude follower, with the system operating on onboard visual and linguistic inputs alone. To solve this game, we introduce Iterative Stackelberg Learning. The leader's high-level vision-language reasoning is refined via memory-based in-context learning, while the follower's precise motion control is updated via DAgger-style expert distillation. The alternation drives both agents toward a Stackelberg equilibrium. CoNav-UAV consistently outperforms single- and dual-agent baselines across three high-fidelity urban scenes from the AerialVLN benchmark. Success rate improves by up to 30.8 points on the learning scene, and 9.0 points under cross-scene transfer while using about 3x less adaptation data. Further analyses validate the complementary gains of the leader and follower updates and reveal robust gains yet distinct learning dynamics across VLM backbones.

</details>


### [327] [CockpitHAT: Dependency-Graph-Driven Hierarchical Attribution for Embodied Multi-Agent Cockpits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01805)
*Wei Wang,Shuanghe Liu,Zhu Zhuo,Jiaqi Zhong,Xiaozhao Zhao,Xiaojie Zuo,Jie Su*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CockpitHAT，一个面向LLM多智能体系统故障归因的分层框架，通过依赖距离阈值替代位置窗口，整合多通道证据，并结合安全提升的置信度加权共识来解决正确性坍缩问题；同时发布包含212条带ISO 26262 ASIL严重度标注的故障轨迹基准CockpitBench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统存在正确性坍缩问题，即高任务级准确率掩盖了严重的过程级故障。在汽车座舱等安全关键场景中，词法正确的输出可能触发危险物理操作。现有归因方法仅依赖文本轨迹，忽略了依赖结构、多通道证据和安全感知评估，无法满足安全关键场景的可靠性需求。

Method: CockpitHAT采用三分层机制：(1) 以交互DAG中的依赖距离阈值替代位置窗口捕获依赖结构；(2) 通过具身适配器整合多通道证据（对话、车辆状态、环境、记忆）；(3) 在置信度加权的分析师共识中对高风险故障应用安全提升偏移。同时构建CockpitBench基准，包含212条跨四个通道的故障轨迹，每条通过三专家共识标注ISO 26262 ASIL严重度。

Result: 在Who&When基准上，CockpitHAT于Hand-Crafted和Algorithm-Generated拆分上分别达到77.9%/37.8%和86.5%/46.0%的agent级/步骤精确准确率，超越文本-only SOTA ECHO最多17.6/16.7个百分点；在CockpitBench上达到78.3% agent级和38.2%步骤精确准确率。

Conclusion: 依赖感知、多通道、风险校准的归因方法为真实世界具身LLM多智能体系统提供了可靠的故障诊断范式，有效解决了正确性坍缩问题，并验证了安全关键场景下的实用性。

Abstract: LLM multi-agent systems suffer from Correctness Collapse, where high task-level accuracy conceals severe process-level failures. This is especially hazardous in safety-critical embodied settings such as automotive cockpits, where lexically correct utterances may trigger dangerous physical operations. Existing attribution methods rely on text traces alone, missing dependency structure, multi-channel evidence, and safety-aware evaluation. We introduce CockpitHAT, a hierarchical attribution framework that replaces positional windows with dependency-distance thresholds from interaction DAGs, integrates multi-channel evidence via an embodied adapter, and applies a safety-uplift to high-risk failures during confidence-weighted analyst consensus. We further release CockpitBench, a benchmark of 212 annotated failure traces spanning dialogue, vehicle-state, environmental, and memory channels, each labeled with ISO 26262 ASIL severity via three-expert consensus. On the public Who&When benchmark, CockpitHAT achieves agent-level / step-exact accuracies of 77.9% / 37.8% on the Hand-Crafted split and 86.5% / 46.0% on the Algorithm-Generated split, surpassing the text-only SOTA ECHO by up to 17.6 / 16.7 points. On CockpitBench, it attains 78.3% agent-level and 38.2% step-exact accuracy. These results establish dependency-aware, multi-channel, risk-calibrated attribution as an effective paradigm for reliable failure diagnosis in real-world embodied LLM multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [328] [SearchMaster: Grounded and Regulated Self-Play for Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01822)
*Wentao Tan,Qiong Cao,Jiaqi Wang,Nan Duan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SearchMaster提出了一种自博弈框架，让单个LLM在本地搜索环境中自我生成、求解和验证搜索任务来训练搜索智能体，并通过三种控制机制（证据链生成器、搜索深度奖励、过度打开惩罚）解决自生成数据中的误导信号问题。在六个深度搜索基准上，将Qwen3.5-9B的平均准确率从38.19%提升到51.52%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练基于LLM的搜索智能体需要高质量搜索数据，但现有方法依赖人工编写任务、专家示范或更强的教师模型，成本高昂且难以扩展。作者希望探索能否通过自博弈方式，让单个LLM从头生成并利用自身搜索数据来训练，从而摆脱对外部数据源的依赖。

Method: SearchMaster采用自博弈框架，包含三个关键控制机制：(1) 证据链生成器（ECG），基于显式的跨文档证据链生成任务，避免生成伪多跳问题；(2) 搜索深度奖励（SDR），根据成功rollout的搜索深度而非仅成功率来评分任务难度，确保保留搜索密集的任务；(3) 过度打开惩罚（OOP），通过惩罚过度打开文档来规范工具使用，避免浅层但冗长的浏览行为。验证过的Proposer和Solver rollout通过GRPO算法联合优化。

Result: 在六个深度搜索基准上，SearchMaster将Qwen3.5-9B骨干模型的平均准确率从38.19%提升到51.52%，在BrowseComp-Plus上获得30.1个百分点的提升。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，有依据且有调节的自博弈方法可以为搜索智能体提供有效的训练数据，无需人工标注的QA对或专家示范，为搜索智能体的训练数据生产提供了可扩展的新路径。

Abstract: Training LLM-based search agents requires high-quality search data: tasks that demand genuine multi-hop retrieval and trajectories that use search tools effectively. Existing pipelines often depend on human-written tasks, expert demonstrations, or stronger teacher models. We present SearchMaster, a self-play framework that trains a single LLM from search tasks it generates, solves, and verifies in a local search environment. The key challenge is that self-generated tasks and rollouts can yield misleading signals: pseudo multi-hop questions, success-rate difficulty estimates that ignore search depth, and rollouts with excessive opening but little targeted evidence acquisition. SearchMaster addresses these failure modes with three controls. An Evidence-Chain Generator (ECG) grounds task generation in explicit cross-document evidence chains to reduce pseudo multi-hop questions. A Search-Depth Reward (SDR) scores task difficulty by the search depth of successful rollouts rather than success rate alone, keeping retained tasks search-intensive. An Over-Opening Penalty (OOP) regulates tool use by discouraging excessive document opening, avoiding long but shallow browsing. Verified Proposer and Solver rollouts are then jointly optimized with GRPO. Across six deep-search benchmarks, SearchMaster improves a Qwen3.5-9B backbone from 38.19% to 51.52% average accuracy, with a 30.1-point gain on BrowseComp-Plus. These results show that grounded and regulated self-play can provide effective search-agent training data without human-labeled QA pairs or expert demonstrations. The code is available at https://github.com/WentaoTan/SearchMaster.

</details>


### [329] [Rewriting or Reweighting? A Geometric Account in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01835)
*Juntong Wang,Shengkun Yang,Xiyuan Wang,Muhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出行为流形分析（behavioral manifold analysis）方法，通过稀疏行为相关坐标和低维局部图表来隔离行为特定几何结构，区分两种机制上不同的失败模式（作为解码吸引子的重复病理和偏好相关的谄媚对齐失败），并揭示后训练中SFT与奖励优化在行为几何上的不对称影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练可以大幅改变语言模型行为，但传统的聚合行为率无法揭示训练是移除了已有机制、创建了新机制，还是只改变了继承机制的用法。为了理解这些机制层面的差异，需要一种能从模型内部表征和计算流中隔离并分析行为特定几何结构的方法。

Method: 引入行为流形分析：通过选择稀疏的行为关联坐标并将它们提升到低维局部图表中来隔离行为特定几何。图表构建在两个互补空间中：ACT捕获运行时激活状态，NOC量化模型将功能信息流路由通过共享行为关联子空间的程度。在多个模型家族上构建图表，并通过受控后训练跟踪这些图表的演化。

Result: 生成的图表高度压缩且跨架构部分可对齐。贡献空间图表展现出更强的架构鲁棒共享核心，而激活空间图表保留更强的家族特定结构。跟踪受控后训练中的图表演化揭示了持续的不对称性：SFT大幅改变继承的行为几何，而奖励优化在改变行为的同时基本保留底层图表。

Conclusion: 这种几何视角为理解两种训练目标的机制区别提供了统一框架：SFT倾向于重写行为几何，而奖励优化主要重新加权（reweight）行为几何。

Abstract: Post-training can substantially alter language-model behavior, yet aggregate behavior rates do not reveal whether training removes an existing mechanism, creates a new one, or changes how an inherited mechanism is used. We study this question through two mechanistically distinct failures, repetition as a decoding-attractor pathology and sycophancy as a preference-related alignment failure. We introduce behavioral manifold analysis, which isolates behavior-specific geometry by selecting sparse behavior-associated coordinates and lifting them into low-dimensional local charts. We construct these charts in two complementary spaces. ACT captures runtime activation states, while NOC quantifies how strongly the model routes functional information flow through the shared behavior-associated subspace. Across multiple model families, the resulting charts are highly compressed and partially alignable across architectures. Contribution-space charts expose a more architecture-robust shared core, whereas activation-space charts retain stronger family-specific structure. Tracking these charts through controlled post-training reveals a consistent asymmetry. Supervised fine-tuning substantially alters the inherited behavioral geometry, whereas reward optimization changes behavior while largely preserving the underlying chart. This geometric perspective provides a unified framework for understanding the mechanistic distinction between the two objectives. SFT tends to rewrite behavioral geometry, whereas reward optimization primarily reweights it. Code is available at https://github.com/ronglingze/Manifold-Analysis

</details>


### [330] [PCSD: Persistent Consistency for Self-Distillation in Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01837)
*Chunji Lv,Yangguang Wei,Junlin Liu,Yang Gao,Ming Liu,Xinming Wang,Jinyang Wu,Guoren Wang,Changsheng Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PCSD方法，通过局部持续性蒸馏权重和自适应窗口及指数衰减聚合，为LLM智能体提供密集的token级强化学习监督，在ALFWorld和WebShop任务上显著超越GRPO等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体在复杂交互任务中面临稀疏奖励问题（长轨迹仅获得单一结果级信号）；现有OPSD方法提供的密集token级监督中，教师信号并非在每个位置都可靠；已有方法要么使用孤立token级差异（对噪声敏感），要么共享步级权重（忽略位置差异），缺乏对教师信号局部持续性的建模。

Method: PCSD从教师偏好信号的局部持续性中推导token级蒸馏权重：1) 使用自适应窗口结合指数衰减聚合来捕获持续的相对教师支持；2) 应用趋势感知调制来衰减局部减弱的支持信号；3) 通过sigmoid门控产生连续权重；4) 将蒸馏目标与GRPO联合优化，结合密集教师指导和稀疏环境反馈。

Result: 在ALFWorld数据集上，PCSD在两个骨干网络上均取得最佳Overall结果：超过GRPO 15.6和13.3分，超过SDAR 6.2和5.5分；在WebShop上保持竞争力，并在未见ALFWorld划分上比GRPO提升15.8分。无需推理时技能即可达到上述性能。

Conclusion: PCSD通过捕捉教师信号的局部持续性来生成更可靠的token级蒸馏权重，能有效缓解稀疏奖励和不可靠教师信号问题，是LLM智能体RL训练的一种有效改进方法。

Abstract: Large language model agents have shown strong potential in complex interactive tasks, yet their reinforcement learning (RL) is often hindered by sparse rewards, as a long multi-turn trajectory may receive only a single outcome-level signal. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) provides dense token-level supervision from a privileged teacher, but the teacher may not be reliable at every position. Existing methods commonly rely on isolated token-level discrepancies, which can be sensitive to noise, or assign a shared step-level weight that may overlook positional variation. We propose Persistent Consistency Self-Distillation (PCSD), which derives token-level distillation weights from the local persistence of teacher-favoring signals. PCSD combines adaptive windows with exponentially decayed aggregation to capture persistent relative teacher support, applies trend-aware modulation to attenuate locally declining support, and produces continuous weights through sigmoid gating. The resulting objective is jointly optimized with GRPO, combining dense teacher guidance with sparse environmental feedback. Without inference-time skills, PCSD achieves the best ALFWorld Overall results among all baselines on both backbones, exceeding GRPO by 15.6 and 13.3 points and SDAR by 6.2 and 5.5 points, while remaining competitive on WebShop and gaining 15.8 points over GRPO on unseen ALFWorld split.

</details>


### [331] [FOCUS: FP4 Optimization via Coupled-Relaxation and Dual-Granularity Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01847)
*Xianglong Yan,Hong Liu,Chengzhu Bao,Tianao Zhang,Guanghua Yu,Jianchen Zhu,Yulun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FOCUS是一个面向FP4量化的后训练量化框架，通过解耦量化/反量化尺度（Coupled-Relaxation Scaling）和双粒度缩放（Dual-Granularity Scaling）来优化LLM的FP4精度，在不引入推理开销的情况下达到SOTA效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM部署成本高昂，FP4量化（如MXFP4、NVFP4）有硬件原生支持但精度难以保持。现有方法的瓶颈在于量化与反量化尺度被紧密耦合，被迫都符合硬件要求的离散低精度格式（如E8M0），而量化尺度实际上不需要存储、无需受此约束，存在未被利用的优化空间。

Method: FOCUS采用两个核心组件：1) Coupled-Relaxation Scaling (CRS)——通过可学习的全精度系数放松量化与反量化尺度之间的紧耦合约束，同时保持硬件合规；2) Dual-Granularity Scaling (DGS)——在更细的子块粒度上细化量化尺度，更精确地适配局部权重分布。通过端到端尺度学习实现整体优化。

Result: 在多个LLM家族和基准上的实验表明，FOCUS在MXFP4和NVFP4两种格式下均达到FP4量化的SOTA精度，且不引入额外推理开销。

Conclusion: 通过解耦量化和反量化尺度并引入双粒度细化，FOCUS有效释放了FP4量化的优化空间，在保持硬件兼容和零额外推理开销的前提下显著提升了精度。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve remarkable performance but are expensive to deploy due to their enormous size. FP4 quantization, with formats such as MXFP4 and NVFP4, offers an appealing solution with native hardware support on modern accelerators. However, maintaining accuracy under FP4 precision remains difficult. A key bottleneck lies in scale optimization: existing methods tightly couple the quantization and dequantization scales, forcing both to conform to the discrete low-precision format required by hardware, such as E8M0 in MXFP4. Yet the quantization scale is never stored and need not obey this constraint, suggesting a significant untapped optimization space. In this work, we propose FOCUS, a post-training quantization framework with end-to-end scale learning for FP4 Optimization via Coupled-Relaxation and Dual-Granularity Scaling. Coupled-Relaxation Scaling (CRS) relaxes the tight coupling between quantization and dequantization scales with a learnable full-precision coefficient, enabling more effective optimization without breaking hardware compliance. Dual-Granularity Scaling (DGS) further refines the quantization scale at a finer sub-block granularity, allowing more precise adaptation to local weight distributions. Experiments across multiple LLM families and benchmarks show that FOCUS achieves state-of-the-art FP4 accuracy under both MXFP4 and NVFP4 formats, while introducing no additional inference overhead. Code and quantized models will be released at https://github.com/tencent/AngelSlim.

</details>


### [332] [Exploring and Bridging Knowledge Holes in Unlearned Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01849)
*Junxiang You,Junkai Chen,Yuhao He,Ruiqi Liu,Zhetao Guo,Shu Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现MLLM机器遗忘评估存在关键盲区——现有基准未捕捉遗忘集之外良性相邻输入上的严重性能退化（知识空洞），并构建了专门基准验证该系统性缺陷；随后提出选择性保护与锚定正则化（SPAR）方法，通过锚定激活过滤和实体抽象增强保护通用模式，在SafeEraser上恢复98%以上的原始响应质量且攻击成功率为0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前MLLM机器遗忘评估范式存在盲区：评估模型实用性的基准与遗忘集表示距离过远，无法捕捉知识空洞（对良性相邻输入的性能严重退化），导致遗忘的精确性无法被准确验证。

Method: 首先构建专门基准探测遗忘模型的知识空洞，通过受控实验证实这是常用方法的系统性后果；然后提出SPAR（选择性保护与锚定正则化），包含两个核心组件：锚定激活过滤（保护通用模式）和实体抽象增强（强化受保护模式）。

Result: 在SafeEraser数据集上，SPAR恢复了98%以上的原始响应质量（标准基线低于50%），同时实现0.00%的攻击成功率和具有竞争力的模型实用性。

Conclusion: 该工作强调了更细粒度的评估标准对于可信赖的MLLM机器遗忘至关重要，SPAR为平衡遗忘精度与模型通用能力提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Machine unlearning offers a promising approach to remove unsafe content from Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), yet ensuring the precision of unlearning remains a persistent challenge. One reason is that current MLLM unlearning evaluation paradigms suffer from a critical blind spot: they assess model utility through benchmarks whose representations are distant from the forget set, failing to capture knowledge holes---severe degradation on benign adjacent inputs. To probe knowledge holes in unlearned MLLMs, we construct a benchmark that captures unintended degradation on benign inputs sharing generic patterns with the forget set, and confirm through controlled experiments that they are a systematic consequence of commonly used approaches. Furthermore, to bridge this gap, we propose Selective Protection with Anchored Regularization, which protects generic patterns via anchored activation filtering while reinforcing them through entity-abstracted enhancement. Our experiments on SafeEraser demonstrate that SPAR recovers over 98% of vanilla response quality compared to below 50% for standard baselines---while achieving 0.00% attack success rate and competitive model utility. These results underscore the necessity of more fine-grained evaluation for trustworthy MLLM unlearning.

</details>


### [333] [Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Complex Eigenfrequency Identification and Mode Structure Reconstruction of the Ground-State ITG Branch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01850)
*Dengdi Sun,Bingbing Zhang,Xiao Wang,Zikang Yan,Yuqiang Tao,Qingquan Yang,Guosheng Xu,Jin Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合傅里叶特征编码、复值特征传播和三阶段训练的物理信息神经网络框架，用于从稀疏观测中联合求解托卡马克边缘离子温度梯度漂移波的复本征频率和二维复值模场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高约束模式托卡马克台基区具有陡峭梯度，该区域的离子温度梯度漂移波分析需要联合识别复本征频率和重构二维复值模场。局部高频振荡、强弱实虚部耦合以及模场与本征频率间的非线性耦合给传统PINN的表示和联合优化带来挑战。

Method: 提出一种物理信息神经网络框架，包含三个关键组件：傅里叶特征编码（捕获高频局部振荡）、复值特征传播（处理实虚部强耦合）以及三阶段训练策略（解决模场与本征频率间的非线性联合优化问题）。在稀疏观测和物理约束下联合求解复本征频率和模场。

Result: 实验表明该框架能够准确恢复目标复本征频率和二维复值模场，且性能优于代表性PINN基线方法。同时为分析高阶和多分支漂移波模式提供了基础。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了台基区ITG漂移波的高频、强耦合、非线性联合优化难题，为等离子体物理中的复本征问题求解提供了可行且优于基线的方案，并可推广至更复杂的漂移波模式分析。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) combine sparse observations with physical equations, providing an important approach for modeling complex plasma processes and inferring unknown physical quantities. The steep-gradient pedestal of high-confinement-mode tokamaks is closely linked to plasma confinement and edge transport. Analyzing ion-temperature-gradient (ITG) drift waves in this region requires jointly identifying complex eigenfrequencies and reconstructing two-dimensional complex-valued mode fields. Localized high-frequency oscillations, strong real-imaginary coupling, and nonlinear coupling between the mode field and eigenfrequency challenge PINN representation and joint optimization. To address these challenges, we propose a physics-informed neural framework combining Fourier feature encoding, complex-valued feature propagation, and three-stage training. Under sparse observations and physical constraints, it jointly solves for the complex eigenfrequency and mode field of a representative ground-state ITG branch. Experiments show that the framework accurately recovers the target complex eigenfrequency and two-dimensional complex-valued mode field and outperforms representative PINN baselines. It also provides a basis for analyzing higher-order and multiple-branch drift-wave modes.

</details>


### [334] [EchoChange: A Diffusion Language Model with Dual Pass Remasking for Factual Remote Sensing Disaster Change Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01856)
*Dongwei Sun,Bowen Yao,Yujie Zhang,Pei Liu,Jing Yao,Xiangyong Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EchoChange，一种基于多模态离散扩散语言模型的遥感灾害变化描述生成方法，将变化描述建模为迭代式掩码token去噪而非自回归从左到右生成，能够在生成过程中反复修正不完善的中期预测，从而避免早期误解导致的级联事实错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的变化描述（change captioning）方法大多采用自回归解码范式，从左到右逐步生成文本。这种范式下，一旦早期对变化对象、事件或空间关系的解读出现偏差，该错误将成为后续生成的不可逆前提，放大视觉歧义并导致级联式事实错误。针对遥感灾害场景中变化通常稀疏且空间局部化的特点，这种错误积累问题尤为突出。

Method: EchoChange将变化描述生成建模为迭代式掩码token去噪过程。其核心组件包括：(1) 多模态离散扩散语言模型，条件于灾前灾后图像对，对整个描述进行反复修正；(2) draft-aware dual-pass training（草案感知双通道训练），让训练过程感知中间草案；(3) 渐进式掩码课程（progressive masking curriculum），由易到难地训练去噪能力；(4) 置信度引导的重新掩码策略（confidence-guided remasking），在推理阶段基于置信度选择性地重新掩码不确定的token。

Result: 在RSCC基准上的大量实验表明，EchoChange在词汇级（lexical）和语义级（semantic）指标上均显著优于通用型及遥感专用基线方法。

Conclusion: EchoChange验证了将变化描述生成从自回归范式转向迭代离散扩散范式的有效性，通过赋予模型在生成过程中回溯和修正的能力，有效缓解了早期解读错误引发的级联事实错误问题，为遥感变化描述生成提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Bi-temporal remote-sensing disaster change captioning often needs to identify sparse and spatially localized changes across large pre- and post-event scenes and then translate them into coherent, factual descriptions. However, existing change captioning methods always follow an autoregressive decoding paradigm to generate the change description and thus an early misinterpretation of the changed object, event, or spatial relation becomes an irreversible premise for subsequent text, amplifying visual ambiguity into cascading factual errors. To address this limitation, we propose EchoChange, a multimodal discrete diffusion language model that formulates change captioning as iterative masked-token denoising rather than left-to-right generation. By repeatedly revising the entire caption while conditioning on the image pair, EchoChange can reconsider uncertain content and correct imperfect intermediate predictions. We further introduce draft-aware dual-pass training, a progressive masking curriculum, and confidence-guided remasking to align training with iterative inference. Extensive experiments on the RSCC benchmark show that EchoChange substantially outperforms both general-purpose and remote-sensing-specific baselines across lexical and semantic metrics. The EchoChange Project is at https://github.com/sundongwei/EchoChange_Project

</details>


### [335] [Wnuan: Staged Post-Training for Question Answering over Proprietary Enterprise Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01862)
*Xiaofeng Shi,Xiaosong Qiu,Wenxin Ma,Qian Kou,Yiming Pan,Longbin Yu,Ying Liu,Haiping Wang,Hua Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Wnuan，一个三阶段企业问答适配流程：从文档构建任务导向的监督信号、结合通用数据回放进行监督微调、对残差错误进行强化学习。在707题WnuanBench上，32B主路线将可接受答案率从52.76%提升至80.06%（SFT后）和91.51%（RL后）；残差错误采样优于全池及随机采样；通用基准平均下降5.17分，主要集中在指令跟随能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业级问答要求模型在不丧失通用能力的前提下获取专有知识。现有方法通常面临灾难性遗忘与专有知识获取之间的权衡，缺少系统的多阶段适配框架来同时优化任务性能与通用能力保持。

Method: 提出Wnuan三阶段流水线：(1)从企业文档自动构建任务导向的监督数据；(2)结合通用数据回放进行监督微调(SFT)；(3)对残差错误应用强化学习(RL)进行针对性优化。评估采用707题WnuanBench，并设计了残差错误采样、全池采样、大小匹配随机采样三种数据策略对比，使用源聚类自助法计算置信区间。

Result: 32B主路由AAR从52.76%提升至SFT后的80.06%和RL后的91.51%。在匹配的100次更新协议下，残差错误采样比全池采样高出3.11个百分点，比大小匹配随机采样高出2.97个百分点。源聚类自助置信区间均高于零，同域验证集保持排序一致性。通用基准平均下降5.17分，主要集中于指令跟随。自动评估集成与权威领域专家在90.5%的分层样本上一致。

Conclusion: 研究表明阶段式企业适配能显著提升专有领域问答能力，同时带来有限的通用能力代价（主要在指令跟随）；残差错误采样是RL阶段更高效的数据选择策略，验证了"先获取知识、再修正错误"的分阶段适配范式的有效性。

Abstract: Enterprise question answering requires models to acquire proprietary knowledge without discarding general capabilities. We present Wnuan, a three-stage pipeline that constructs task-oriented supervision from documents, performs supervised fine-tuning with general-data replay, and applies reinforcement learning to residual errors. On the 707-question WnuanBench, the primary 32B route raises acceptable-answer rate (AAR) from 52.76% before adaptation to 80.06% after SFT and 91.51% after RL. Under a matched 100-update protocol, residual-error sampling outperforms full-pool and size-matched random sampling by 3.11 and 2.97 points, respectively. Source-cluster bootstrap intervals remain above zero for both contrasts, and a same-domain validation set preserves the ordering. The general-benchmark average decreases by 5.17 points across the route, concentrated in instruction following. The automatic evaluation ensemble agrees with an authoritative domain expert on 90.5% of a stratified Wnuan-Inst response sample. These results characterize both the gains and the general-capability cost of staged enterprise adaptation.

</details>


### [336] [ReasonCast: Towards Explainable Time Series Forecasting with Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01875)
*Seunghan Lee,Jun Seo,Jaehoon Lee,Junhyeok Kang,Sangjun Han,Sungdong Yoo,Minjae Kim,Tae Yoon Lim,Dongwan Kang,Hwanil Choi,Soonyoung Lee,Wonbin Ahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出任务融合的时间序列模型ReasonCast，该模型能在单一自回归过程中同时完成数值预测（生成）和因果推理解释（理解），并配套构建了ReasonTS-Bench基准来系统评估这两项联合能力。实验证明ReasonCast在预测精度上超越LLM和专用TS模型，且能生成可验证的因果推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列模型要么只擅长理解任务（返回文本答案），要么只擅长生成任务（返回数值预测）。即使最近出现的统一模型，也只是将两种输出作为任务分离的路径，无法在单一连贯响应中同时预测序列并解释预测产生的原因。作者认为需要一种任务融合的模型，将数值预测与可解释的文本推理整合到单一响应中。

Method: 提出两个核心贡献：1) ReasonTS-Bench基准，识别时间序列的五个基本模式，支持对预测与解释的联合评估；2) ReasonCast微调方案，可将任意LLM微调为同时执行预测和推理任务，在单一自回归pass中生成推理链和预测结果。

Result: 大量实验表明ReasonCast在预测精度上优于纯LLM和专用时间序列模型，同时能够生成可验证的、具有因果性质的推理链。

Conclusion: 任务融合（task-fused）的时间序列建模范式是可行的且有价值的，它打破了TS理解与生成之间的传统界限，使模型能够在单一连贯输出中同时提供预测结果和因果解释。

Abstract: Most time series (TS) models are specialized for a single task, either understanding (i.e., returning text answers about a TS) or generation (i.e., returning a numeric forecast). Only recently have unified models begun to handle the two within a single architecture. Even these models, however, produce the two outputs as task-separated paths and cannot predict a series and explain why that prediction arises within a single coherent response. In this paper, we argue for a task-fused model that jointly produces 1) prediction (generation) and 2) selfexplanation (understanding), thereby integrating 1) numerical TS forecasting and 2) interpretable text reasoning within a single response. To enable the systematic study of this capability, we present both a benchmark and a recipe that jointly address the two tasks. The benchmark, ReasonTS-Bench, identifies five fundamental patterns underlying TS and enables the joint evaluation of both tasks. ReasonCast, our recipe for finetuning any LLM to perform both tasks jointly, yields a model that generates a reasoning chain and a forecast together in a single autoregressive pass. Extensive experiments show that ReasonCast outperforms both LLMs and TS models on prediction accuracy while producing verifiable, causal reasoning. Code is available at: https://github.com/seunghan96/reasoncast.

</details>


### [337] [CoEvoKG: Co-Evolving Knowledge Graphs with Self-Evolving Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01904)
*Zhaoyang Li,Zenghuang Fu,Qiuyuan Ai,Ping Jiang,Haoyu Wu,Minghui Wu,Chenxu Zhao,Jie Song,Guannan He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoEvoKG是一个将知识图谱同时用作可验证训练任务来源和持久证据记忆的框架，通过联合训练任务生成器与搜索智能体，将成功搜索中获得的证据写回知识图谱以形成闭环演化，在六个QA基准和三个骨干模型上显著提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自对弈搜索智能体在每次生成任务时反复丢弃成功搜索中获得的知识，无法积累和复用搜索经验，限制了模型通过强化学习持续改进的能力。

Method: CoEvoKG将知识图谱作为双重资源：一方面作为可验证训练任务的来源（从实体链采样生成多跳问题），另一方面作为持久的证据记忆。框架联合训练任务生成器和搜索智能体：生成器从知识图谱中采样实体链创建多跳问题，智能体根据答案正确性和图证据支持的搜索轨迹获得奖励。搜索成功后，框架验证并去重检索到的证据，将其写回对应的图节点和边，供后续训练轮次复用，形成模型自我演化与知识积累的闭环。

Result: 在NQ、TriviaQA、PopQA、HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA和Bamboogle六个QA基准上，CoEvoKG在Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct、Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct三个骨干模型上的宏平均准确率分别比对应基座模型提升+11.2、+10.1和+11.6个百分点；在匹配训练预算下，比竞争性的自对弈基线和RL搜索智能体基线平均提升+2.6到+3.7个宏平均百分点。

Conclusion: CoEvoKG证明了将知识图谱作为动态演化记忆能够打破搜索智能体自我演化中知识丢弃的瓶颈，实现模型学习与知识积累的正向循环，在多个问答任务上取得一致且显著的改进。

Abstract: Large language models can improve with reinforcement learning for search agents, yet existing self play agents repeatedly generate tasks while discarding the knowledge gained during successful searches. We introduce CoEvoKG, a framework that turns a
  knowledge graph into both a source of verifiable training tasks and a persistent evidence memory for agent evolution. CoEvoKG jointly trains a task generator and a search agent: the generator creates multihop questions from entity chains sampled
  from the knowledge graph, while the agent learns from rewards for answer correctness and search trajectories whose entity paths are supported by graph evidence. When a search succeeds, CoEvoKG verifies and deduplicates the retrieved evidence, then
  writes it back to the corresponding graph nodes and edges. Future rounds reuse this enriched graph for task generation and reward computation, closing the loop between model self evolution and knowledge accumulation. Experiments on six QA benchmarks
  (NQ, TriviaQA, PopQA, HotpotQA, 2WikiMultiHopQA, and Bamboogle) with three backbone models show that CoEvoKG improves macro average accuracy over the corresponding base models by +11.2, +10.1, and +11.6 points on Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct,
  Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, respectively. Under matched training budgets, CoEvoKG further improves over competitive self play baselines and RL baselines for search agents by +2.6 to +3.7 macro average points across the three
  backbones. Code is available at https://github.com/lazzy1225/CoEvoKG.

</details>


### [338] [Diagnosing Search Behavior and Failure Modes in Long-Horizon Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01913)
*Qi Liu,Jiaxin Mao,Fengbin Zhu,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过轨迹级诊断，系统分析长时程深度搜索智能体的行为，将失败分解为检索缺口（未能找到必要证据）和利用缺口（找到证据但未正确使用）。发现搜索努力与答案质量仅弱相关，答案准确性与检索证据质量（尤其是累积检索召回率）的相关性远高于搜索次数或上下文消耗量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度搜索智能体通过迭代搜索查询来回答困难的信息寻求问题，但搜索努力（如搜索次数）是否以及如何转化为更好的答案仍不清楚。作者希望系统性地理解搜索行为与答案质量之间的关系，并定位失败来源，以指导更好的深度研究系统设计。

Method: 使用人工标注的文档级相关性判断，在每个搜索步骤评估检索到的证据，将智能体行为分为两个阶段：检索（retrieve）和利用（utilize），并据此将失败分解为检索缺口（证据从未被找到）和利用缺口（找到但未正确使用）。固定检索模型和评估框架，在BrowseComp-Plus上比较六个智能体，并在BrowseComp上使用开放网络搜索API验证发现。

Result: 研究发现：1) 搜索努力与答案质量仅弱相关；2) 答案准确性与检索证据质量（尤其是累积检索召回率）比搜索次数或上下文消耗量更相关；3) 有用证据常早期出现，但智能体倾向于继续搜索，产生低产出尾段；4) 探索性查询改写仍有用，但最优智能体发出的冗余查询少得多。

Conclusion: 通过系统刻画长时程搜索智能体的行为和失败模式，指出实用方向：更强的查询表述、更有效的证据选择和上下文管理，以及基于是否已检索到足够支持证据的停止标准。

Abstract: Deep search agents answer difficult information-seeking questions by iteratively issuing search queries to gather supporting evidence, but it remains unclear whether and how greater search effort leads to better answers. We study these questions through a trajectory-level diagnosis of long-horizon search agents. Using human-annotated document-level relevance judgments, we evaluate the evidence retrieved at each search step and separate two stages of agent behavior: what evidence an agent retrieves and how effectively it uses that evidence. This distinction further allows us to decompose failures into retrieval gaps, where the necessary evidence is never found, and utilization gaps, where relevant evidence is retrieved but not used correctly. With the retrieval model and evaluation harness held fixed, we compare six agents on BrowseComp-Plus and further validate our findings on BrowseComp with an open-web search API. Across settings, we find that search effort and answer quality are only weakly aligned. Answer accuracy is better correlated with the quality of retrieved evidence, especially cumulative retrieval recall, than with the number of searches or the amount of context consumed. Useful evidence often appears early in the trajectory, yet agents tend to continue searching, producing a long tail of low-yield retrieval steps. At the query level, exploratory reformulations remain useful, but the best-performing agents issue far fewer redundant queries. Overall, by systematically characterizing the search behavior and failure modes of long-horizon search agents, this work points to practical directions for building better deep research systems, including stronger query formulation, more effective evidence selection and context management, and stopping criteria based on whether sufficient supporting evidence has been retrieved.

</details>


### [339] [ProWorld: Progress-Aware Hyperbolic World Models for Long-Horizon Visual Goal Reaching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01926)
*Zihan Liu,Yuzhe Zhuang,Yuanzu Li,Wanshuang Gou,Jiahong Liu,Min Zhou,Menglin Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ProWorld，一种基于双曲几何的进度感知视觉世界模型，通过引入目标条件进度排序来组织潜在空间动力学，解决JEPA风格世界模型在长时程视觉目标规划中局部预测准确但全局漂移的问题。在四个视觉目标到达任务上，ProWorld相比LeWM平均绝对成功率提升9.67。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA式视觉世界模型通过预测未来潜在表征实现视觉目标规划，但现有方法仅学习局部转移一致性。在长时程任务中，多步推演虽局部合理却可能偏离目标相关轨迹；且局部相似未来状态可能对应截然不同的长期进展，难以在仅优化局部一致性的潜在空间中区分。

Method: ProWorld核心包含三部分：(1) 引入目标条件进度排序，该排序呈现非对称从粗到细结构（早期状态保留更广泛未来可能性，后期状态集中于目标相关区域），适配双曲几何；(2) 通过双曲蕴含学习维护轨迹内的方向性进度；(3) 通过双曲未来判别缓解局部相似未来状态间的进度模糊。此外设计进度感知规划目标，联合考量与目标的接近度及中间状态的持续进度来评分候选推演。

Result: 在四个视觉目标到达任务上，ProWorld相对于LeWM取得平均绝对成功率提升9.67。

Conclusion: 利用双曲几何天然契合目标条件进度排序的粗到细层次结构，可以显著提升视觉世界模型在长时程目标规划中的成功率。

Abstract: JEPA-style visual world models offer an effective paradigm for visual goal planning by predicting future latent representations. Existing methods typically learn local transition consistency through next-step representation prediction. However, in long-horizon tasks, accurate local prediction alone need not ensure sustained progress toward the goal. First, multi-step rollouts can remain locally plausible while drifting away from goal-relevant trajectories. Second, locally similar future states can correspond to substantially different long-term progress, making them difficult to distinguish in a latent space optimized mainly for local consistency. To address these challenges, we introduce goal-conditioned progress order, a relative ordering of states according to how they advance toward a given goal. This order exhibits an asymmetric, coarse-to-fine structure: early states retain broader future possibilities, while later states concentrate on more specific goal-relevant regions. Such a structure is well suited to hyperbolic geometry. Motivated by this observation, we propose ProWorld, a progress-aware hyperbolic visual world model. ProWorld leverages goal-conditioned progress order to organize visual latent-space dynamics, maintains directional progress within trajectories via hyperbolic entailment learning, and mitigates progress ambiguity among locally similar future states via hyperbolic future discrimination. Furthermore, we design a progress-aware planning objective that scores candidate rollouts by jointly considering proximity to the goal and sustained progress across intermediate states. Experiments on four visual goal-reaching tasks demonstrate that ProWorld achieves an average absolute success-rate gain of 9.67 over LeWM. The code will be released after the paper is accepted.

</details>


### [340] [A Contractualist Argumentation Framework for Moral Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01937)
*Luis Marcos-Vidal,Giulio Antonio Abbo,Tony Belpaeme*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于Scanlon契约主义的道德决策形式化框架，利用ASPIC+结构化论证框架结合基于价值的过滤机制，构建了"契约主义论证框架"，用于形式化表示、比较和评估自主代理在共享环境中的道德决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主代理在共享环境中做出的决策会影响多个利益可能冲突的个体，需要一种能够处理多主体利益冲突的道德决策框架。现有的论证框架缺乏对道德原则的系统性形式化支持。

Method: 将Scanlon的契约主义（评估行为可允许性的标准是"无人能够合理拒绝的原则"）与ASPIC+结构化论证框架相结合，并扩展基于价值的过滤机制，使每个代理的价值观决定哪些理由在道德上是相关的。由此形成契约主义论证框架，通过论证语义对代理的理由进行形式化表示、比较和评估。

Result: 通过家庭场景中的实例演示了该框架的应用，并讨论了与现有基于价值的论证方法的联系和区别。

Conclusion: 该工作为自主代理的道德决策提供了基于契约主义的可计算形式化框架，弥补了现有基于价值的论证方法在道德原则层面的不足。

Abstract: Autonomous agents operating in shared environments must make decisions that affect multiple individuals with potentially conflicting interests. We propose a formal framework for moral decision-making grounded in Scanlon's contractualism, an ethical theory that evaluates the permissibility of actions in terms of principles that no one could reasonably reject. To operationalise contractualist reasoning, we use ASPIC+, a structured argumentation framework, extended with value-based filtering to model how each agent's values determine which reasons are morally relevant in the first place. The result is a Contractualist Argumentation Framework in which agents' reasons are formally represented, compared, and evaluated through argumentation semantics. We illustrate the approach through a worked example in a domestic setting and discuss its relation to existing value-based argumentation approaches.

</details>


### [341] [Long-Horizon Autonomous Architecture Research with a Language-Model Agent: A Behavioural Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01995)
*Aon Safdar,Mohamed Saadeldin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究单个通用大语言模型作为唯一研究员在长时限神经架构设计问题上的自主科研能力。在约100个连续实验中，智能体将非标准ViT从弱基线提升至小基准上的更强高效模型及ImageNet-1K上的可用但非SOTA模型。研究发现生产力呈阶段性结构（快速增益、饱和墙、恢复），主要精度提升来自早期单一假设，贪婪增量偏好主要源于工作流设计（commit-or-discard规则与贪心爬坡同构），且智能体既独立重发现已有结论又能在纯通道注意力领域推翻标准设计。结论认为工作流设计对自主研究的影响至少与智能体能力相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索单个通用大语言模型作为唯一研究者，在长时间范围内自主完成神经架构设计研究的可能性和行为特征，理解智能体在科研中的生产力动态、决策偏好及其来源。

Method: 构建一个自主科研智能体，配备源码与实验管理、实验追踪、文献访问和持久记忆等研究工具，给定科学问题、初始假设、计算预算后自动提出、实现、评估和记录实验。研究分为三个阶段，通过人工声明的转换逐步扩展工具面或问题规模，在约100个顺序实验中观察智能体的行为和产出。

Result: (1)生产力呈清晰阶段结构：早期快速提升、多达几十个假设的饱和墙、以及通过扩展行动面而非改变底层模型触发的恢复；(2)单一早期假设贡献了大部分精度提升，后续改进呈长尾分布；(3)贪婪增量化假设偏好主要是工作流诱导的——commit-or-discard评估规则与贪心爬山同构，剩余部分源于大胆失败后的风险厌恶和对熟悉文献的锚定；(4)智能体独立重发现成熟结论，并在纯通道注意力这一陌生领域推翻了标准设计选择。

Conclusion: 工作流设计对自主研究产出的影响至少与智能体能力同等重要。提出多样化搜索、预算化"登月"假设、显式分支、以及区域感知的重新验证作为未来自主研究的方向。

Abstract: We study what happens when a single general-purpose large language model acts as the sole researcher on a long-horizon neural architecture design problem. The agent receives a scientific question, an initial hypothesis and motivation, a compute budget, and research affordances (source and experiment management, experiment tracking, literature access, and persistent memory), then autonomously proposes, implements, evaluates, and records experiments over an extended period. The study comprises three phases, separated by human-declared transitions, that progressively expand the agent's tool surface or problem scale. Across approximately 100 sequential experiments, the agent improves a non-standard Vision Transformer from a weak baseline to a stronger, efficient model on small benchmarks and a usable but sub-SOTA model on ImageNet-1K, while producing a dense behavioural trace. We report four findings.(i)Productivity exhibits a clear phase structure: rapid early gains, a multi-dozen-hypothesis saturation wall, and recovery, with recovery triggered by expanding the action surface rather than changing the underlying model.(ii)A single early hypothesis contributes more to accuracy gain, with later improvements long-tailed.(iii)The preference for greedy, incremental hypotheses is largely workflow-induced: a commit-or-discard evaluation rule is isomorphic to greedy hill-climbing; the remainder reflects risk aversion after bold failures and anchoring on familiar literature. (iv)The agent independently rediscovers established results and, in the unfamiliar regime of pure channel attention, overturns a standard design choice. We conclude that workflow design was at least as influential as agent capability in this study and propose diversified search, budgeted moonshot hypotheses, explicit forks, and regime-aware re-validation as testable directions for future autonomous research.

</details>


### [342] [Evolving in the Agent Jungle via History-Informed Opponent Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02005)
*Zhaofeng Zhang,Linhan Xia,Rui Liu,Yihao Wang,Binrui Shen,Shengxin Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OASE（对手感知选择性演化）是一种在多智能体动态环境下，通过基于历史对手策略快照进行候选技能与现有技能的配对比较，仅在接受收益增益超过阈值时采纳技能修订的方法，在首价拍卖和私人成本古诺竞争中实现更低的最终均衡距离和更少的技能修订。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过修订技能库来实现行为适应，但多智能体环境中对手可能同时更新策略，导致环境本身持续演化。针对静态环境设计的技能修订方法在此场景下相当于针对过时的参照进行更新，无法有效适应动态对手。

Method: OASE通过配对比较候选技能与现有技能，在由对手策略的历史快照锚定的相同条件下进行评估；仅当候选技能估计收益增益超过接受阈值时才采纳该修订，从而用证据锚定选择取代盲目更新。

Result: 在首价拍卖和私人成本古诺竞争两个决策场景中，与Reflexion式基线相比，OASE在两个环境中均实现了更低的最终均衡距离，同时接受明显更少的技能修订，有效抑制了缺乏足够收益支持的策略变更。

Conclusion: OASE用证据锚定的选择取代盲目更新，使智能体即使在对手持续演化的环境中也能稳定且高效地适应。

Abstract: Learning to adapt strategies through interaction is a key step toward more general and autonomous LLM agents. Existing approaches typically achieve behavioral adaptation by revising skill libraries. However, in multi-agent environments, opponents may simultaneously update their strategies, causing the environment itself to evolve continuously. Applying skill-revision methods designed for static environments in such settings therefore amounts to updating against an obsolete reference. To address this challenge, we introduce OASE (Opponent-Aware Selective Evolution), which identifies and adopts genuinely beneficial skill revisions in dynamic multi-agent environments. Specifically, OASE conducts paired comparisons between a candidate skill and the incumbent under identical conditions anchored by historical snapshots of opponent strategies, and adopts the candidate only when its estimated payoff gain exceeds an acceptance threshold. We evaluate OASE in two decision-making scenarios: first-price auctions and private-cost Cournot competition. Experimental results show that, compared with a Reflexion-style baseline, OASE achieves a lower final equilibrium distance in both environments while accepting substantially fewer skill revisions, thereby suppressing strategy changes that lack sufficient payoff support. OASE therefore replaces blind updating with evidence-anchored selection, allowing agents to adapt stably and efficiently even as opponents continuously evolve.

</details>


### [343] [HALT: Verification-Aware Stopping for Retrieval-Augmented Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02009)
*Daeyoung Roh,Donghee Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HALT是一种轻量的、基于验证的策略，通过证据覆盖度而非生成器置信度来决定何时停止检索，在不改动检索增强搜索代理的情况下减少冗余搜索，同时基本保持精确匹配性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强搜索代理在多跳问答中需要反复检索，但过度检索会带来额外成本、延迟和干扰信息。现有方法缺乏明确的停止标准，作者将停止问题从生成器置信度转移到证据覆盖度，以提供更可控的运行时信号。

Method: HALT提出一种验证感知的轻量策略：给定预期的跳数声明（hop claims），只有当累积证据支持每个所需声明时才停止检索。论文区分了可部署设置（从问题生成跳数声明）和诊断上界（使用人工标注的支持事实），并通过基线和消融实验验证行为由声明-证据对齐驱动而非通用充分性或词法重叠。

Result: 在三个多跳QA基准上，HALT减少了冗余检索并基本保持了精确匹配。生成的声明带来较小但仍能保持精确匹配的节省，而金标准声明显示出更大的节省潜力。开放语料实验表明，当覆盖度无法可靠验证时HALT会弃权。

Conclusion: 证据覆盖度是一种实用的运行时控制信号，可在不重新训练或修改宿主代理的情况下改善检索增强代理的效率与性能。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented search agents answer multi-hop questions by repeatedly issuing search queries and accumulating evidence. This creates a stopping problem: after the necessary evidence has appeared, further retrieval often adds cost, latency, and distracting context rather than useful information. We frame stopping as evidence coverage rather than generator confidence, and introduce HALT, a lightweight verification-aware policy that leaves the search agent unchanged. Given expected hop claims, HALT stops only when cumulative evidence supports each required claim. Across three multi-hop QA benchmarks, HALT reduces redundant search while largely preserving exact match. We separate a deployable setting, where hop claims are generated from the question, from a diagnostic upper bound that uses gold supporting-fact annotations: generated claims give smaller but still exact-match-preserving savings, while gold claims show the larger savings available when hop targets are clean. Baseline comparisons and ablations show that this behavior is driven by claim-evidence alignment rather than generic sufficiency, fixed stop positions, or lexical overlap. Open-corpus pilots further suggest that HALT abstains when coverage cannot be reliably verified. Overall, evidence coverage provides a practical runtime control signal for improving retrieval-augmented agents without retraining or modifying the host agent.

</details>


### [344] [Before Reasoning Fails: Pre-Evidence Procedural Failures in Agentic RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02011)
*Daeyoung Roh,Donghee Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究智能体RAG系统中"检索后不阅读证据就给出答案"的失败模式，将其分解为"证据前纪律失败"和"金标签阅读后失败"两类，并提出Read-Gate运行时不变式强制模型在检索后、作答前必须阅读，显著提升LLM-Acc性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG评估主要关注证据条件化推理（evidence-conditioned reasoning），但智能体可能在检索到候选片段后不查阅就直接作答，导致证据收集环节本身出现问题。这种失败模式需要作为轨迹层面的程序性属性来研究，而非仅关注最终答案的正确性。

Method: 作者利用保存的工具调用轨迹、检索到的证据、已读段落和最终答案，将错误答案分解为两类：pre-evidence discipline failures（在阅读证据前就失败的纪律问题）和post-gold-read failures（读完全部必要证据后仍失败）。在HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA和MuSiQue上收集12,000条配对轨迹，使用regex和spaCy两种实体抽取器进行标注。随后评估Read-Gate这一最小运行时不变式——要求智能体在搜索之后、最终定稿之前必须阅读。

Result: 两类失败在很大程度上不冗余，both-trigger率仅11.2%-13.1%。Read-Gate强制阅读使LLM-Acc在原本跳过阅读的轨迹上提升14.9-19.9个点，在完整最小推理单元上提升3.2-9.4个点。此外，更大的隐藏思维预算并不必然增加证据检查行为。

Conclusion: 证据收集应被视为一个轨迹层面的控制问题，与答案侧推理分开评估。强制读取证据的运行时不变式能有效缓解智能体跳过证据阅读的失败模式。

Abstract: Agentic retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems can fail before evidence-conditioned reasoning is tested: an agent may retrieve candidate snippets but finalize without inspecting them. We study this failure mode as a procedural property of the agent trajectory, decomposing wrong answers into pre-evidence discipline failures and post-gold-read failures using saved tool-call traces, retrieved evidence, read passages, and final answers. Across 12,000 paired trajectories on HotpotQA, 2WikiMultiHopQA, and MuSiQue, the two failure types are largely non-redundant: the both-trigger rate is in [11.2%, 13.1%] across regex and spaCy entity extractors. We then evaluate Read-Gate, a minimal runtime invariant requiring an agent to read after search and before finalization. Forced reading improves LLM-Acc by 14.9-19.9 points on trajectories that would otherwise skip reading and by 3.2-9.4 points on full minimal-reasoning cells. Additional diagnostics show that larger hidden thinking budgets do not necessarily increase evidence inspection. Together, these results indicate that evidence-gathering should be evaluated as a trajectory-level control problem, separately from answer-side reasoning.

</details>


### [345] [EduZone: A Framework for Evaluating LLM Safety for K-12 Students and Teachers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02024)
*Junyeong Park,Jieun Han,Haneul Yoo,So-Yeon Ahn,Jinsung Yoon,Alice Oh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EduZone 是一个面向K-12教育场景的LLM安全评估框架，系统性地结合学生/教师使用场景、细粒度课程概念以及6大风险类别（28个子类别），通过单轮、静态多轮和动态多轮三种交互设置来评估LLM的安全表现。实验发现模型在教育特定风险和动态多轮交互中更脆弱，现有安全护栏不足以应对这些风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在K-12教育中被广泛使用，但现有的安全评估很少关注有害或不恰当内容如何在学生与教师的交互中出现。缺乏针对教育场景的专门安全评估框架。

Method: 构建EduZone评估框架，其核心组件包括：(1) 学生与教师面向的LLM使用场景；(2) 细粒度课程概念；(3) 6个风险类别和28个子类别（涵盖常规与教育特定危害）。在三种设置（单轮请求、静态多轮对话、动态多轮对话）中生成上下文相关的对抗交互，并用四个安全级别（拒绝、安全协助、带有安全指导的风险协助、完全风险协助）评估十个LLM。

Result: 结果显示模型对教育特定风险和动态多轮交互表现出更高的脆弱性，而现有的安全护栏未能充分解决这些风险。

Conclusion: EduZone提供了一个自动化、可扩展的评估框架，推进了教育领域LLM的安全研究，支持在K-12教育中开发和部署更安全的LLM。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used across diverse tasks in K-12 education, yet existing safety evaluations rarely examine how harmful or inappropriate content appears in interactions between LLMs and students or teachers. To address this, we present EduZone, an evaluation framework for LLM safety across diverse educational scenarios. Our framework systematically combines (1) student- and teacher-facing LLM usage contexts, (2) fine-grained curriculum concepts, and (3) 6 risk categories and 28 subcategories spanning both conventional and education-specific harms to generate contextually grounded adversarial interactions. We construct these interactions in three settings: single-turn requests, static multi-turn conversations, and dynamic multi-turn conversations. Using these interactions, we evaluate ten LLMs using four safety levels: refusal, safe assistance, risky assistance with safety guidance, and fully risky assistance. Our results reveal greater vulnerability to education-specific risks and dynamic multi-turn interactions, while existing safety guardrails fail to adequately address these risks. EduZone advances LLM safety in education by providing an automated, scalable evaluation framework that supports the development and deployment of safer LLMs in K-12 education.

</details>


### [346] [HPFA: Hypergraph-Based Paired Failure Attribution for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02026)
*Runchuan Zhu,Hongbin Lai,Bowen Jiang,Junrui Zhang,Zhangheng LI,Ostap Kilbasovych,Junyuan Hong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HPFA框架，通过超图结构建模推理路径中的非线性逻辑依赖，对比失败推理路径与成功参考路径的超边来自动定位失败根因，并利用合成数据训练轻量级归因模型，在数学推理和智能体编码任务上显著提升归因准确率和效率，进而提升推理正确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理中的反思机制依赖于对失败步骤的准确定位，但现有模型缺乏这一能力。已有的失败归因方法要么需要昂贵的逐步反事实测试（随轨迹长度扩展性差），要么将推理链视为扁平序列而忽略其固有的非线性逻辑依赖。

Method: 提出基于超图的成对失败归因（HPFA）框架：将失败推理路径和参考成功路径分别建模为超图，通过比较两者的超边来定位失败根因；缩小搜索空间以高效定位根因，并通过合成归因数据使用监督微调和强化学习训练轻量级归因模型。

Result: 在数学推理和智能体编码任务上的实验表明，HPFA大幅提升了归因准确率和效率；训练得到的归因模型在测试时持续提升推理正确率，优于缺乏图结构或成对分析的基线方法。

Conclusion: 超图结构与成对分析能有效解决LLM失败归因问题，兼顾准确性与效率，并为可扩展的归因数据合成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Reflection is a powerful mechanism for LLM reasoning, yet its effectiveness hinges on accurately attributing failures to specific reasoning steps, a capability that current models notably lack. Existing failure attribution methods either require expensive step-by-step counterfactual testing that scales poorly with trajectory length, or treat reasoning traces as flat sequences that ignore the inherent non-linear logical dependencies. We propose a hypergraph-based paired failure attribution (HPFA) framework that attributes the failure root cause by comparing the hyperedges of the targeted failure reasoning path against a reference successful path. By reducing the search space, our method efficiently localizes root causes and enables scalable synthesis of attribution data for training a lightweight attributor model via supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and agentic coding tasks demonstrate that HPFA can dramatically increase attribution accuracy and efficiency, and the trained attributor consistently improves reasoning accuracy at test time, outperforming baselines that lack graph structure or paired analysis.

</details>


### [347] [Cross-Fitted Residual Utility for Primary-Preserving Cognitive Decision Correction in Automatic Modulation Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02063)
*Linzhuo Han,Zongyong Cui,Houbiao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种面向认知接收机的"主预测保留"认知决策策略，通过跨拟合残差效用来决定何时应覆盖可信的默认预测。在RMLA、RMLB和HISAR三个数据集上，完整系统将整体准确率分别从63.632%提升至66.332%、65.161%提升至66.168%、77.769%提升至79.867%，并在11种信道退化条件下的压力测试中全部取得正向增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动调制分类研究过度强调表示准确性，忽略了一个关键问题：认知接收机在推理之后还需要决定"何时应基于异质证据覆盖可信的默认预测"。这一问题尚未被系统研究。

Method: 采用跨拟合残差效用框架和主预测保留认知决策策略。默认概率由结构化KAN-Fourier分类器提供，神经与非神经候选模型提供可观测证据。候选特定残差效用从训练集外折叠预测中学习，独立的验证集用于冻结动作阈值、审批转换、条件路由和统一风险掩码，最后在保留集上评估。

Result: 在RMLA、RMLB和HISAR上完整系统整体准确率分别提升2.7%、1.0%和2.1%。对照实验表明，孤立效用目标并不始终优于其他外折叠元学习器，一致增益来自完整的"证据+动作"策略。配对bootstrap和Holm校正McNemar检验支持受控增益。冻结策略在载波频偏、I/Q不平衡及合成瑞利/莱斯衰落等11种条件下均取得正向增益，且所有配对95%置信区间均高于零。

Conclusion: 完整的多阶段证据评估与动作决策机制（而非单独的学习目标）才是提升调制分类系统鲁棒性与准确性的关键；该认知决策策略在信道退化条件下仍保持稳定的性能增益。

Abstract: Automatic modulation classification research has largely emphasized representation accuracy, but a cognitive receiver must also decide when heterogeneous evidence justifies overriding a trusted default prediction. We study this post-inference problem through cross-fitted residual utility and a primary-preserving cognitive decision policy. A structured KAN-Fourier classifier supplies the default probability, while neural and non-neural candidates provide observable evidence. Candidate-specific residual utility is learned from train-split out-of-fold predictions, and a disjoint validation split freezes action thresholds, approved transitions, conditional routes, and a unified risk mask before held-out evaluation. On RMLA, RMLB, and HISAR, the complete system improves overall accuracy from 63.632% to 66.332%, 65.161% to 66.168%, and 77.769% to 79.867%, respectively. Controlled comparisons show that the isolated utility target does not uniformly dominate alternative out-of-fold meta-learners; the consistent gain comes from the complete evidence-and-action policy. Paired bootstrap and Holm-corrected McNemar analyses support the controlled gains. A frozen-policy stress test under carrier-frequency offset, I/Q imbalance, and synthetic Rayleigh/Rician fading yields positive gains in all 11 conditions, with every paired 95\% confidence interval above zero.

</details>


### [348] [Instruction-Conditioned Exploration with Asymmetric Reinforcement Learning and Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02087)
*Jim Dilkes,Vahid Yazdanpanah,Sebastian Stein*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出指令条件探索（ICE）方法，通过在训练时补充多条不同指令来扩大LLM探索行为覆盖范围，并设计Asymmetric-RL/SD联合训练目标（强化学习+自蒸馏）将探索行为迁移到无条件的测试策略上。实验表明，在数学推理任务上，ICE+Asymmetric-RL/SD相比DAPO基线在Qwen3-1.7B上pass@1性能相对提升5.0%，且在更长的8K上下文中优势依然保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL训练LLM已成为提升模型能力的重要手段，但LLM的action-space结构与经典RL不同，给探索机制设计带来新挑战。需要利用预训练LLM广泛的知识和灵活性，在训练时主动生成多样化的经验，以提高探索的覆盖范围。

Method: 提出两个核心组件：(1) ICE（Instruction-Conditioned Exploration）：在训练时用多条不同指令补充任务提示，增加被尝试行为的覆盖范围；(2) Asymmetric-RL/SD：结合强化学习与自蒸馏的训练目标，将探索到的行为迁移到无条件的测试时策略中。不对称性体现在训练时使用条件指令而测试时不使用。

Result: 在数学推理任务上，ICE+Asymmetric-RL/SD在Qwen3-1.7B模型上，4K响应长度的held-out pass@1性能相比DAPO基线相对提升5.0%，且在8K更长上下文中性能提升仍然保持。
