Conclusion: 指令条件探索配合不对称RL/自蒸馏目标能有效提升LLM在RL训练中的探索效率和行为迁移能力，从而改善测试时的泛化性能，尤其是在长上下文的数学推理场景中表现出持续性优势。

Abstract: Post-training Large Language Models (LLMs) with Reinforcement Learning (RL) has become an important tool for improving model capabilities, but the LLM action-space structure introduces challenges distinct from classical RL, with implications for inducing exploration. New methods are required that leverage the broad knowledge and flexibility of pre-trained LLMs to deliberately generate diverse experience at training time. We propose Instruction-Conditioned Exploration (ICE), which supplements task prompts during training with one of several distinct instructions, increasing the coverage of behaviours attempted. To facilitate ICE, we propose Asymmetric-RL/SD, a combined Reinforcement Learning and Self-Distillation training objective, to transfer explored behaviours to the unconditioned test-time policy. ICE with the Asymmetric-RL/SD objective improves Qwen3-1.7B held-out pass@1 performance at $4$K response length on mathematical reasoning tasks by $5.0\%$ relative to training with DAPO, with improvement persisting at a longer 8K context.

</details>


### [349] [Fetch-then-Explore: Decoupling Selection from Extraction over a Persistent Workspace for Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02097)
*Qi Liu,Yiqun Chen,Zidan Chen,Yan Gao,Yi Wu,Yao Hu,Jiaxin Mao,Fengbin Zhu,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Fetch-then-Explore架构，将页面选择与证据提取分离，把页面持久化到文件系统工作区而非上下文窗口或瞬时会话，使代理能在后续轮次按需重新访问页面提取证据，在BrowseComp和WideSearch基准上优于visit-and-read和browsing基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有搜索代理的核心瓶颈不在如何找到页面，而在如何阅读页面。当前两种主流文档接口——visit-and-read和stateful browsing——都将页面与打开时刻绑定：前者在获取时固定阅读内容，后者仅持有一页且切换即释放。导致页面在后继轮次需要时须重新抓取和渲染，效率低下且丢失信息。

Method: Fetch-then-Explore分离页面选择与证据提取：页面被记录在每问题专属的文件系统工作区中（而非上下文窗口或瞬时会话），证据按需延迟提取并可随假设细化而重复提取。在统一ReAct框架、固定搜索条件下，对比snippet-only、visit-and-read和browsing三种基线，使用三个agent backbone在BrowseComp和WideSearch两个开放网络基准上评估。

Result: Fetch-then-Explore在BrowseComp所有backbone上均领先准确率，在WideSearch上总体匹配或超越基线。行为分析表明收益源于其核心动作——离开后返回页面进行二次访问，该行为远超任何瞬时接口，使首轮遗漏的证据可在后续恢复。

Conclusion: 将页面持久化存储而非绑定到单一访问时刻，能够显著提升搜索代理的证据累积能力，使代理可以在假设演进时反复挖掘已选页面，克服瞬态界面的信息丢失问题。

Abstract: Search agents now answer questions that take dozens of searches to settle, yet how such an agent reads a page has drawn far less attention than how it finds one. Nearly all of them use one of two document interfaces, and both tie a page to the moment it is opened. \emph{Visit-and-read} injects a reading of the page into the message history at fetch time, fixing that reading before the agent knows which fact it will need. Stateful \emph{browsing} instead extracts on demand from the page in hand, but holds one page at a time and releases it as soon as the agent opens another. Either way, a page that turns out to matter many turns later has to be fetched and rendered into context all over again. We propose \textbf{Fetch-then-Explore}, which separates page selection from evidence extraction and keeps what it selects: pages are recorded in a per-question workspace on the filesystem rather than the context window or a transient session, and evidence is pulled from them on demand later. Selection becomes almost free, extraction can wait until the agent knows what to look for and be repeated as its hypothesis sharpens, and pages are not released when the agent moves on, so evidence accumulates across the trajectory. In a unified ReAct harness with fixed search, we compare Fetch-then-Explore against snippet-only, visit-and-read, and browsing baselines on two open-web benchmarks, BrowseComp and WideSearch, across three agent backbones. It leads BrowseComp accuracy at every backbone and generally matches or exceeds the baselines on WideSearch, and a behavioral analysis traces the gains to the workspace's defining move: returning to a page after leaving it, which it does far more than any transient interface, so evidence missed on a first pass can still be recovered later.

</details>


### [350] [MemArbiter: Decision-Time Memory Arbitration for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02113)
*Jiajun Dong,Yutao Hu,Fengrui Fan,Shihan Dou,Yueming Wu,Deqing Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MemArbiter，一个功能感知的记忆仲裁框架，用于解决LLM智能体在长程任务中记忆可访问但难以有效指导行动的问题（即"记忆-行动鸿沟"）。该方法在ALFWorld基准上显著超越基线，分别达到82.8%和92.5%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要提升记忆的可访问性，但即使信息可被检索到，若其形成、组织、优先级排序或呈现方式不佳，仍无法有效指导当前决策。作者将此问题定义为"记忆-行动鸿沟"（Memory-Action Gap），特别关注记忆管理所导致的那部分鸿沟。

Method: MemArbiter将交互历史分解为原子项，组织成五个功能性记忆库（Memory Banks）；结合库级需求、项级相关性、焦点-环境表示（focal-ambient representations）以及时间呈现门控（temporal presentation gate）来动态控制记忆显著性。

Result: 在ALFWorld上，对照统一每步记忆预算下的Flat Retrieval和Flat Recency基线，MemArbiter在500和750 token预算下分别达到82.8%和92.5%的成功率，分别超出最强基线20.9和25.4个百分点；同时改善失败后恢复能力，减少失败动作重复和状态-动作复发。

Conclusion: 功能感知的记忆仲裁机制能够使可访问的信息更有效地指导智能体行动，说明记忆管理层面的优化是弥合记忆与行动鸿沟的关键方向。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents must retain and use cross-step information to act coherently in long-horizon tasks. Existing methods improve memory accessibility, yet action-relevant information may still fail to guide the current decision because it is poorly formed, organized, prioritized, or presented. We call this post-access failure the Memory-Action Gap. We propose MemArbiter, a function-aware memory arbitration framework that addresses the memory-management-induced component of this gap. MemArbiter decomposes interaction histories into atomic items, organizes them into five functional Memory Banks, and combines bank-level demand, item-level relevance, focal-ambient representations, and a temporal presentation gate to dynamically control memory salience. We evaluate MemArbiter on ALFWorld against Flat Retrieval and Flat Recency under unified per-step memory budgets. With an open-weight action-generation model, MemArbiter achieves success rates of 82.8% and 92.5% under 500- and 750-token budgets, outperforming the strongest baseline by 20.9 and 25.4 percentage points, respectively. It also improves post-failure recovery and reduces failed-action repetition and state-action recurrence. These results show that function-aware memory arbitration enables accessible information to guide actions more effectively.

</details>


### [351] [Beyond Solution-Centric Search: Adaptive Inquiry and Knowledge Revision for Autonomous ML Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02143)
*Shaokang Fu,Yulong Tao,Linbo Jin,Jiarong Zhao,Qiming Shi,Tianjun Pan,Haonan Li,Chengyu Wang,Jia Wu,Chengfu Huo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出信息范式替代传统的以解为中心的搜索范式来解决长时程自主研究任务。作者将其实例化为Iris系统，一个"探究-修正"循环，通过演化中的信息状态来引导解决方案改进。在MLE-Bench基准上，Iris在12小时预算下达到64.9%的任意奖牌率，为所有对比系统中最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在长时程自主研究任务（如机器学习工程）中，通常通过树、图或链式结构组织候选方案的改进，即"以解为中心的搜索"。这种设计使搜索过程决定了信息的获取和管理方式，导致决策质量受限于搜索拓扑。作者认为应当转向信息范式，让系统对任务的理解（信息状态）来主导解决方案的改进，从而更好地应对有限预算下的相互依赖决策。

Method: Iris采用"探究-修正"循环。在信息获取方面，Iris从当前信息状态生成局部行动计划，使用认知动作探测决策关键的不确定因素，而不修改已保留的解决方案。在信息管理方面，Iris将跨实验的观察结果综合为任务知识，该知识由可修订的主张（claim）构成，每个主张带有明确的适用范围（scope）和状态（status）。随着新证据出现，Iris更新这些知识，并根据所需细节层级，从原始证据、结构化摘要或任务知识中构建每个决策上下文。

Result: 在MLE-Bench基准上，Iris在12小时预算下达到64.9%的任意奖牌率，为所有对比系统中最高的表现。在涵盖机器工程构建和模型后训练的四项任务中，Iris展现出跨领域的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 信息范式下的Iris系统证明了以信息状态为中心设计的有效性，显著优于传统的以解为中心的搜索方法。通过明确的认知动作和信息管理机制（可修订主张、范围与状态标注、多粒度决策上下文构建），Iris能够在有限预算下做出更高质量的相互依赖决策，并展现出良好的跨领域泛化能力。

Abstract: Long-horizon autonomous research tasks such as machine learning engineering require systems to make interdependent decisions under a limited budget. Existing LLM-based agents typically organize candidate-solution improvement through tree, graph, or chain structures, meaning that the search process determines how information is acquired and managed. We call this design solution-centric search and propose instead the information paradigm, in which an evolving information state represents the system's understanding of the task and guides solution improvement. We instantiate this paradigm in Iris, an inquiry-revision loop. For information acquisition, Iris generates local action plans from the current information state and uses epistemic actions to probe decision-critical unknowns without modifying the retained solution. For information management, Iris synthesizes observations across experiments into task knowledge composed of revisable claims with explicit scope and status. It updates this knowledge as new evidence arrives and constructs each decision context from raw evidence, structured summaries, or task knowledge at the required level of detail. On MLE-Bench, Iris attains a 64.9% any-medal rate under a 12-hour budget, the highest among compared systems. Across four tasks spanning harness engineering and model post-training, Iris also demonstrates cross-domain generalization.

</details>


### [352] [Beyond the Mean: Multi-Moment Policy Optimization for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02149)
*Yijun Zhang,Yule Xie,Jiaxin Ding,Xin Ding,Fan Xu,Haoxiang Zhang,Luoyi Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出矩视角下的LLM推理策略优化框架MMPO，将失败概率视为随机变量并通过联合最小化其多阶矩来实现更优的策略优化，在五个数学推理基准上超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法优化LLM推理能力时，通常仅针对失败概率的单一矩（如期望）进行优化，忽略了失败概率分布的整体结构，导致优化目标不够全面。

Method: 提出Multi-Moment Policy Optimization (MMPO)框架，将失败概率视为随机变量，联合最小化其多个矩；同时开发了通用的矩变换框架，可系统性地诱导不同矩分布特征，统一了更广泛的策略优化目标族。

Result: 在五个数学推理基准数据集和不同规模的模型上，MMPO一致性地优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 矩视角为LLM推理策略优化目标的设计提供了新见解，MMPO通过多阶矩联合优化能够更好地刻画失败概率分布，从而提升推理性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has become a central paradigm for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models. Existing methods generally aim to reduce the failure probabilities induced across problems. In this paper, we introduce a moment-based perspective on policy optimization for LLM reasoning by treating the failure probability of a randomly sampled problem as a random variable and characterizing optimization objectives through its moments. Under this perspective, many existing methods optimize only a single moment of the failure-probability distribution, leaving its broader distributional structure largely uncharacterized. We propose \textbf{M}ulti-\textbf{M}oment \textbf{P}olicy \textbf{O}ptimization (MMPO), a novel policy optimization framework that jointly minimizes multiple moments of the failure-probability distribution. MMPO admits a direct operational interpretation as minimizing the expected truncated time required to obtain the first successful response. Beyond MMPO, we further develop a general moment-transformation framework that systematically induces different moment profiles and provides a unified view of a broader family of policy optimization objectives. Experiments across five mathematical reasoning benchmarks and models of different scales demonstrate that MMPO consistently outperforms strong baselines. We hope this moment-based perspective offers new insights into the design of policy optimization objectives for LLM reasoning.

</details>


### [353] [Auditing Data Provenance in LLM Fine-tuning via Intrinsic Distributional Fingerprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02154)
*Zirui Huang,Yunlong Mao,Wei Tong,Tingting Wu,Xin Ge,Sheng Zhong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Distribution Provenance Audit (DPA)，一种事后审计框架，用于在黑盒和恶意设置下检测LLM微调中的数据知识产权侵权。DPA基于核心洞察：无论规避手段如何，模型为保持实用性必须保留语义内容与词法形式的交集，该交集被捕获为内在分布指纹。DPA将审计形式化为统计假设检验，通过无偏输出采样量化指纹以可靠拒绝"未使用"原假设，在医疗和法律微调任务上优于现有基线，并对对抗性规避（释义和知识蒸馏）保持鲁棒性。文章还指出高保真指纹的双重用途张力：既可支持可靠审计，也可能促进隐私攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定制化LLM的泛滥通过未经授权的微调带来了严重的数据知识产权（Data IP）侵权风险。现有审计技术存在两方面局限：一是需要在数据准备或训练阶段进行干预（无法事后审计）；二是在恶意混淆手段（如数据释义、知识蒸馏）下表现脆弱。因此需要一种无需前期干预、能在黑盒和恶意场景下可靠检测数据IP侵权的审计框架。

Method: DPA框架的核心方法包括：(1) 核心洞察——无论微调策略如何规避来源追溯，保持实用性的需求迫使模型保留语义内容与词法形式的交集，该交集被捕获为内在分布指纹；(2) 将审计问题形式化为统计假设检验，通过无偏输出采样量化分布指纹，以可靠拒绝"模型未使用专有数据"的零假设；(3) 该框架支持黑盒访问设置，无需了解模型内部结构或训练细节。

Result: 在医疗和法律两种领域的微调任务上进行的大量实验表明，DPA在检测数据知识产权侵权方面持续优于现有基线方法。在面对采用释义和知识蒸馏进行规避的对抗性训练者时，DPA依然保持鲁棒性。此外，文章揭示了基本的双重用途张力：用于可靠审计的高保真分布指纹同样可能被用于促进隐私攻击。

Conclusion: DPA提供了一种有效的事后数据IP侵权审计方案，能在黑盒和恶意设置下可靠检测LLM未经授权的微调行为，突破了现有方法需早期干预和抗混淆能力弱的局限。同时，该工作揭示了审计能力与隐私风险之间的内在矛盾，为数据IP保护与隐私安全之间的权衡提供了重要启示。

Abstract: The proliferation of customized Large Language Models (LLMs) poses critical risks of Data Intellectual Property (Data IP) infringement via unauthorized fine-tuning on proprietary data. Existing audit techniques are limited, as they require intervention during data preparation or training and remain fragile under malicious obfuscations such as data paraphrasing and knowledge distillation.
  We propose \textit{Distribution Provenance Audit (DPA)}, a post-hoc framework for auditing data IP infringement in LLM fine-tuning under black-box and malicious settings. DPA is grounded in a critical insight: regardless of fine-tuning tactics to evade provenance, the practical necessity of maintaining utility constrains the model to preserve the fundamental intersection of semantic substance and lexical form. Accordingly, DPA captures this persistent lexical-semantic intersection as intrinsic distributional fingerprints. The framework formulates the audit as a statistical hypothesis test, effectively quantifying these fingerprints via unbiased output sampling to reliably reject the null hypothesis of non-usage.
  Extensive experiments on medical and legal fine-tuning tasks show that DPA consistently outperforms existing baselines, remaining robust against adversarial trainers employing paraphrasing and knowledge distillation. We further highlight a fundamental dual-use tension: the same high-fidelity distributional fingerprints enabling reliable auditing may also facilitate privacy attacks.

</details>


### [354] [From Simple QA to Deep Research: A Verifiable Benchmark Constructed through Iterative Task Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02163)
*Can Wang,Haoran Chen,Haowen Gao,Hao Ding,Zhaoyang Liu,Zhiying Tu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个包含500个深度研究任务的验证性基准测试，覆盖31个主题和10大类别，通过自动化的Explorer-Formalizer-Challenger流程构建，每个任务以有向无环图（DAG）表示原子步骤和检查点，实现可溯源、与人类对齐且稳定的细粒度评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度研究基准测试要么依赖专家编写，要么依赖已有的人工材料，而完全自动化的构建方式难以保证一致性和可溯源的验证。因此作者旨在填补这一空白，构建一个既能自动生成又具备可靠评估能力的大规模深度研究基准。

Method: 采用迭代式Explorer-Formalizer-Challenger流水线，将简单问题逐步转化为深度研究任务。每个任务被表示为原子步骤构成的有向无环图（DAG）及关联的检查点，使查询、DAG和评分标准能够以受控方式协同演化。评估采用基于事实依据的逐点评分标准。

Result: 实验表明该基准能够清晰区分不同模型和查询类型之间的表现差异，基于事实的逐点评分标准实现了细粒度、与人类对齐且稳定的评估效果。

Conclusion: 该基准通过自动构建与可验证评估相结合的方式，为深度研究任务提供了可靠的评测框架，数据集、实现代码和实验结果均已公开。

Abstract: Deep research benchmarks require expert-level tasks and reliable evaluation grounded in task-specific knowledge. Existing benchmarks rely heavily on expert authoring or pre-existing human-authored materials, while fully automatic construction struggles to ensure consistent and traceable verification. To address this gap, we introduce a verifiable benchmark of 500 deep research tasks spanning 31 topics and 10 major categories, with three query forms designed to probe complementary capabilities required for deep research. The benchmark is constructed automatically using an iterative Explorer-Formalizer-Challenger pipeline that progressively transforms simple questions into deep research tasks. Each task is represented as a directed acyclic graph (DAG) of atomic steps and associated checkpoints, enabling the query, DAG, and rubrics to evolve together in a controlled manner. Experiments demonstrate that the benchmark clearly discriminates among models and query types, while its fact-grounded pointwise rubrics enable fine-grained, human-aligned, and stable evaluation. Our data, implementation, and results are publicly available.

</details>


### [355] [From Profiling to Synthesis: Benchmarking Implicit Behavioral Alignment in Personalized LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02171)
*Jiajia Song,Bobo Li,Haiwen Yi,Zibo Ji,Meishan Zhang,Hao Fei,Min Zhang,Mong-Li Lee,Wynne Hsu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IBA-Bench，一个用于评测LLM智能体隐式行为对齐（从含噪声、隐式线索和时间不一致性的纵向交互历史中推断用户约束）的基准，并提出了IBA-Agent框架，通过广泛检索和轨迹级对齐来调和冲突的用户偏好，实验表明其显著提升了九个领域中的行为对齐能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化智能体评测主要依赖静态偏好快照、固定交互日志或对预定义用户档案进行问答，无法捕捉用户偏好随时间演化的复杂性，也忽略了偏好条件化的任务执行——作者将其称为"知识到行动差距"（knowledge-to-action gap）。

Method: 作者提出IBA-Bench基准，利用纵向交互历史（包含噪声、隐式线索和时间不一致性）评测智能体是否能在执行任务时满足从历史交互中推断出的隐式用户约束。同时提出IBA-Agent框架，通过广泛检索（broad retrieval）和轨迹级对齐（trajectory-level alignment）来调和冲突的用户偏好优先级。

Result: 实验结果表明，最先进的LLM智能体在IBA-Bench上的个性化表现仍面临显著挑战；而提出的IBA-Agent在九个应用领域的复杂场景中大幅提升了行为对齐能力。

Conclusion: 有效的个性化对现有SOTA LLM智能体仍是重大挑战，IBA-Agent通过轨迹级对齐和广泛检索可显著改善复杂场景下的隐式行为对齐，为个性化智能体评测提供了更贴近真实场景的基准。

Abstract: Large Language Models have enabled increasingly capable autonomous agents, yet personalization remains critical for making such agents practically useful. Recent benchmarks have begun evaluating personalization in agents, but they largely rely on static preference snapshots, fixed interaction logs, or question answering over predefined user profiles. Such designs fail to capture the complexity of evolving user preferences and neglect preference-conditioned task execution-a discrepancy we term as the knowledge-to-action gap. To address this challenge, we introduce IBA-Bench, a benchmark for implicit behavioral alignment constructed from longitudinal interaction histories that contain noise, implicit cues, and temporal inconsistencies. Unlike prior work, IBA-Bench evaluates whether an agent can execute tasks while satisfying implicit user constraints inferred from historical interactions. We further propose IBA-Agent, an agent framework that reconciles conflicting priorities through broad retrieval and trajectory-level alignment. Experiment results on IBA-Bench show that effective personalization remains a significant challenge for state-of-the-art LLM agents, and the proposed IBA-Agent substantially improves behavioral alignment in complex scenarios across nine application domains.

</details>


### [356] [PAC Approximation and DIRECT Optimization for Parametric Markov Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02184)
*Zhiming Chi,Ying Liu,Andrea Turrini,Lijun Zhang,David N. Jansen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对参数化马尔可夫决策过程（pMDP）中的参数综合与优化问题，采用情景方法（scenario approach）通过采样参数配置并求解线性规划，获得PRCTL属性满足值的概率近似正确（PAC）多项式逼近。该框架可与统计模型检验（SMC）结合以分析黑盒参数模型，并进一步集成DIRECT算法进行无导数全局优化，在Lipschitz和PAC好集假设下建立了条件最优性差距保证。在2997个基准上的实验表明，DIRECT变体虽解决的实例少于情景优化器，但在共同成功的实例上通常返回更优的目标值且运行更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算pMDP中PRCTL属性的有理函数f(即参数估值到满足值的映射)在计算上非常昂贵，尤其是当最优策略在整个参数空间上变化时。传统精确计算方法复杂度高，需要更高效的近似方法来综合和优化参数。

Method: 采用情景方法：通过采样参数配置并求解线性规划，获得PAC近似多项式逼近，保证误差边界在给定置信度下对参数域除ε比例外全部有效。将PAC框架与统计模型检验结合以支持黑盒参数模型分析。基于PAC近似，集成DIRECT（划分矩形）算法进行无导数全局优化，并在Lipschitz和PAC好集假设下建立条件最优性差距上界。

Result: 在2997个基准上的实证评估表明：DIRECT变体解决的实例数少于情景优化器，但其共同成功解决的实例上通常返回略优的目标值且运行速度更快，结果与情景值在PAC误差范围内保持接近。

Conclusion: 该工作为pMDP参数综合与优化提供了高效且具有理论保证的近似方案，DIRECT在解质量与运行时间上具有竞争力，但完整情景优化器在可解实例覆盖面方面仍然占优。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the parameter synthesis and optimization problem for parametric Markov decision processes (pMDPs), the extension of classical MDPs where exact probability values are replaced by parametric expressions. Computing the rational function $f_{\lsf}$ that maps parameter valuations to the satisfaction value of a PRCTL property $\lsf$ is a computationally expensive task, particularly for pMDPs where the optimal policy may vary across the parameter space. We adopt the \emph{scenario approach} to efficiently synthesize a probably approximately correct (PAC) approximation $\ApproxFunOfProperty{f}$ of $f_{\lsf}$: by sampling parameter configurations and solving a linear program, we obtain a polynomial approximation whose error margin $\margin$ is guaranteed, with prescribed confidence, for all but an $\errorRate$-fraction of the parameter domain under the sampling distribution. We further show how this PAC framework can be combined with statistical model checking (SMC), enabling the analysis of black-box parametric models. Building on the PAC approximation, we integrate the DIRECT (DIviding RECTangles) algorithm for derivative-free global optimization over the parameter space. We establish conditional optimality-gap guarantees: under explicit Lipschitz and PAC-good-set assumptions, the difference between the true optimum $f_{\lsf}(\parameters^{*})$ and the value found by DIRECT is bounded by a partition-diameter term and, in the PAC case, an additional approximation-error term. An empirical evaluation on 2997 benchmarks focuses on the new DIRECT-based optimization component. The results show that DIRECT variants solve fewer instances than the scenario optimizer, but on their common successful instances they often return slightly better objective values and usually run faster, while remaining close to the scenario values within the PAC margin.

</details>


### [357] [MEGRAG: Multi-Granular Evidence Graphs for Answer-Aware Multi-Hop RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02195)
*Weidong Bao,Yingying Sun,Jun Yang,Yilin Wang,Zili Wei,Yubin Bao,Fangling Leng,Minghe Yu,Tiancheng Zhang,Ge Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MEGRAG，一种面向多跳问答的答案感知框架，将多跳推理建模为路径结构的多粒度证据图，通过跨粒度索引和自适应证据选择，以中间答案判断原始问题是否已解决，从而避免冗余证据累积和中间检索错误传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有迭代检索增强生成（iRAG）方法在多跳问答中存在两个局限：一是每个推理步骤仅支持单一粒度的证据，难以在信息密度与上下文噪声之间取得平衡；二是现有方法通常在聚合所有中间步骤检索到的证据后才回答原始问题，导致冗余证据和中间检索错误逐渐累积，降低最终答案质量。

Method: MEGRAG包含离线与在线两个阶段。离线阶段，通过跨粒度索引将段落与其句子和抽取的三元组进行链接。在线阶段，为当前查询检索段落并选择对齐的证据，先从紧凑的三元组开始，按需逐步添加句子或段落上下文。系统利用中间答案和先前推理判断初始查询是否已解决；若未解决，则识别缺失信息并构造聚焦的下一个查询；若已解决，则停止检索并返回答案。

Result: 大量实验表明，MEGRAG在多种RAG基线上取得了持续的性能提升。

Conclusion: MEGRAG通过多粒度证据图和答案感知的迭代推理机制，有效缓解了多跳问答中证据粒度单一和错误累积的问题，验证了其在检索增强生成中的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-hop question answering is a fundamental challenge in retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), because deriving an answer requires integrating dispersed evidence. Iterative RAG (iRAG) is widely used for this challenge, but existing methods have two limitations. First, most methods still support each reasoning step with single-granularity evidence, making it difficult to balance information density and contextual noise. Second, existing methods often answer the original question only after aggregating evidence retrieved across intermediate steps, so redundant evidence and intermediate retrieval errors may accumulate and degrade the final answer. To address these limitations, we propose MEGRAG, an answer-aware framework that represents multi-hop reasoning as a path-structured multi-granular evidence graph. Offline, MEGRAG links passages to their sentences and extracted triples through a cross-granularity index. Online, it retrieves passages for the current query and selects aligned evidence, starting with compact triples and adding sentence or passage context as needed. MEGRAG uses the resulting intermediate answer and prior reasoning to decide whether the Initial Query has been resolved. If not, it identifies the missing information and formulates a focused next query; otherwise, it stops retrieval and returns the answer. Extensive experiments demonstrate consistent gains over a diverse set of RAG baselines.

</details>


### [358] [PosterMELD: Multi-Agent Paper-to-Poster Generation for Controllable Design Diversity with Editable Print-Ready Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02218)
*Haojie Hu,Chenhao Dang,Yaojia Liu,Hengrui Kang,Conghui He,Weijia Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PosterMELD是一个基于模板条件的多智能体管线，通过容量感知的槽位引导写作、确定性门控和VLM审阅实现可编辑的科学海报生成，在621篇论文上达到81.3%的打印就绪率，以每请求0.38美元的低成本保留原生可编辑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学海报生成系统存在根本性缺陷：1）仅对完整输出进行评分，隐藏了请求级别的失败；2）直接图像生成缺乏元素级可编辑性；3）编码智能体工作流成本高昂。迫切需要一种既能生成高质量、可编辑海报又具成本效益的方案。

Method: PosterMELD采用模板条件多智能体管线：1）容量感知的槽位在渲染前引导写作；2）确定性门控配合VLM审阅将失败路由到有界修复；3）每个接受的请求导出可编辑的PPTX和PNG产物；4）提供显式设计控制以生成同论文变体。评估采用PRR（几何、可读性、资产完整性、明显事实错误检查）以及冻结VLM分配的条件CHE评分。

Result: 在621篇论文上，PosterMELD达到81.3%的PRR，是P2P的3.4倍、PosterGen的5.2倍；在生成方法中条件CHE最高且支持多个打印就绪输出；原生可编辑性和设计控制以每请求0.38美元的平均成本保留，仅为Codex+Skill成本的3.5%。

Conclusion: PosterMELD证明了在显式暴露请求级失败的同时，可以实现高质量、可编辑且低成本的科学海报生成，打破了现有系统在质量、可编辑性与成本之间的权衡。代码和资源已开源。

Abstract: Scientific poster construction compresses a long multimodal paper into a readable, editable canvas. Existing systems hide request-level failures by scoring only completed outputs; direct image generation is not element-editable, while coding-agent workflows are costly. PosterMELD is a template-conditioned multi-agent pipeline: capacity-aware slots guide writing before rendering, and deterministic gates plus vision-language model (VLM) review route failures to bounded repair. Each accepted request exports editable PowerPoint (PPTX) and Portable Network Graphics (PNG) artifacts; explicit design controls yield same-paper variants. Across 621 papers, Print-Ready Rate (PRR) counts requests passing geometric, readability, asset-integrity, and obvious-factual-error checks, with native editability reported separately. A frozen VLM assigns conditional Craftsmanship-Harmony-Expressiveness (CHE) scores to print-ready outputs. PosterMELD attains 81.3% PRR, 3.4 times P2P's rate and 5.2 times PosterGen's, and the highest conditional CHE among generated methods with multiple print-ready outputs. Native editability and explicit design controls are retained at a mean cost of USD 0.38 per request, 3.5% of Codex+Skill's. Code and resources are available at https://github.com/Shannon4Science/PosterMELD.

</details>


### [359] [Trustworthy AI in Digital Health: A Comprehensive Review of Robustness and Explainability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02238)
*Abdullah Mamun,Shovito Barua Soumma,Hassan Ghasemzadeh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了数字健康领域可信AI的关键维度，重点聚焦鲁棒性和可解释性，构建了涵盖方法、挑战与解决方案的框架，并拓展至LLM时代的可信AI系统开发与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有大量文献涉足可信AI的不同方面，但针对医疗健康场景下鲁棒性与可解释性的综合综述仍然有限，缺乏一个系统的、聚焦于数字健康语境的整合框架。

Method: 文章分为三部分：(1)介绍可信AI的五大支柱（鲁棒性、可解释性、公平性、问责性、隐私性）及技术伦理挑战；(2)探讨重症监护、新生儿健康、代谢健康等领域的应用特定信任考量；(3)综述数据稀缺和分布漂移下的鲁棒性提升技术，以及从特征归因到梯度解释和反事实解释的可解释AI方法。

Result: 文章提供了数字健康中鲁棒性和可解释性贡献的详细讨论，涵盖LLM时代可信AI系统开发及有效性、保真度、多样性等评估指标。

Conclusion: 该综述旨在为研究人员和从业者提供可访问的结构化框架，支持在数字健康领域开发可靠且可解释的AI解决方案。

Abstract: Ensuring trust in AI systems is essential for the safe and ethical integration of machine learning systems into high-stakes domains such as digital health. Key dimensions, including robustness, explainability, fairness, accountability, and privacy, need to be addressed throughout the AI lifecycle, from problem formulation and data collection to model deployment and human interaction. While various contributions address different aspects of trustworthy AI, a focused synthesis on robustness and explainability, especially tailored to the healthcare context, remains limited. This review addresses that need by organizing recent advancements into an accessible framework, highlighting both technical and practical considerations. We present a structured overview of methods, challenges, and solutions, aiming to support researchers and practitioners in developing reliable and explainable AI solutions for digital health. This review article is organized into three main parts. First, we introduce the pillars of trustworthy AI and discuss the technical and ethical challenges, particularly in the context of digital health. Second, we explore application-specific trust considerations across domains such as intensive care, neonatal health, and metabolic health, highlighting how robustness and explainability support trust. Lastly, we present recent advancements in techniques aimed at improving robustness under data scarcity and distributional shifts, as well as explainable AI methods ranging from feature attribution to gradient-based interpretations and counterfactual explanations. This paper is further enriched with detailed discussions of the contributions toward robustness and explainability in digital health, the development of trustworthy AI systems in the era of LLMs, and various evaluation metrics for measuring trust and related parameters such as validity, fidelity, and diversity.

</details>


### [360] [Homebot: A Personal AI Agent for Conversational Home Assistance and Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02254)
*Shengyuan Ye,Yixin Zhang,Han Liang,Liekang Zeng,Jiangsu Du,Mu Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Homebot是一个可本地部署的AI智能体，用于家庭对话式辅助和自动化，通过共享运行时统一处理语音和即时消息请求，将语言模型响应与已注册工具及任务特定技能相结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭场景需要本地可部署、隐私友好的AI助手，能够在语音和即时消息等多种交互方式下提供辅助，同时支持可定制的工具和技能扩展。

Method: Homebot采用共享运行时架构，将通用请求处理与会话所有权分离：消息历史限定在频道和聊天范围内，语音交互由唤醒词激活界定边界。语音模式结合本地唤醒词检测、流式语音识别与合成，并采用显式对话状态协议来处理结束、跟进或继续对话等交互状态。

Result: 通过清晰的频道、工具和技能契约，实现了语音与消息两种交互方式的有效统一，支持实用的家庭场景定制化部署。

Conclusion: Homebot通过模块化的运行时设计和明确的接口契约，在本地部署条件下兼顾了多模态交互支持与可扩展性，为家庭自动化场景提供了一种实用的AI智能体方案。

Abstract: \texttt{Homebot} is a locally deployable AI agent for conversational household assistance and automation. It accepts voice and instant-messaging requests through a shared runtime that combines language-model responses with registered tools and task-specific skills. The design separates common request processing from session ownership: messaging history remains scoped to a channel and chat, whereas voice interaction is bounded by wake-word activation. For hands-free use, \texttt{Homebot} combines local wake-word detection, streaming speech recognition and synthesis, and an explicit dialogue-state protocol for ending, following up, or continuing a conversation. Clear channel, tool, and skill contracts support practical customization for household use.

</details>


### [361] [Self-Certification of Representation Adequacy: Sequential Certification at Minimum Task Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02267)
*Zijie Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个四层理论框架，用于解决基于压缩历史表征的智能体所面临的结构性风险——当表征混淆了不同最优动作的历史时，任何基于该表征的规则都会产生不可约的回合损失。论文从静态层（决策理论充分性的贝叶斯风险分组恒等式与一次性外部验证）、序贯层（认证作为最优停止问题，提出Covering LP定义环境级认证复杂度常数，给出Certification Track-and-Stop策略）、以及边界层（显式核切换示例并识别开放性定理）三个层面系统发展表征充分性的自认证理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于压缩历史表征行动的智能体存在结构性风险：当表征将具有不同最优动作的历史混为一谈时，任何可测量的规则都无法避免每回合的固有损失，且智能体可能无法从其自身转录中检测到这一问题。因此需要形式化地理解何种条件下、以何种代价可以自我认证表征的充分性。

Method: 论文构建了四层理论：1）静态层通过贝叶斯风险分组恒等式定义决策理论充分性，并以外部的精确全变差阈值给一次性验证定价；2）序贯层将认证形式化为以任务损失为货币的最优停止问题，通过covering线性规划定义环境级认证复杂度常数，证明所有delta-正确策略的信息-任务损失下界，并提出Certification Track-and-Stop策略使之渐近匹配；3）边界层通过显式核切换示例给出说明，并识别了覆盖策略切换或表征修复所需的开放性定理；主要定理的证明完整给出在附录中。

Result: 给出了表征充分性的统一形式化框架，确立了静态与序贯两层认证的复杂度特征：序贯层导出了环境级认证复杂度常数及所有delta-正确策略的严格下界，同时构造了一个渐近最优的Certification Track-and-Stop策略。边界层通过核切换示例揭示了固定核保证不能泛化到表征修订场景。

Conclusion: 论文建立了"表征充分性自认证"的完整理论骨架：静态与序贯层提供了可操作的复杂度刻画与最优策略，而边界层则诚实地界定了当前理论的适用范围——不覆盖策略切换或表征修复场景，并将此作为开放问题留待后继研究。

Abstract: Agents that act on a compressed representation of their history face a structural risk: if the representation aliases histories with different optimal actions, no rule measurable with respect to the representation can avoid an irreducible per-round loss, and the agent may be unable to detect this from its own transcript. This paper develops a four-layer theory of self-certification of representation adequacy. The static layer defines decision-theoretic adequacy through a Bayes-risk grouping identity and prices a one-shot external verification by an exact total-variation threshold. The sequential layer poses certification as an optimal-stopping problem in the currency of task loss: we define an environment-wise certification complexity constant through a covering linear program, prove an information-task-loss lower bound for every delta-correct strategy, and give a Certification Track-and-Stop policy whose cost matches the bound asymptotically. A final boundary layer gives an explicit kernel-switching example and identifies the open theorem needed to cover policy switching or representation repair; it does not claim that the fixed-kernel guarantees extend to representation revision. The proofs of the two main theorems are given in full in the appendices.

</details>


### [362] [Harness-R1: Learning to Edit Executable Runtime Harnesses from Agent Failure Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02276)
*Shuai Shao,Kangning Zhang,Qingyao Li,Shijian Wang,Hao Wang,Wenxiang Jiao,Yuan Lu,Yi Guo,Weiwen Liu,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Harness-R1，首次让大语言模型代理的运行系统（harness）编辑成为一种学习能力。通过在线强化学习对专门的harness工程师进行后训练，基于目标代理的失败轨迹生成可执行的补丁，并在冻结目标代理上重新运行以获取奖励信号。在WebShop、ALFWorld和DBBench上，Harness-R1将Qwen3.5-9B的成功率从44.3%提升至53.6%；在目标代理微调后，进一步从59.2%提升至64.2%，证明了harness工程师与目标代理协同进化的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的代理在部署过程中不断积累交互轨迹，但行为通常保持固定。除了更新模型权重，这些轨迹还可以改进代理的harness（即构造上下文、协调工具、验证动作、恢复执行的部分）。现有方法中没有让harness的编辑成为一种可学习的、以实际任务成功为优化目标的能力，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: Harness-R1方法包含：(1) 一个专门的9B harness工程师模型，将目标代理的失败批处理转换为可执行的补丁；(2) 使用在线强化学习（组相对策略优化，GRPO）后训练该工程师，其优化目标是编辑后任务的实际成功率；(3) 冻结目标代理不变，仅更新工程师，通过重新运行同一批次的目标代理获得结果奖励；(4) 使用冷启动监督微调初始化编辑策略，再进行在线训练。

Result: 在WebShop、ALFWorld和DBBench三个基准上，Harness-R1将vanilla Qwen3.5-9B的成功率从44.3%提升至53.6%（+9.3个百分点）；对目标代理直接微调后，目标特定的工程师将平均成功率从59.2%进一步提升至64.2%（+5.0个百分点）。这些增益在目标微调前后均保持。

Conclusion: Harness-R1证明了基于失败条件、生命周期级运行的编辑可以成为学习能力，且由于在目标代理微调前后增益均成立，该方法指向harness工程师与目标代理协同进化的新方向，即两者可以相互迭代改进而非仅各自独立优化。

Abstract: Agents built around large language models continually accumulate interaction trajectories during deployment, yet their behavior typically remains fixed. Beyond updating model weights, these trajectories can improve the agent harness that constructs context, mediates tools, validates actions, and recovers execution. We introduce Harness-R1, the first method, to our knowledge, that makes failure-conditioned, lifecycle-wide editing of an existing executable runtime a learned capability. It post-trains a dedicated harness engineer with online reinforcement learning so that its edits are optimized for the realized task success they produce, rather than proposed by a fixed editor. A separate 9B engineer converts batches of target-agent failures into validated executable patches; fresh same-batch reruns of the frozen target provide outcome rewards, so training updates only the engineer. Cold-start supervised fine-tuning initializes this editing policy, which is then trained online with group-relative policy optimization. Across WebShop, ALFWorld, and DBBench, Harness-R1 raises vanilla Qwen3.5-9B success from 44.3% to 53.6% (+9.3 percentage points). After direct target-agent fine-tuning, a target-specific engineer raises the average further from 59.2% to 64.2% (+5.0 points); because these gains hold both before and after fine-tuning the target, Harness-R1 points toward co-evolving the harness engineer and the target agent.

</details>


### [363] [SKT: Skill-Use Training at Scale via Verified Synthetic Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02287)
*Zelin Tan,Yiqun Zhang,Hao Li,Zhiyao Cui,Hejia Geng,Shao Zhang,Hangfan Zhang,Yang Chen,Xiaosong Wang,Lilong Wang,Zhenfei Yin,Shuyue Hu,Chen Zhang,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SKT，一个基于验证的数据合成管线，从大量智能体技能中构建技能驱动的任务和可执行轨迹，生成大规模高质量训练数据；同时构建SkillEval基准来评估技能使用能力。实验表明基于SKT生成的轨迹进行监督微调能持续提升模型的技能使用性能，且验证数据合成是一种可扩展的有效方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为语言模型智能体提供的技能(skills)本身并不能保证模型能够有效地识别、应用和协调这些技能。当前模型在技能使用能力上存在不足，缺乏用于训练和评估技能使用能力的高质量数据与基准。

Method: SKT是一种验证驱动的数据合成管线：(1) 选择合适的单技能和多技能配置；(2) 通过基于规则和基于智能体的验证方法合成任务，并附带反馈引导的修复机制；(3) 仅保留那些实质性使用了每个所需技能的成功轨迹。基于相同管线和不重叠的测试池，构建了SkillEval，一个留出的可执行技能使用评估基准。

Result: 利用2,000个公开技能，SKT生成了4,000个任务包和27,164条已验证轨迹。跨多个模型、基准和智能体框架的实验表明，在SKT生成的轨迹上进行监督微调能持续提升技能使用性能。验证消融、跨框架评估和扩展实验进一步证明这些增益依赖于高质量监督，可超越单一智能体界面，并随技能覆盖范围扩大而增加。

Conclusion: 验证驱动的数据合成是提升语言模型智能体技能使用能力的有效且可扩展的方法，为技能使用训练提供了高质量的数据基础和评估基准。

Abstract: Agent skills have become an important mechanism for equipping language-model agents with reusable procedural knowledge. However, providing skills alone does not guarantee that current models can effectively identify, apply, and coordinate them. To improve skill-use capabilities, we introduce SKT, a verified data synthesis pipeline that constructs skill-grounded tasks and executable trajectories from large collections of agent skills. SKT selects suitable single-skill and multi-skill configurations, synthesizes tasks through rule-based and agent-based verification with feedback-guided repair, and retains only successful trajectories that substantially use every required skill. Using 2,000 public skills, SKT produces 4,000 task packages and 27,164 verified trajectories. Based on the same pipeline and a disjoint test pool, we further construct SkillEval, a held-out executable benchmark for evaluating skill use. Experiments across diverse models, benchmarks, and agent harnesses show that supervised fine-tuning on SKT-generated trajectories consistently improves skill-use performance. Verification ablations, cross-harness evaluation, and scaling experiments further demonstrate that these gains depend on high-quality supervision, extend beyond a single agent interface, and increase with broader skill coverage. Together, these results establish verified data synthesis as an effective and scalable approach for skill-use training.

</details>


### [364] [Shared Prefixes, Better Credit: Adaptive Routing for Multi-Agent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02291)
*Yiqing Liu,Zihao Wang,Hantao Yao,Wu Liu,Yongdong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TreeCredit，一种共享前缀信用分配框架，通过状态匹配的下游比较来估计多智能体推理中每个操作符的效用，训练轻量级状态路由器以动态选择下一操作符，在六个推理基准上以更低的推理成本获得相当的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应多智能体推理（MAR）方法通常依赖查询级标签或轨迹级回报来学习路由决策，这种粗粒度监督无法准确估计多步协作中单个操作符在特定状态条件下的效用，导致路由决策不精确。

Method: TreeCredit构建共享前缀协作树：从相同中间状态扩展候选操作符，基于终端正确性和累计额外代价为每个状态-操作符对分配正确性优先的后缀信用；这些结构化信用被转化为状态局部操作符偏好，用于训练轻量级成对状态路由器，在推理时动态选择下一个可执行操作符。

Result: 在六个推理基准上的实验表明，TreeCredit在精度上略有提升，同时大幅降低推理成本，相比代表性MAR方法实现了更好的精度-成本权衡。

Conclusion: 通过共享前缀信用分配实现更细粒度的操作符效用估计，TreeCredit能够在保持甚至略微提升推理准确率的同时显著降低推理开销，验证了状态级信用分配在自适应多智能体推理中的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-agent reasoning (MAR) improves reasoning reliability through iterative solution exchange and refinement. Existing adaptive MAR methods typically learn routing decisions from query-level labels or trajectory-level returns, but such coarse supervision cannot accurately estimate the state-conditioned utility of individual operators in multi-step collaboration. We propose TreeCredit, a shared-prefix credit assignment framework for efficient adaptive MAR. Its core insight is to estimate operator utility through state-matched downstream comparisons, rather than directly attributing trajectory-level outcomes to preceding decisions. TreeCredit constructs shared-prefix collaboration trees by expanding candidate operators from the same intermediate state and assigns each state--operator pair a correctness-prioritized suffix credit based on the terminal correctness and cumulative additional cost of its complete continuation. These structured credits are converted into state-local operator preferences to train a lightweight pairwise state router, which dynamically selects the next admissible operator during inference. Experiments on six reasoning benchmarks show that TreeCredit modestly improves accuracy while substantially reducing inference cost, achieving a better accuracy--cost trade-off than representative MAR methods.

</details>


### [365] [MechGeo: Autoformalizing and Proving Euclidean Geometry in Lean 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02295)
*Hao Shen,Junyu Guo,Tian Cui,Yuxuan Xiao,Lihong Zhi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MechGeo是一个面向欧几里得几何的Mathlib原生智能体框架，联合解决忠实自动形式化与认证证明构造问题。它通过GeoFormalizer将非形式化问题表示为GeoIR并确定性地翻译为Lean 4代码，通过GeoProver构造几何证明计划并选择性代数化子目标。在43道IMO几何题中，GeoFormalizer生成的形式化命题有29道被GeoProver成功证明；在14道LEAP的Lean-IMO-Bench几何题中，MechGeo首次证明12道，正式反驳剩余2道，并证明了修正后的命题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧几里得几何的自动形式化和认证证明构造是两个相互关联的挑战：非形式化问题翻译为Lean等证明助手代码时容易产生语义偏差，且几何推理与代数计算需要统一在可信的内核验证体系下。现有方法在自动形式化保真度和证明通过率上均有限，尤其对直接翻译能力较弱的模型。

Method: MechGeo包含两个核心组件：GeoFormalizer使用GeoIR中间表示承载非形式化问题，确定性地将其翻译为Lean 4代码，并通过结构诊断和语义评估迭代修复候选命题；GeoProver构造几何证明计划、推导中间引理，并通过Lean库验证后的方式选择性地对合适子目标进行代数化。Singular或SymPy可生成代数证书，但所有证明和反例最终都由Lean内核检查。

Result: 在7种LLM后端上的实验显示，自动形式化能力有显著提升，尤其对直接翻译能力较弱的模型。在43道历史IMO几何题中，29道被证明；其余14道构造了Lean验证的反例并在专家修正后全部证明。在LEAP的Lean-IMO-Bench的14道几何命题中，12道首次被证明，2道被正式反驳，修正后均获证明。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了反例引导诊断、几何推理和认证符号计算作为可信赖形式化几何的实用基础，并提供了据作者所知最大规模的可自动生成、经内核验证的IMO几何题Lean证明集。

Abstract: We present MechGeo, a Mathlib native agentic framework that jointly addresses faithful autoformalization and certified proof construction for Euclidean geometry. In this framework, GeoFormalizer represents informal problems in GeoIR, deterministically translates them into Lean 4, and iteratively repairs candidate statements using structural diagnostics and semantic evaluation. GeoProver constructs geometric proof plans, derives intermediate lemmas, and selectively algebraizes suitable subgoals through a library verified in Lean. Singular or SymPy may generate algebraic certificates, but all resulting proofs and counterexamples are checked by Lean's kernel. Experiments across seven LLM backbones show substantial improvements in autoformalization, particularly for models with weaker direct translation performance. On 43 historical IMO geometry problems, GeoFormalizer generates formal statements that GeoProver proves in 29 cases; for the remaining 14, it constructs counterexamples verified in Lean and proves all repaired statements after expert correction. Together with IMO 2026 Problem 2, this yields, to the best of our knowledge, the largest reported collection of automated, kernel-checked Lean proofs for IMO geometry problems. On the 14 geometry statements in LEAP's Lean-IMO-Bench, MechGeo proves 12 for the first time, formally refutes the remaining two, and proves both repaired statements. These results establish counterexample guided diagnosis, geometric reasoning, and certified symbolic computation as a practical foundation for trustworthy formal geometry.

</details>


### [366] [Trajectories That Segment Themselves: Agent-Declared Boundaries as a Training Unit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02302)
*Jingxi Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"收集时语义自分割"方法，让编码智能体在轨迹生成过程中通过可证伪的因果假设主动暴露自身的语义边界，从而产生变长的语义阶段。该方法无需里程碑词汇、金补丁、环境回放或教师logits，即可生成可审计的监督信号，并将一条轨迹转化为四个监督目标。作者进一步验证了删除声明后分割的鲁棒性，以及下游DPO微调的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视界编码智能体的轨迹与可用的信用分配单元之间存在严重不匹配：单个动作没有稳定的价值，一条轨迹的标签将富有成效的探索与废弃方向混为一体，而固定窗口的截断又切在日志记录机制失效的位置。作者希望以一种更合理的语义粒度来划分轨迹，以获得更有效的监督信号。

Method: 核心方法是"收集时语义自分割"（collection-time semantic self-segmentation）：声明式契约（declarative contract）要求正在行动的智能体在轨迹生成过程中自行暴露其决策边界，并以可证伪的因果假设（falsifiable causal hypotheses）形式命名其猜想。由于智能体以名称命名其猜想，审查者可按名称进行否定，从而制造"错误原因→纠正"的转换。一次收集可产生四种监督目标，包括来自失败区域的审计监督。作者还进行了删除声明（deletion）实验来验证分割的鲁棒性，包括假设归因任务和边界放置任务。

Result: 在假设归因任务中，给定切分点但不给假设，模型将动作块归因到其控制假设的正确率是随机的两倍以上，优于等长块（配对符号检验p=0.0002），且通过词语控制检验、在标签置换下崩溃。在边界放置任务中，代码盲标注者在40个边界中匹配24个（随机11.5个），而机械性测试事件规则在严格到宽松的参数扫描两端均无法超过随机。下游DPO中，2,551对阶段边界对在91个对抗性保留项上未改变任何决策，而60个匹配构造项中有4个发生改变（全部由错转对），两个对照未改变任何项。

Conclusion: 语义分割产生的段是连贯的且不能通过廉价手段复现。下游实验中，分割边界的价值已得到初步验证，但改变变量数量的瓶颈已不再是边界的质量，而是语料库的多样性——需要用更多生成器来丰富训练数据。

Abstract: Long-horizon coding-agent trajectories are poorly matched to the credit units available to train on: a single action has no stable value, an episode label merges productive exploration with abandoned directions, and a fixed window cuts where the logging mechanics fall. We introduce collection-time semantic self-segmentation, in which a declarative contract has the acting agent expose its own boundaries while the trajectory is generated. Instantiated with falsifiable causal hypotheses, successive adoptions expose variable-length semantic phases, and no milestone vocabulary, gold patch, environment replay, teacher logits, or retrospective segmenter places a boundary. Because the agent names its conjecture, a reviewer can negate it by name, which lets our protocol manufacture wrong-cause-then-correction transitions that recorded work rarely contains; one collection then yields four supervised targets, including audit supervision from exactly the failed regions an episode label discards. We then ask what survives deleting the declaration. Given the cut points but not the hypothesis, a model attributes action blocks to their governing hypothesis at over twice chance, beating equal-length blocks over the same trajectories (paired sign test $p = 0.0002$), surviving a lexical control and collapsing under label permutation. Asked instead to place boundaries, a code-blind annotator matches 24 of 40 where random placement matches 11.5, while a mechanical test-event rule beats chance at neither end of a strict-to-permissive sweep. The segments are therefore coherent and not cheaply reproducible. Downstream, DPO on 2,551 phase-boundary pairs changes no decision on 91 adversarial held-out items, while four of 60 change on matched-construction items, all wrong to right, where two controls change none: with 1,825 pairs from one generator, the variable to vary next is corpus diversity, not the boundary.

</details>


### [367] [Hard Constraints, Smooth Gradients: Learning Feasible Inventory Policies via Differentiable Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02343)
*Patrick Helm,Jan-Niklas Doerr,Joren Gijsbrechts,Stefan Minner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将可微凸优化模块嵌入深度强化学习策略的方法，通过神经网络提出连续动作目标、二次规划投影到松弛可行集、对偶信息整数映射恢复整数性并保持可行性，从而在具有大规模组合动作空间和相互依赖硬约束的序贯决策问题中实现端到端训练。在多级生产-库存规划中，该方法在小规模实例上最优性缺口低于1%，在更大网络中优于现有基准策略最高9.75%，并在ASML工业案例中平均成本降低3.22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合整数线性规划（MILP）能灵活处理相互依赖的约束但随机环境下扩展性差；深度强化学习（DRL）可扩展但现有方法要么通过惩罚而非强制约束，要么依赖在约束交互时会失效的可行性机制。两者之间存在缺口，需要一种既能像MILP一样灵活处理硬约束、又具有DRL可扩展性的方法。

Method: 将可微凸优化模块嵌入策略内部：(1) 神经网络提出连续动作目标；(2) 二次规划（QP）将动作投影到松弛可行集；(3) 对偶信息引导的整数映射恢复整数性同时保持可行性。在可微模拟器下，利用路径梯度（pathwise gradients）从采样轨迹端到端训练策略。

Result: 该方法的可行性强制执行相对于精确整数投影具有有界误差，并确保整个可行动作空间可达。在小规模实例上平均最优性缺口低于1%；在更大网络中优于最先进的echelon base-stock策略最高9.75%，优于滚动时域多阶段随机规划至少7.7%。在ASML工业规模案例中，相对于最佳已知基准策略平均成本降低3.22%，在产能紧张和高需求波动的系统中节省最大。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，DRL可以在具有相互依赖硬约束的序贯决策问题中产生经济上显著的收益。所提出的可行性强制执行机制兼具MILP的约束处理灵活性和DRL的可扩展性，为实践中广泛存在的复杂约束序贯决策问题提供了一种有效解决方案。

Abstract: Many operational problems are constrained sequential decision processes with large, combinatorial action spaces and interdependent feasibility constraints. Mixed-integer linear programs (MILPs) handle such constraints flexibly but scale poorly in stochastic environments. Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) promises scalable decision rules, but existing methods either penalize constraints rather than enforce them, or rely on feasibility mechanisms that break down once constraints interact. We bridge this gap by embedding a differentiable convex optimization module inside the policy: a neural network proposes continuous action targets, a quadratic program projects them onto the relaxed feasible set, and a dual-informed integer mapping restores integrality while preserving feasibility. Given a differentiable simulator, the policy trains end to end from sampled trajectories using pathwise gradients, while handling hard constraints with similar flexibility to MILPs. We show that our feasibility enforcement has bounded error relative to an exact integer projection and ensures the entire feasible action space is reachable. We apply the method to multi-echelon production-inventory planning under shared resource and material constraints. Our policy attains an average optimality gap below 1% on small instances. It further outperforms state-of-the-art echelon base-stock policies by up to 9.75% and a rolling-horizon multi-stage stochastic program by at least 7.7% in larger networks. On an industry-scale case study from ASML, it reduces average cost by up to 3.22% relative to the best-known benchmark policy. The savings are largest where planning is hardest: in tightly capacitated systems with high demand variability. More broadly, our work shows that DRL can deliver economically significant savings in sequential decision problems with interdependent hard constraints, which are widespread in practice.

</details>


### [368] [Mamba with Hierarchical Memory: Solving Representation Bottleneck in Long Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02347)
*Qinwen Wang,Jieping Luo,Aoxiang Qin,Ruoyu Zhao,Jianxiong Tang,Wei Zhang,Zhichao Lu,Luziwei Leng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HMM提出了一种层次化记忆增强的Mamba架构，通过工作记忆和持久长期记忆的协同，有效克服了循环线性注意力模型在长序列建模上的表示瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环线性注意力模型（如Mamba）虽在长序列建模中高效，但其固定容量的循环状态限制了长序列的理解能力，存在表示瓶颈。

Method: 在预训练Mamba骨干之上，集成轻量级工作记忆以从骨干隐藏状态中提取慢速段落级语义（PLS），再将PLS压缩为持久长期记忆用于任务相关检索，实现层次化语义处理。

Result: 在Passkey Retrieval和LongBench-E任务上，相较强Mamba基线模型，检索成功率提升34.3-37.1%，推理准确率提升1.6-14.2%，且仅增加2%参数量并需极小的训练开销。

Conclusion: 层次化记忆机制能有效突破RLA的容量限制，并使模型具备跨任务泛化能力，这是其他长上下文增强Mamba变体所不具备的。

Abstract: Recurrent linear attention models (RLAs) such as Mamba offer efficient linear-time sequence modeling as an alternative to Transformers, yet their fixed-capacity recurrent states limit long-sequence modeling. Drawing inspiration from hierarchical human memory, we propose Hierarchical Memory Mamba (HMM) to address this limitation. Building upon a pre-trained Mamba backbone, HMM integrates a lightweight working memory that extracts slow paragraph-level semantics (PLS) from the fast sensory memory embedded in the backbone's hidden states. The PLS is subsequently compressed into persistent long-term memory for task-relevant retrieval. The hierarchical processing of semantic information overcomes the representation bottleneck of RLAs and endows HMM cross-task generalization through parametric learning, which is not observed in other long-context enhanced Mamba variants. Evaluations on Passkey Retrieval and LongBench-E tasks demonstrate that HMM improves retrieval success by 34.3--37.1% and reasoning accuracy by 1.6--14.2% over strong Mamba-based models, while adding only 2% extra parameters and with minimal training overhead.

</details>


### [369] [KC-Agent: A Dual-Process Cognitive Architecture for Efficient ML Model Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02351)
*Gusseppe Bravo-Rocca,Jordi Guitart,Ajay Dholakia,David Ellison,Puneet Jain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KC-Agent是一种双过程认知架构的自动化ML模型改进系统，结合System 1（快速模式识别）和System 2（谨慎增量更新），通过结构化记忆系统和原子变更原则实现对数据漂移场景下的高效模型更新，在五个数据集上达到76.8%准确率和13.2s执行时间，优于多种基线认知架构，并通过知识整合机制实现91%的速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的机器学习系统面临数据漂移的严峻挑战，需要持续更新模型以维持性能。现有方法缺乏有效的认知启发性架构，无法在保证可靠性的同时实现高效的模式识别与增量更新。

Method: 提出KC-Agent双过程认知架构：(1) System 1负责快速模式识别，通过结构化记忆系统复用System 2先前发现的成功解决方案；(2) System 2负责谨慎的增量更新，并采用原子变更原则和回滚机制确保生产环境中的可靠、可验证更新。

Result: 在五个数据集（含NASA涡扇真实时间退化数据和合成漂移数据集）上取得76.8%准确率和13.2s执行时间，优于CodeAct(+2.4%)、Tree of Thoughts(+3.6%)、ReAct(+8.0%)和Reflexion(+8.9%)；LLM专家组共识评估的Smartness得分8.33/10；知识整合机制比慢速变体实现91%加速并保持更高准确率。

Conclusion: KC-Agent展示了认知启发式自动ML改进系统在理论和实践上的可行性，能够有效处理复杂真实世界数据漂移场景，兼顾效率与可靠性。

Abstract: Data drift poses significant challenges for machine learning systems in production, requiring continuous model updates to maintain performance. We present KC-Agent, a dual-process cognitive architecture for automated ML model improvement that combines fast pattern recognition (System 1) with deliberate incremental updates (System 2). Our approach implements structured memory systems enabling System 1 to leverage successful solutions previously discovered by System 2, achieving efficient pattern-based responses without costly re-computation. KC-Agent incorporates atomic change principles and rollback capabilities to ensure reliable, verifiable updates in production environments. We evaluate our method on five datasets including real-world NASA turbofan data with authentic temporal degradation and synthetic datasets with controlled drift scenarios. KC-Agent achieves state-of-the-art performance (76.8% accuracy) while maintaining optimal efficiency (13.2s execution time), outperforming established cognitive architectures: CodeAct (+2.4%), Tree of Thoughts (+3.6%), ReAct (+8.0%), and Reflexion (+8.9%). Consensus evaluation by a panel of state-of-the-art LLMs confirms superior strategic efficacy (8.33/10 Smartness score), significantly outperforming baseline agents. The knowledge consolidation mechanism delivers 91% speedup over the slow variant while maintaining higher accuracy. Our approach demonstrates both theoretical foundations and practical viability for cognitive-inspired automated ML improvement systems capable of handling complex real-world data drift scenarios.

</details>


### [370] [SkillTrace: Traversing a Query-Skill Graph for Composable LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02356)
*Yue Yao,Shengyuan Wang,Xin Chen,Minke Zhang,Jia He,Bingjun Luo,Tom Gedeon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillTrace是一个基于三层图结构的技能检索框架，通过组合关系、语义相似度和依赖传播来解决大语言模型智能体的技能组合检索问题，在SkillsBench和ALFWorld上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体需要从技能库中组合可复用的技能来求解复杂任务，但关键挑战不仅是检索单个相关技能，而是要识别完整且可执行的技能组合。现有方法无法有效建模技能之间的组合关系与依赖。

Method: SkillTrace构建一个三层图结构：(1) 技能查询之间的组合关系层；(2) 查询与技能库中候选之间的相似度层；(3) 选中候选之间的依赖关系层。系统将用户查询组织成语义层次结构，进行技能查询与候选的匹配，并在技能依赖图上进行传播以确定完整可执行的技能组合。

Result: 在SkillsBench上达到53.17%的成功率，在ALFWorld上达到91.43%的成功率，均达到当前最优水平。同时在不同骨干语言模型上均取得一致的性能提升，展示了图基技能检索方法的泛化性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SkillTrace通过三层图结构建模组合关系、语义相似度和依赖传播，有效解决了复杂任务下的技能组合检索问题，验证了图方法在技能检索中的通用性与可靠性。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly solve complex tasks by composing reusable skills from a library. To address this, the key challenge is not merely to retrieve individually relevant skills, but to identify a complete and executable skill composition. In this paper, we argue that this problem can be solved in a graph with three levels: compositional relations among skill queries, similarity between queries and candidates in the skill library, and the dependencies among the selected candidates. We introduce SkillTrace, which organizes the user query into a semantic hierarchy, matches skill queries and candidates, and propagates over the skill dependencies. Experiments on SkillsBench and ALFWorld demonstrate that SkillTrace achieves state-of-the-art performance, reaching a success rate of 53.17% on SkillsBench and 91.43% on ALFWorld. SkillTrace also delivers consistent improvements across different backbone language models, demonstrating the generality and robustness of graph-based skill retrieval.

</details>


### [371] [Faster-WAM: Do World Action Models Need Deep Action Modules?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02365)
*Liheng Ma,Rui Heng Yang,Zhanguang Zhang,Mateo Clemente,Ziwen Hu,Tongtong Cao,Yingxue Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Dock of Transformer (DoT)设计原则，将预训练视频Transformer作为表示枢纽，通过对接接口连接轻量输出头，并基于此提出Faster-WAM——在30层视频骨干上对接单层动作头，在LIBERO和RoboTwin 2.0上取得竞争性表现，推理延迟仅66.5ms，比Fast-WAM快3.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAMs）采用共享骨干或Mixture-of-Transformers设计，将动作模块深度与视频骨干绑定，导致计算开销大、推理延迟高。需要一种灵活的架构设计来解耦动作模块深度与视频骨干深度，降低推理延迟。

Method: 提出DoT设计原则：将预训练视频Transformer作为表示枢纽（representation hub），通过对接接口连接轻量输出头。对接接口融合来自视频骨干所有层的keys和values，并应用RoPE重对齐。基于此原则提出Faster-WAM实例化，将单层动作头对接至30层视频骨干。

Result: Faster-WAM无需额外的具身预训练，在LIBERO和RoboTwin 2.0上达到竞争性性能，在LIBERO-Plus上展现强分布外泛化能力；在受控对比中取得最低端到端延迟，每次推理仅需66.5ms，相比Fast-WAM实现3.2倍加速。

Conclusion: 视频中心的DoT架构支持灵活的任务特定头设计，同时实现低推理延迟、强动作预测性能和稳健的分布外泛化，验证了视频世界模型作为通用表示枢纽的有效性。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) couple robot action prediction with video world models. Existing WAMs with shared-backbone and Mixture-of-Transformers designs generally tie the depth of the action module to that of the video backbone, resulting in substantial computational overhead and high inference latency. To address this limitation, we introduce Dock of Transformer (DoT), a video-centric design principle that treats a pretrained video Transformer as a representation hub and connects lightweight output-heads through docking interfaces. This enables flexible output-head design while providing direct access to representations from all layers of the backbone. We then introduce \textbf{Faster-WAM}, an instantiation of DoT for WAMs, which docks a single-layer action head onto a 30-layer video backbone. The docking interface fuses keys and values from all video layers and applies RoPE realignment. Without additional embodied pretraining, Faster-WAM achieves competitive performance on LIBERO and RoboTwin 2.0 while demonstrating strong out-of-distribution generalization on LIBERO-Plus. Faster-WAM also achieves the lowest end-to-end latency in our controlled comparison, requiring only 66.5 ms per inference --- a \(3.2\times\) speedup over Fast-WAM. Overall, these results demonstrate that the video-centric DoT architecture supports flexible task-specific head design while delivering low inference latency, strong action-prediction performance, and robust generalization.

</details>


### [372] [Chess on Ice: Curling Tactical Decision-Making via Backward Induction and Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02379)
*Patrick Oberlin,Matteo Cederle,Aren Karapetyan,Saverio Bolognani,Gian Antonio Susto,Florian Dörfler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个强化学习框架来定量评估和比较冰壶比赛中的战术选项。该框架采用深度确定性策略梯度（DDPG）算法，适配了冰壶游戏的有限时域结构，解决了连续状态/动作空间、动作结果随机性和状态转移对扰动高度敏感等建模挑战。实验表明，在简化四石变体上，学习得到的智能体无需任何人工标注数据即可匹配手工专家启发式策略的性能，并且学习到的critic还能为整个连续动作空间提供密集的价值估计，用于赛后表现分析和运动员备赛决策支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冰壶常被称为"冰上国际象棋"，其决策过程具有很高的战术复杂性，但相比国际象棋，冰壶在机器学习领域的研究非常不足，以往工作主要局限于统计方法。作者希望通过强化学习框架定量评估和比较冰壶中的战术选项，填补这一空白。

Method: 采用深度确定性策略梯度（DDPG）actor-critic算法，并针对冰壶游戏的有限时域结构进行适配。该框架处理了三个主要建模挑战：连续状态和动作空间、反映选手技能差异的随机动作结果、以及状态转移对执行动作小扰动的高度敏感性。训练过程完全自监督，不需要任何人工标注数据。

Result: 在简化的四石变体上，学习到的智能体完全匹配手工专家启发式策略的性能（在该启发式接近最优的机制下），这种对等关系通过与变体固有锤子优势的量化对比得到验证。此外，学习到的critic能为整个连续动作空间提供密集的价值估计，使战术替代方案的定量比较成为可能。

Conclusion: 强化学习能够在不依赖人类标注数据的情况下自主获取有效的冰壶策略，且该方法超越了单一策略输出——学习到的critic可作为定量战术分析工具，服务于赛后表现分析和运动员备赛期间的决策支持等应用场景。

Abstract: Curling is often referred to as "Chess on Ice", owing to the tactical complexity of its decision-making process. Yet unlike chess, curling remains largely underexplored from a machine learning perspective, with prior work confined mainly to statistical approaches. We propose a reinforcement learning framework capable of quantitatively evaluating and comparing tactical options in curling. The game poses several modeling challenges: continuous state and action spaces, stochastic action outcomes reflecting player skill variability, and state transitions that are highly sensitive to small perturbations in the executed action. To address them, we employ the Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient actor-critic algorithm, adapted to exploit the finite-horizon structure of the game. Our experiments show that effective curling strategies can be acquired in a fully self-supervised manner, without any human-annotated data: on a reduced four-rock variant, the learned agent matches a hand-crafted expert heuristic in a regime where that heuristic is close to optimal, a parity we quantify against the intrinsic hammer advantage of the variant. Beyond the resulting policy, the learned critic provides a dense value estimate over the entire continuous action space, enabling the quantitative comparison of tactical alternatives for applications such as post-game performance analysis and decision support during athlete preparation.

</details>


### [373] [Cooperative Coevolution for Resource-Constrained Agentic LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02391)
*Zhiyuan Wang,Shengcai Liu,Jiahao Wu,Ning Lu,Hui Ouyang,Shaofeng Zhang,Haoze Lv,Ke Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoPES是一种协作参数子空间进化策略方法，将全参数空间分解为低维子空间并行搜索，在资源受限的GPU环境下实现比标准ES更好的训练效率和性能，仅用GRPO八分之一的GPU内存即恢复了92%的验证准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用型LLM智能体产生长多轮轨迹，使基于梯度的后训练内存密集；进化策略虽内存高效但GPU小时需求过高，在仅有少量GPU的资源受限场景下训练时间过长，亟需一种兼顾内存效率和训练时间的优化方法。

Method: 提出协作参数子空间进化策略CoPES，采用协作协同进化算法将全参数空间分解为低维子空间，并在子空间上进行协作搜索以提高优化效率。该方法无需反向传播即可进行全参数后训练。

Result: 在Qwen3.5-4B工具使用智能体的数学任务后训练中，在GRPO最佳验证检查点的GPU小时预算内，CoPES恢复了GRPO验证准确率增益的92%（标准ES仅为67%），理论GPU内存需求不足全参数GRPO的八分之一；在五个基准的所有pass@k指标上一致优于标准ES和基于LoRA的GRPO，并在问答任务上进一步验证了优势。

Conclusion: CoPES在资源受限条件下实现了内存需求与训练时间之间更好的权衡，为智能体化LLM后训练提供了一种高效的替代方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Tool-using large language model (LLM) agents produce long, multi-turn trajectories, making gradient-based post-training memory-intensive. Evolution strategies (ES) enable memory-efficient full-parameter post-training without backpropagation and can eventually match the performance of gradient-based reinforcement learning (RL). However, resource-constrained settings typically offer only a few GPUs, so the high GPU-hour requirements of ES translate into prohibitively long training times. To address this, we introduce Cooperative Parameter-subspace Evolution Strategy (CoPES), a cooperative coevolutionary method that decomposes the full parameter space into lower-dimensional subspaces and searches over them cooperatively to improve optimization efficiency. We post-train a Qwen3.5-4B tool-using agent for the math task and evaluate it on five benchmarks of varying difficulty. Under the GPU-hour budget of full-parameter GRPO's best validation checkpoint, CoPES recovers 92% of GRPO's validation-accuracy gain, versus 67% for standard ES, while its theoretical GPU memory requirement is less than one-eighth that of full-parameter GRPO. It consistently outperforms standard ES and LoRA-based GRPO on all evaluated pass@k metrics across the five benchmarks. Additional experiments further show the advantage of CoPES on the question-answering task. These results demonstrate an improved trade-off between memory requirements and training time for agentic LLM post-training under resource constraints. The code is open-sourced in https://github.com/MetaronWang/CoPES

</details>


### [374] [MonitrLLM: A Community-Centered Evaluation Infrastructure for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02409)
*Victor Ojewale,Ro Encarnación,Suresh Venkatasubramanian,Danaé Metaxa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MonitrLLM，一种开源基础设施，用于将完整对话记录与用户自报的任务意图和结果评估联系在一起，将三者视为主要评估信号而非可选元数据。通过为期两周的26名大学生试点研究（206份评估报告），发现高满意度（4.19/5）与高任务失败率（23.1%）并存，且多轮对话的失败率是单轮对话的2.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM评估基础设施存在关键空白：基准套件关注受控任务，大规模对话语料捕捉自然使用但不含用户反馈，界面内反馈机制记录满意度但不含任务目的——三者都无法将交互轨迹与用户定义的结果联系起来。

Method: 构建开源的MonitrLLM基础设施，将完整对话记录与用户自报的任务意图和结果评估三个信号统一收集。随后进行为期两周的可行性试点，招募26名大学生在日常使用ChatGPT过程中记录评估报告。

Result: 试点收集了206份含完整对话记录的评估报告。参与者平均满意度高达4.19/5，但目标任务失败率却达23.1%；多轮对话被报告为失败的比率是单轮对话的2.5倍，表明扩展交互可能反映困难而非参与度。

Conclusion: 将直接用户反馈与观测数据结合能为LLM评估提供更全面的视角，克服传统评估方法中满意度与任务结果脱节的问题。文章讨论了实现这一目标所需的基础设施可能性。

Abstract: Benchmark suites assess model capability on controlled tasks; large-scale conversation corpora capture naturalistic use without user feedback; and in-interface feedback mechanisms record satisfaction without task purpose. Together, they leave a critical gap in LLM evaluation: no existing infrastructure routinely links interaction trajectories to user-defined outcomes. We introduce MonitrLLM, open-source infrastructure for community-centered LLM evaluations that links full conversation transcripts to user-reported task intent and outcome assessments, treating all three as primary evaluative signals rather than optional metadata.
  To demonstrate the value of this approach, we conducted a two-week feasibility pilot with 26 college students using ChatGPT, collecting 206 evaluation reports with full conversation transcripts. The findings from our pilot demonstrate the value of connecting conversation trajectories with user-reported outcomes. For instance, despite reporting high average satisfaction (4.19/5) with their LLM interactions, participants also experience a substantial 23.1% failure rate on their goal tasks. We also find that multi-turn conversations are reported as failing at 2.5 times the rate of single-turn exchanges, a pattern that reframes extended interaction as a signal of difficulty rather than engagement. We conclude by discussing the value of incorporating direct user feedback with observational data for robust LLM evaluations, and the possibilities for infrastructure that enables this goal.

</details>


### [375] [Real-Time Detection and Repair of LLM Agent Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02464)
*Sunny Dubey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于可观测步骤遥测数据的轻量级LLM代理失败检测与修复系统：利用只训练在健康轨迹上的一类回声状态网络集成（配合CUSUM告警）以微秒级开销检测失败，并结合确定性验证层（复算工具结果总和、确认必要调用）实现零误报的强校验；检测后的失败轨迹被回滚并以放量重跑来修复，总体上以极低成本显著提升了任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理在执行过程中经常中途失败（死循环、级联工具错误、偏离目标、伪造结果或吸收损坏内容），而标准做法是用第二个LLM评判每一步，成本甚至超过代理本身。因此作者希望仅从可观测的步骤遥测数据出发，用微秒级开销的监控器实现尽可能高的失败检测率。

Method: 方法分三层：(1) 轻量监控器：基于仅由健康运行数据训练的一类回声状态网络（echo-state network）集成，配合CUSUM告警机制，对每步遥测数据实时检测异常；(2) 确定性验证层：复算运行声称的总数与实际收到的工具结果的一致性，并确认所有必需调用均已完成，该层零误报且可跨模型泛化；(3) 修复闭环：对被标记失败的运行进行回滚并实时重跑，恢复故障。

Result: 在2,823条提交的代理轨迹（覆盖三个框架、三个本地模型及gemini-2.5-flash API）上，监控器在5%误报预算下检测出71%的失败（AUROC 0.872），对无记忆基线的优势随故障后视界单调增长；确定性验证层零误报捕获60%失败（含覆盖检查达96%）；修复环节在16%重采样对照下恢复45%的失败（p=0.0005），任务成功率从52%升至73%，每步开销约200微秒，比judge调用低三个数量级。

Conclusion: 从步骤遥测数据出发的轻量监控加确定性验证能够在极低开销下实现强大的失败检测，且检测可闭环为修复以显著提升任务成功率。监控器的排序能力可跨语料库迁移，但需要按部署重新校准；确定性验证则无需校准且零误报，二者形成互补。

Abstract: LLM agents fail mid-episode -- they loop, cascade tool errors, drift off goal, fabricate results, or silently absorb corrupted content -- and the standard remedy, judging every step with a second LLM, costs more than the agent itself. We ask how much detection is achievable from observable step telemetry alone, using monitors costing microseconds per step and trained only on healthy runs.
  On 2,823 committed agent episodes across three frameworks, three local models (qwen2.5 7b/3b, llama3.1 8b) and a commercial API (gemini-2.5-flash), a one-class echo-state-network ensemble with CUSUM alarms detects 0.71 of failures at a 5% false-alarm budget (AUROC 0.872). Its advantage over a memoryless baseline is a monotone function of post-onset horizon (+0.09 at <=3 steps, +0.40 at >=9), predicting its own failure region out of sample on AFTraj-2K. Ranking transfers with no retraining to two corpora from other groups (AFTraj-2K 0.745, ATBench 0.779).
  Monitors carry two burdens: a per-deployment healthy null (they do not transfer -- AUROC 0.527 cold against 0.885 recalibrated) and a residual false-alarm rate. We add a layer carrying neither: deterministic verification, which recomputes a run's stated total from the tool results it actually received and confirms every required call was made. Head-to-head it catches 60% of failures (96% with the coverage check) at 0 of 63 false positives against the monitor's 54% at 17%, transfers unchanged to llama3.1:8b (110 of 110 at 0 of 10), and trips on 0 of 1825 healthy episodes.
  Detection is then closed into repair: each flagged run is rolled back and re-run live, recovering 45% of failures against a 16% resampling control (p=0.0005) and lifting task success from 52% to 73% for about one extra model call per run. The system runs at ~200 microseconds per step, three orders of magnitude below a judge call. Code, traces and results are released.

</details>


### [376] [xPress: Parallel Refinement for Diffusion Drafters in Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02438)
*Zheng Wang,Davis Wertheimer,Yu Chin Fabian Lim,Mudhakar Srivatsa,Raghu K. Ganti,Minjia Zhang,Naigang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: xPress是一个轻量级因果精炼器，用于修复扩散草稿器中缺失的因果依赖关系。它在不引入逐token循环的情况下，通过并行精炼重构整个扩散块的因果依赖，从而提升推测解码的接受长度和端到端吞吐量。在Qwen3-8B上，相比原始的dFlash扩散草稿器，xPress将接受长度平均提升约30%（最高+56%），端到端解码吞吐量平均提升约1.3倍（最高1.7倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dFlash等块扩散草稿器虽然通过单次前向传播生成整块草稿token，大幅降低了推测解码中多token起草的开销，但其单次离散去噪的最终步骤是基于每个位置的对数几率分布独立采样条件独立token。这导致生成的草稿是逐位置边际分布而非联合分布——没有草稿token保证依赖于其前序token。独立采样的边际分布容易产生"单看每个token都很可能，但在目标模型的条件验证下联合概率很低"的序列，导致早期拒绝并限制接受长度。

Method: xPress提出一种轻量级因果精炼器，通过并行精炼方式一次性调和整个扩散块，在不使用逐token循环的情况下恢复并在草稿token之间传播因果依赖关系，从而修复扩散草稿器中缺失的因果性。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B上，跨越数学、代码和聊天七个基准测试，xPress相比原始的dFlash扩散草稿器，接受长度平均提升约30%（最高+56%），端到端解码吞吐量平均提升约1.3倍（最高1.7倍）。

Conclusion: xPress通过恢复扩散草稿器中的因果依赖关系，有效解决了独立边际采样导致的早期拒绝问题，显著提升了推测解码的接受长度和端到端吞吐量，同时保持了轻量设计和并行处理的优势。

Abstract: Block-diffusion drafters like dFlash generate an entire block of draft tokens in a single forward pass, drastically reducing the overhead of multiple-token drafting in speculative decoding. The crucial final step of the single-pass discrete denoising process involves using the logit distribution at each position to sample conditionally independent tokens. The resulting draft is thus a set of per-position marginals, rather than a joint distribution: no draft token is guaranteed to depend on its predecessors. Such independently sampled marginals tend to produce sequences with tokens that are individually likely, but jointly improbable under the target model's distribution, which verifies each token conditionally. This can cause early rejection and limits acceptance length. To address this, we propose xPress as a means to restore the missing causality in diffusion drafters. xPress is a lightweight causal refiner that reconciles the whole diffusion block at once through parallel refinement, restoring and propagating causal dependencies across the draft without a token-by-token loop. On Qwen3-8B, across seven math, code, and chat benchmarks, xPress raises acceptance length by about 30% on average (up to +56%) and its end-to-end decoding throughput by about 1.3 on average (up to 1.7) compared to the original dFlash diffusion drafter.

</details>


### [377] [Optimizing Minimax Regret in Uncertain MDPs with Small Sets of Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02509)
*Sterre Lutz,Daniël Vos,Matthijs T. J. Spaan,Anna Lukina*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出k-自适应策略合成问题（k-adaptable policy synthesis），用于在不确定马尔可夫决策过程（UMDPs）中预先准备有限数量的策略，并在模型不确定性在临近执行时被解决后从中选择最合适的策略，以最小化极小极大遗憾为目标。作者证明该问题是NP难的，并提出精确的嵌套分支定界算法KAPS。实验表明从一到两个策略的增量带来最大的遗憾降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实世界的序贯决策中，环境模型常常存在不确定性。传统方法要么优化单一策略（可能牺牲性能），要么为每个可能的MDP准备单独优化策略（可能违反操作、监管或可解释性约束）。实际场景中模型不确定性在临近执行前才被解决，因此需要一种折中方案：预先准备有限数量的策略供后续选择。

Method: 将问题形式化为k-adaptable策略合成，目标是最小化极小极大遗憾。证明该问题为NP难，并提出KAPS算法——一个精确的嵌套分支定界方法，包含问题特定的界和启发式规则。KAPS联合优化哪些MDP共享同一策略以及策略本身。

Result: 在多种UMDP基准上的实验表明，将策略数量从1增加到2时遗憾降低最大；在单策略设定下，KAPS在解质量上与现有方法相当，并且在证明最优性方面显著更频繁。

Conclusion: KAPS为多策略自适应决策提供了一种精确求解方法，填补了单一策略与每MDP单独策略之间的空白，能够在有限策略数约束下有效平衡性能与部署可行性。

Abstract: Sequential decision-making in real-world applications often involves uncertainty about the environment's model. Uncertain Markov decision processes (UMDPs) represent the possible environments as a set of MDPs with shared states and actions but potentially different transition probabilities and rewards. Optimizing a single policy across all possible MDPs may sacrifice performance, while preparing an individually optimized policy for every MDP may violate operational, regulatory, or interpretability constraints on the number of policies that can be prepared and deployed. We consider settings in which model uncertainty is resolved shortly before execution, allowing the most suitable policy to be selected from a limited set prepared in advance. We introduce $k$-adaptable policy synthesis, which optimizes such a set of $k$ policies under a minimax-regret objective. We prove that the problem is NP-hard and develop KAPS, an exact nested branch-and-bound algorithm with problem-specific bounds and heuristics. KAPS jointly optimizes which MDPs share a policy and the policies themselves. Experiments across various UMDP benchmarks show that the largest reduction in regret consistently occurs when increasing from one to two policies. In the single-policy setting, KAPS is competitive with existing methods in solution quality and proves optimality substantially more often.

</details>


### [378] [Agentic Commerce World: An Auditable and Verifiable Environment for Vibe Commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02441)
*Shicheng Fan,Mingdai Yang,Duohao Wang,Canyu Chen,Yongfeng Zhang,Hua Wei,Manling Li,Julian McAuley,Kun Zhang,Philip S. Yu,Kejing Yu,Zhiwei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍Agentic Commerce World (ACWorld)，一个用于评估Buyer和Merchant两类AI代理在共享市场中进行持续交易的环境。通过Vibe Commerce Protocol (VCP)验证代理动作并记录交互，ACWorld提供了200个任务的能力覆盖基准和60个任务的785,022个可交易列表的大目录基准，并证明了过程级证据在代理评估中的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 继vibe coding之后，vibe commerce允许人们用自然语言表达买卖目标并委托给AI代理。但商业交易要求Buyer和Merchant两类独立控制的代理在共享市场中交互，同时保留各自的私有目标和不同权限。现有的代理评估缺乏能够模拟这种多代理商贸场景并保证可审计、可复现的环境，因此需要构建专门的评估体系。

Method: ACWorld通过Vibe Commerce Protocol (VCP)实现：在更新共享交易状态之前验证代理的每个动作，并记录所有交互结果，使代理行为可审计且评估可复现。基准包含两个轨道：(1) 200个任务的能力覆盖轨道；(2) 60个任务的大目录轨道，搜索785,022个可交易列表。

Result: 在十个模型上，能力覆盖轨道的平均得分范围为65.9%至85.6%，大目录轨道的平均得分范围为56.1%至91.4%。分析表明最终状态容易遗漏评估错误，不完整的轨迹仍保留有用的过程信号，大目录任务暴露了各阶段瓶颈。

Conclusion: 过程级证据（而非仅依赖最终状态）对于准确评估商贸代理至关重要。ACWorld为vibe commerce中的多代理系统提供了一个可审计、可复现的评估基础设施，揭示了当前模型在处理大型目录和复杂多阶段交易任务时的能力边界。

Abstract: In vibe coding, people describe software in natural language and delegate implementation to AI agents. By analogy, vibe commerce allows people to express buying or selling goals in natural language and delegate the corresponding tasks to agents. Commerce, however, requires independently controlled Buyer and Merchant agents to interact in a shared market while preserving their private objectives and distinct authority. We introduce Agentic Commerce World (ACWorld), an environment for evaluating such agents across ongoing transactions. Through its Vibe Commerce Protocol (VCP), ACWorld validates agent actions before updating shared transaction state and records the resulting interactions, making agent behavior auditable and evaluation reproducible. The ACWorld Benchmark contains a 200-task capability-coverage track and a 60-task large-catalog track that searches 785,022 transactable listings. Across ten models, mean scores range from 65.9% to 85.6% and from 56.1% to 91.4%, respectively. Our analysis shows that process-level evidence is necessary: final state alone can miss evaluated errors, incomplete trajectories still retain useful process signals, and large-catalog tasks expose bottlenecks across stages.

</details>


### [379] [Right Answer, Wrong Method: Shortcut Hacking Misleads the Evaluation of LLM Reasoning on Frontier Science Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02442)
*Xuan Ren,Weiqi Zhai,Tianle Pu,Yihua Zhu,Yihua Zhu,Hu Wei,Bing Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了大型语言模型在科学推理基准测试中的"解决方案黑客"（Solution Hacking）失败模式，即模型通过数值搜索、枚举、猜测或先验证后推理等捷径获得正确答案而非进行有效的任务定向推导。该现象随基准难度急剧增加（从基础问题的2.2%升至奥林匹克级问题的28.3%和HLE的37.4%），前沿模型中8.2%-44.1%被判定正确的答案实际为被黑客的解决方案。研究者提出了自动评判器和测试时指令等反黑客策略，发现抑制捷径行为会显著降低报告的准确率，而对正确且非黑客的准确率影响较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学推理基准通常仅以最终答案准确率评估LLM，但这无法确保模型确实展示了目标推理能力——正确的答案可能来自非法捷径而非有效推理，这导致对前沿模型科学推理能力的过度估计。

Method: 系统性地跨难度级别、科学领域和前沿模型分析"解决方案黑客"现象；开发专家启发的反黑客策略，包括自动评判器（automatic judge）和测试时指令（test-time instruction）来识别和抑制捷径行为。

Result: 解决方案黑客现象随难度大幅上升，从常见问题的2.2%到奥林匹克级问题的28.3%和HLE的37.4%；不同前沿模型中8.2%-44.1%被判正确的答案实为黑客方案；应用反黑客策略后报告准确率显著下降，而正确且非黑客的准确率受影响较小。

Conclusion: 仅基于最终答案的评估会高估前沿LLM的科学推理能力，需要在基准评估中引入对抗捷径行为的策略以更准确地反映模型的真实推理水平。

Abstract: Scientific reasoning benchmarks typically evaluate large language models (LLMs) using final-answer accuracy. However, a correct answer does not necessarily demonstrate the reasoning capability targeted by the problem. We identify Solution Hacking, a failure mode in which an LLM reaches the correct answer through invalid shortcuts, such as numerical search, enumeration, guessing, or answer-first verification, without providing a valid task-targeted derivation. We systematically analyze this phenomenon across difficulty levels, scientific domains, and frontier models. Solution hacking increases sharply with benchmark difficulty, from 2.2\% on common problems to 28.3\% on Olympiad-level problems and 37.4\% on HLE. Moreover, 8.2\%-44.1\% of answers credited as correct across frontier models are identified as hacked solutions. We further develop expert-inspired anti-hacking strategies, including an automatic judge and a test-time instruction. The results show that suppressing shortcut behavior substantially reduces reported accuracy while having a smaller effect on correct and non-hacked accuracy. These findings reveal that answer-only evaluation can overestimate the scientific reasoning capabilities of frontier LLMs.

</details>


### [380] [ParEvalLayer: When Partial LLM-Agent Evaluations Support a Decision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02444)
*Wei-Jung Huang,Bonan Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ParEvalLayer，一个用于LLM代理系统评测的决策层，它通过读取配对任务结果和预先选择的比较策略，在部分评测进行中判断被测试的代理系统是否更好、是否不比指定程度更好、是否需要更多证据或应放弃判断。通过回放已完成基准数据模拟提前停止，评估发现使用主要比较规则时，三个公开基准在仅观察15%-25%任务结果后就能得出与完整评测相同的结论，而其他基准需要更多任务结果，这说明了部分分数本身不足以作为报告依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理评测中，任务结果通常在完整基准运行完成前就已产生，部分分数虽诱人但无法表明已观察任务是否支持与完整评测相同的结论。早期任务可能遗漏基准的重要部分，先运行更便宜的任务会扭曲样本，只决定简单配对比较的规则可能看起来准确但留下大量未解决的比较。因此需要一种能在评测早期可靠判断最终结论可能与决策信任度的方法。

Method: ParEvalLayer是一个决策层，它读取两个代理系统的配对任务结果以及预先选择的比较策略。对每次部分运行，它记录被测试代理系统是否按所需幅度更好、是否未按该幅度更好、是否需要更多证据或应弃权。评估方法是在已完成公开基准数据上回放，模拟每次评测提前停止：在每一点只使用已观察的结果应用策略，若得出两个比较判断之一，则检查该判断是否与相同系统对的完整数据一致。

Result: 使用主要比较规则时，三个公开基准在仅观察15%到25%的任务结果后就能达到与完整评测相同的决策；其他基准需要更多任务结果。这种差异表明不同基准的收敛速度存在显著变化，部分分数在不同基准上的可靠性差异很大。

Conclusion: 部分分数本身不足以作为评测报告的依据。报告应同时说明所使用的决策规则以及尚未作出决策的比较数量，以便读者评估早期结论的可信度。

Abstract: LLM-agent evaluations often produce task outcomes long before the full benchmark run is complete. A partial score is tempting to report, but it does not show whether the observed tasks support the same conclusion as the completed evaluation. Early tasks can omit important parts of a benchmark, running cheaper tasks first can distort the observed sample, and a rule that decides only easy pairs can appear accurate while leaving many comparisons unresolved. We introduce ParEvalLayer, a decision layer that reads paired outcomes for two agent systems and a comparison policy chosen in advance. For each partial run, it records whether the tested agent system is better by the required amount, is not better by that amount, needs more evidence, or should abstain. We evaluate ParEvalLayer by replaying completed public benchmark data as if each evaluation had stopped earlier. At each point, ParEvalLayer applies the policy using only the outcomes observed so far; if it reaches one of the two comparison judgments, we check whether that judgment matches the completed data for the same system pair. With the main comparison rule, three of the public benchmarks reach the same decision as the completed evaluation after observing only 15% to 25% of task outcomes. Other benchmarks require more task outcomes. This variation shows why a partial score alone is not enough: reports should also state the decision rule and how many comparisons remain without a decision.

</details>


### [381] [Infinite Trace Objectives with Finite Trace Techniques: Translating LTL to LTLf+](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02454)
*Christoph Weinhuber,Maximilian Prokop,Giuseppe De Giacomo,Moshe Y. Vardi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次提出了从LTL到LTLf+的翻译方法，通过将LTL公式归一化为Manna-Pnueli层次结构的语法反应性片段，再对每个组件进行线性翻译，使得LTLf+的有限自动机技术可以应用于LTL相关的人工智能问题，且渐近复杂度保持不变（双指数）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上解决LTL相关的AI问题（如反应式综合、马尔可夫决策过程中的随机规划、强化学习）需要将LTL规范转换为非确定性无限词自动机再进行确定性化，这一步骤在理论和实践中都极其困难。LTLf+具有与LTL相同的表达能力，但保留了LTLf的关键优势——基于有限词上的有限自动机，具有规范最小表示和高效确定性化过程。然而此前缺乏从LTL到LTLf+的转换方法，限制了LTLf+技术在实际LTL问题中的应用。

Method: 本文首先将LTL公式归一化为Manna-Pnueli层次结构中的语法反应性（syntactic reactivity）片段，以生成LTLf+的一般基于片段的形状；然后针对该片段的每个独立组件提出线性翻译方法，最终组合得到从LTL到LTLf+的完整翻译。

Result: 成功构建了首个从LTL到LTLf+的翻译；该翻译使得针对LTLf+开发的丰富技术体系可以直接应用于当前以LTL表述的各种AI问题；同时证明了该翻译在渐近复杂度上没有额外代价——从LTL经LTLf+到自动机的完整流程仍保持双指数复杂度。

Conclusion: 通过将LTL翻译为LTLf+，原本基于有限词自动机的高效推理技术（包括规范性最小表示和高效确定性化）可以无缝用于LTL相关的人工智能问题，且不增加渐近复杂度。这为AI领域的LTL任务提供了新的、更实用的工具链。

Abstract: Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) is one of the most widely adopted languages for specifying temporal extended objectives in AI, with applications ranging from reactive synthesis to stochastic planning in Markov decision processes and reinforcement learning.
